TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度推导出“仅凭话语形式无法完全恢复说话者意图”的理论上界：任何基于文本特征（包括大语言模型隐藏状态）的听者/解码器，其恢复说话者意图的概率都受制于话语形式对意义所留下的固有不确定性；该不确定性分为不可约部分与只能由语境消解的部分，因此无论训练数据多么充足，任何表征都无法突破这一上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图回答一个根本问题：听者能否仅从话语形式（而不借助语境）恢复说话者的意义。这一问题的答案对语言理解模型的能力边界具有深远影响，因此作者希望给出一个不依赖具体模型架构的信息论层面的严格回答，并证明该限制对所有特征提取器（包括当代大语言模型）都成立。

Method: 作者将语言使用建模为意义、语境与话语三者上的联合分布，基于信息论推导出解码器从“话语表征”中恢复说话者意图概率的上界。该上界由形式对意义留下的不确定性决定，并进一步将该不确定性分解为不可约部分（形式本身固有）与只能由话语外语境消解的部分。理论同时适用于离散与连续意义空间；实验上通过在人工语言、中文零代词消解和颜色指称三条路径提供经验验证。

Result: 理论推导表明：任何由文本特征驱动的解码器，其恢复说话者意图的概率都存在由语言本身固有不确定性决定的上界；该上界对任意表征（包括大语言模型的隐藏状态）始终成立。人工语言、普通话零代词消解和颜色指称三类实验均提供了支持该理论的经验证据。

Conclusion: 仅凭话语形式无法完全恢复说话者意义是语言的内在属性而非表征的缺陷：形式留下的不确定性中，只有语境才能消解的那一部分决定了任何模型的能力上限。这为语言理解模型设定了一个不可逾越的“理论天花板”，提示模型若要逼近说话者真实意图，就必须依赖话语外语境信息。

Abstract: Can a listener recover what a speaker means from the form of an utterance alone? We answer this question information-theoretically, and for a listener given by any featurizer of text, including the hidden states of contemporary large language models. Modeling language use as a joint distribution over meanings, contexts, and utterances, we derive upper bounds on the probability that a decoder recovers a speaker's intended meaning from a representation of the utterance. The bounds are governed by the uncertainty that form leaves about meaning, which splits into an irreducible part and a part that only (extralinguistic) context, but never the utterance alone, can resolve. Because these quantities are intrinsic to language, no representation, however much text or supervision produced it, can surpass them; the bounds hold whether the space of meanings is discrete or continuous. Experiments on artificial languages, Mandarin zero-pronoun resolution, and color reference provide empirical evidence in support of the theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Refundable Deposits: How to Restore Cooperation in Finitely Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27536)
*Giulio Salizzoni,Domenico Mergoni Cecchelli,Edward Plumb,Maryam Kamgarpour,Galit Ashkenazi-Golan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在有限重复博弈中通过"押金"机制扩大均衡集的方法：每期参与者可自愿向中立中介存入可退还押金，若偏离行为则被没收。押金支付在每个阶段都是自愿且激励相容的，不需要参与者任何承诺，仅要求中介在博弈开始前承诺固定的退还规则。该机制可在不改变底层博弈、不进行参与者间转移支付的条件下，维持比标准均衡更有效率的收益配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限重复博弈拥有丰富的纳什均衡集，但有限重复博弈通常只具有很小且往往低效率的均衡集。作者希望在不改变底层博弈结构的前提下，扩大有限重复博弈的均衡集合，使其能够支撑更有效率的收益配置（如合作），以弥补标准有限重复博弈中合作无法出现的缺憾。

Method: 引入中立中介与押金机制：每个阶段参与者可选择存放一定可退还押金，押金在博弈结束时返还，一旦发生偏离则被没收。押金支付是自愿的，且在每一阶段均满足激励相容条件，故不需要参与者的事前承诺；唯一需要的承诺是中介在博弈开始前固定好的退还规则。该机制不涉及参与者之间的直接转移支付，也不改变原始博弈。

Result: 该机制能够支撑比标准有限重复博弈均衡更有效率的收益配置。作者在囚徒困境、拥塞博弈和公共品博弈三个标准有限重复版本中无法出现合作的场景中验证了机制的有效性，并将其推广应用于动态公共池资源问题，表明该构造可扩展到超越静态重复博弈的情形。

Conclusion: 通过一个仅需中立中介承诺、参与者自愿且阶段激励相容的押金机制，可以在有限重复博弈中实现比标准均衡更高效的收益结果，为有限重复博弈中合作的实现提供了一种新的构造性方法，并具有向动态环境推广的潜力。

Abstract: While infinitely repeated games admit a rich set of Nash equilibria, finitely repeated games typically have a much smaller and often inefficient one. We show how to enlarge this set using deposits: in each period a player may place a refundable sum with a neutral intermediary, returned when the game ends and forfeited following a deviation. Paying these deposits is voluntary and incentive compatible at every stage, so no commitment by the players is assumed, the only commitment required being that of the intermediary to a refund rule fixed before play begins. The mechanism sustains payoff profiles more efficient than those of the standard equilibria, without altering the underlying game and without transfers between players. We demonstrate it on the prisoner's dilemma, a congestion game, and a public goods game, all settings where cooperation cannot emerge in the standard finitely repeated version. We also apply it to a dynamic common-pool resource, suggesting that the construction extends beyond repeated stage-games.

</details>


### [135] [Approval-Based Apportionment: Like Portioning, Approximately like Committee Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27605)
*Paul Gölz,Hannane Yaghoubizade*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究批准式席位分配（候选人可获多个席位），证明EJR、EJR+与FJR三公理等价，PJR、PJR+与FPJR等价；Lindahl可定价性等价于PAV分数下的近似最优性；局部PAV最优委员会具有可定价性；并建立将分配公理提升到比例分配（portioning）的构造，揭示分配与比例分配在公理蕴含关系上更为接近，且这些蕴含在委员会选举中不成立但部分可近似成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批准式席位分配介于委员会选举（每位候选人0或1席）与比例分配（分数席位）之间，作者希望厘清其与其他两类问题的公理关系，并通过统一视角理解比例性公理之间的蕴含结构，同时借助分配作为“中间地带”为委员会选举中的近似关系提供猜想依据。

Method: 采用公理化分析方法：证明分配情境下多组比例性公理的等价性；用Lindahl定价机制刻画PAV最优性的等价条件；并构造一个从分配提升到比例分配的“公理提升”构造，用以在两类模型间传递公理及蕴含关系。

Result: 得到三组核心结论：(1) EJR/EJR+/FJR两两等价，PJR/PJR+/FPJR两两等价；(2) Lindahl可定价性等价于PAV分数的某种近似最优性，且局部PAV最优委员会具有Lindahl可定价性；(3) 所构造的提升构造可将分配公理及其蕴含关系带到比例分配中，且新得到的蕴含关系在委员会选举中均不成立、但多项可近似成立。

Conclusion: 分配问题在公理结构上比想像中更接近比例分配而非委员会选举；分配可作为研究委员会选举中近似比例关系的有效“试验场”，为猜想与证明近似比例性结论提供新工具与依据。

Abstract: We study approval-based apportionment, a variant of committee elections in which candidates ("parties") can be selected several times. We show that the proportionality axioms EJR, EJR+, and FJR coincide, and so do PJR, PJR+, and FPJR; that Lindahl priceability, an axiom implying core stability, is equivalent to a notion of approximate optimality with respect to the proportional approval voting (PAV) score; and that locally PAV-optimal committees are priceable.
  Approval-based apportionment (where a candidate receives an integer number of seats) lies between committee elections (zero or one seat) and portioning (a fractional number of seats). We formally connect portioning and apportionment by giving a construction that lifts axioms from apportionment to portioning and preserves implications between them. Several of our new implications between apportionment axioms are natural from a portioning perspective, leading us to believe that apportionment sits closer to portioning than to committee elections. None of them holds in committee elections, but several extend approximately, which makes apportionment a fruitful setting for conjecturing approximate relationships in approval-based committee elections.

</details>


### [136] [Condorcet-Winning Sets and Peer Selection in Planar Metric Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27653)
*Gabriel de Azevedo,Ulysse Hennebelle*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究平面度量选举中的 Condorcet 获胜集：在选民按给定范数下的距离对候选人排序的情形下，找出能被多数投票者接受的小规模候选人群体。作者将一般度量选举、同行选择（peer selection）与单轮 Voronoi 博弈统一建模为二人 Stackelberg 博弈并纳入同一层级，同时提出强同行选择（strong peer selection）变体。主要结果表明：在 ℓ1 与 ℓ∞ 范数下，每个平面实例都存在大小至多为 3 的 Condorcet 获胜集（强同行选择下亦然），其证明依赖于关于强矩形 ε-网的改进结果（平面中任意点集可选至多 3 个输入点与所有包含超过半数的轴平行矩形相交，改进 Ashok 等人的 9/16 阈值）；在 ℓ2 范数下，每个平面度量选举存在大小至多为 4 的 Condorcet 获胜集，改进 Song 等人的一般上界 5；此外还给出单轮 Voronoi 博弈的新范数无关界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在排名制投票中，Condorcet 获胜集是一种特殊的候选人群体：不存在外部候选人被大多数选民优先于该群体中的每个成员。已有研究在平面度量选举（选民按给定范数下的距离对候选人排序）中给出了若干界（如 Song 等的上界 5、Ashok 等的 9/16 阈值），但仍存在改进空间；同时，一般度量选举、同行选择与单轮 Voronoi 博弈之间缺乏统一的框架，且同行选择此前未考虑更强的变体。

Method: 作者首先将一般度量选举、同行选择与单轮 Voronoi 博弈统一建模为二人 Stackelberg 博弈，并置于一个共同的层级结构中，引入新的强同行选择变体。随后，针对 ℓ1 与 ℓ∞ 范数，建立了强矩形 ε-网的新结果：平面中任意点集可选取至多 3 个输入点，使其与每个包含超过半数的点的轴平行矩形相交（改进了 Ashok 等此前 9/16 的阈值），由此推导出存在大小至多为 3 的 Condorcet 获胜集；对 ℓ2 范数则给出大小至多为 4 的构造性证明，并对单轮 Voronoi 博弈给出新的范数无关下界/上界。

Result: 在 ℓ1 与 ℓ∞ 范数下，每个平面实例都存在 Condorcet 获胜集，大小至多为 3，且在强同行选择下依然成立；在 ℓ2 范数下，每个平面度量选举存在大小至多为 4 的 Condorcet 获胜集，改进 Song 等人的一般上界 5；同时得到单轮 Voronoi 博弈的新的范数无关界。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一的 Stackelberg 博弈框架将多个相关问题是联系在一起，利用强矩形 ε-网这一新工具，在 L1/L∞ 范数下将 Condorcet 获胜集的大小紧到常数 3，在 L2 范数下改进到 4，并给出 Voronoi 博弈的范数无关结果，为平面度量选举的组合性质提供了新的理论刻画。

Abstract: In ranked-choice voting, a Condorcet-winning set is a group of candidates for which no outside candidate is preferred to every member of the group by a majority of voters. We study Condorcet-winning sets in planar metric elections, where voters rank candidates according to their distance under a given norm. We formulate general metric elections, peer selection, and the one-round Voronoi game as instances of a two-player Stackelberg game and place these problems in a common hierarchy. We also introduce a new variant, which we call strong peer selection.
  Our main result concerns peer selection under the $\ell_1$ and $\ell_\infty$ norms. We prove that every planar instance admits a Condorcet-winning set of size at most three, even under strong peer selection. This follows from a new result for strong rectangular $\varepsilon$-nets. We show that, for every set of points in the plane, one can choose at most three input points that intersect every axis-parallel rectangle containing more than half of the points, improving the previous threshold of $9 / 16$ due to Ashok et al. Under the $\ell_2$ norm, we prove that every planar metric election admits a Condorcet-winning set of size at most four, improving on the general bound of five due to Song et al. Finally, we give new norm-independent bounds for the one-round Voronoi game.

</details>


### [137] [Horizon-Independent Contraction for Continuous-Time Discounted Regularized Mean-Field Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27723)
*Junji Yan,Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了带折扣与熵正则化的非平稳连续时间平均场博弈（MFG）的收缩性质。代表智能体的状态由受控连续时间马尔可夫链驱动，状态与动作空间均为有限。作者证明在足够大的折扣率下，有限时域MFG存在与视界无关的收缩条件，且该条件与对应无限时域非平稳收缩条件一致；并由此得到有限与无限时域均值场均衡之间的显式收敛速率。每个有限时域均导出由一正算子谱半径刻画的精细化收缩判据，其大时域极限与视界无关收缩因子一致；最后给出折扣与无折扣有限时域正则化均衡之间的显式误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳、有限时域的连续时间平均场博弈在实际应用中比平稳/无限时域情形更常见也更具挑战性，其收缩性质缺乏系统刻画。已有结果多集中于无折扣情形，而对折扣熵正则化情形下收缩条件是否依赖于视界、以及有限与无限时域均衡之间的定量关系并不清楚，这阻碍了对MFG均衡的数值逼近与理论保证。

Method: 采用算子论与收缩映射框架：通过构造支配策略误差传播的正算子，分析其谱半径以建立有限时域的收缩判据；基于折扣率参数，对比有限时域与无限时域的非平稳收缩条件，证明二者在充分大折扣率下一致；并利用收缩性对均衡进行显式误差估计。

Result: (1) 在足够大的折扣率下，有限时域MFG存在与视界无关的收缩条件，且与无限时域非平稳情形下的收缩条件重合；(2) 由此获得有限与无限时域均值场均衡之间的显式收敛速率；(3) 每个有限时域可由一个正算子的谱半径得到更精细的收缩判据，其大时域极限恰为视界无关的收缩因子；(4) 给出折扣与无折扣有限时域正则化均衡之间的显式误差界。

Conclusion: 折扣和熵正则化有效改善了非平稳连续时间有限状态MFG的收缩性：当折扣率足够大时，问题本质上是"视界无关"的，有限时域与无限时域均衡可被统一刻画并具备显式收敛速率；同时，谱半径方法给出了比单一折扣条件更精确的收缩刻画，为有限时域MFG均衡的数值求解与误差分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study contraction properties of non-stationary continuous-time mean-field games (MFGs) under discounting and entropy regularization. The state of the representative agent evolves according to a controlled continuous-time Markov chain, and both the state and action spaces are finite. In contrast to the undiscounted case, we show that, under a sufficiently large discount rate, finite-horizon MFGs admit a horizon-independent contraction condition, which also coincides with the corresponding infinite-horizon non-stationary contraction condition. As a byproduct, we obtain an explicit convergence rate between finite- and infinite-horizon mean-field equilibria. For each finite horizon, we further derive a refined contraction criterion from the spectral radius of a positive operator that majorizes the propagation of policy errors, and show that its large-horizon limit agrees with the horizon-independent contraction factor. Finally, we provide an explicit error bound between discounted and undiscounted finite-horizon regularized equilibria.

</details>


### [138] [Fine-Tuning Autobidders with Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28199)
*Anton Safin,Alexandra Khirianova,Andrey Pudovikov,Aleksandr Katrutsa,Egor Samosvat*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Autobidding GRPO，将原本用于大语言模型后训练的免评论家（critic-free）策略梯度方法 GRPO 适配到在线广告自动出价场景，以解决 actor-critic RL 交替训练带来的不稳定性和对噪声鲁棒性差的问题。实验表明该方法在 BAT、iPinYou、AuctionNet 基准上点击量一致优于基线，转化量上排名第一或第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告自动出价中，标准 RL 方法采用 actor-critic 框架，但 actor 与 critic 的交替训练会导致训练不稳定，且对噪声的鲁棒性较差。同时，自动出价场景与 LLM 后训练类似，最优出价在事先是未知的（ground-truth target 未知），因此需要一个能够应对这种不确定性的 critic-free 训练框架。

Method: 将 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 框架从 LLM 后训练场景适配到自动出价（Autobidding GRPO）。GRPO 是一种无需 critic 的策略梯度方法，利用组内相对奖励替代价值估计，从而避免 critic 网络引入的不稳定因素。与 LLM 中 GRPO 用于微调预训练模型类似，该方法用于增强现有的强启发式基线策略（将启发式作为初始策略进行提升）。

Result: 在 BAT、iPinYou 和 AuctionNet 三个基准上与 actor-critic 模型、简单启发式方法以及基于控制器的方法进行对比，大量实验表明 Autobidding GRPO 在点击量指标上持续优于所有基线，在转化量指标上为最佳或第二佳方法。

Conclusion: Autobidding GRPO 展示了将 LLM 后训练技术（GRPO）迁移到广告自动出价领域（两者共享未知目标这一性质）的有效性，以免评论家的策略梯度方式解决了 actor-critic 方法训练不稳定和噪声鲁棒性差的问题，为自动出价提供了一种更稳定且性能更强的训练范式。

Abstract: Automated bidding (autobidding) is a core component of modern online advertising systems. Within this component, advertisers delegate sequential bid decisions to algorithms that must maximize campaign value while adhering to constraints such as a limited budget and a target cost-per-click (CPC). One of the approaches to resolve the autobidding problem is to formulate it as a Markov decision process and use reinforcement learning (RL) to train a bid generation function. The standard RL framework is actor-critic, which consists of an actor network that generates actions and a critic network that estimates the value of those actions. In our setting, the action is typically a bid or related pacing multiplier, and the value is the expected return from the auction given the bid. However, the alternating training of actor-critic RL models leads to instability and reduced robustness to noise. To address these issues, we adapt the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework to the autobidding setting. This framework is a \emph{critic-free} policy-gradient method originally developed for large language model post-training, where the ground-truth target is unknown. The autobidding setting shares this property, since the optimal bid is unknown in advance. Moreover, GRPO in the LLM domain is used to fine-tune the pre-trained model, and we use the same technique to enhance the performance of the strong heuristic baseline. We empirically compare Autobidding GRPO with actor-critic models, simple heuristics, and controller-based methods on the BAT, iPinYou, and AuctionNet benchmarks. Extensive experiments show that Autobidding GRPO consistently outperforms baselines in clicks and is the best or second-best method in conversion volume.

</details>


### [139] [A Constant Metric Distortion Protocol for Approval Voting Given Plurality Polls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28340)
*Fabian Frank,Jannik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文首次研究在只有 plurality polls（每个候选人的第一偏好投票占比）信息下的批准投票（approval voting）规则。作者提出一个由参数 k∈(0,1) 控制的简单协议：每位选民批准其偏好列表中包含至少 k 比例选民第一选择的最短前缀。在 metric distortion 框架下，该协议在最优 k 下可达失真 2+√5≈4.236；相关证明技术还给出 Bucklin 规则的 metric distortion 上界为 5；并证明该协议在噪声下的上界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批准投票简单且受欢迎，但存在一个关键局限：当选民对候选人有严格排序时，不清楚他们应该批准哪些候选人。本文希望利用一种简单易得的信息——plurality polls（每位候选人作为第一选择所占的选民比例）——来指导选民如何投票，以弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出一个参数化为 k∈(0,1) 的投票协议：每个选民批准其偏好列表中最短的前缀，使得该前缀包含至少 k 比例的选民的第一选择。通过 metric distortion（度量失真）框架对该协议进行理论分析，寻找最优 k；并借助相关证明技术分析 Bucklin 投票规则的失真上界；最后通过鲁棒性分析验证协议在噪声下的表现及上界的 tightness。

Result: 在最优参数 k 下，所提协议达到 metric distortion 2+√5≈4.236；证明 Bucklin 投票规则的 metric distortion 至多为 5；协议在噪声干扰下获得的失真上界是紧的。

Conclusion: 基于 plurality polls 的批准投票协议不仅简单可操作，而且在 metric distortion 意义上具有良好的理论保证，其界优于或可与已有规则对比；同时证明技术可用于改进 Bucklin 规则的分析，且结果对噪声稳健（上界紧）。这为在有限信息条件下设计实用的投票规则提供了理论与实证依据。

Abstract: Approval voting is a simple and well-regarded voting rule: voters submit approval ballots (subsets of the candidates) and the candidate receiving the most approvals wins. One major limitation of approval voting is that it is not clear which candidates voters should approve if they have an underlying strict order over the candidates. In this paper, we initiate the study of approval voting under a simple kind of information: plurality polls. That is, we assume that for each candidate we know the share of voters who rank this candidate as their top choice. Using these plurality polls, we suggest a simple protocol parameterized by a fraction $k \in (0,1)$: every voter should approve the smallest prefix of their preference list containing the first choices of at least a fraction $k$ of the voters. We evaluate this protocol via the framework of metric distortion and show that for the optimal choice of $k$, this rule achieves a metric distortion of $2 + \sqrt{5} \simeq 4.236$. The proof techniques we use for this statement also show that the Bucklin voting rule has a metric distortion of at most $5$. Finally, we evaluate the robustness of our protocol to noise and show that the upper bounds obtained for our protocol are tight.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MCC-PGPSE（边际覆盖贡献——并行状态熵最大化），通过 leave-one-policy-out 覆盖度与状态归属专业化来估计每个策略的独立贡献，并以不改变奖励总质量的非负辅助内在奖励重分配方式来抑制冗余访问、促进互补覆盖，在受控环境、7 个公开离散状态基准及原始 PGPSE 协议的 Room/Maze 设置上均取得正向覆盖收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE 通过在相同环境的多个副本中训练独立参数化的策略来扩大状态空间覆盖，但其所用的池化团队熵评分只衡量集体探索程度，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余的覆盖，导致并行策略可能产生大量冗余访问。

Method: MCC-PGPSE 将 leave-one-policy-out 覆盖度量与状态所有者（state-owner）专业化相结合，估计策略专属的贡献分数；它在保留 PGPSE 池化目标的同时，依据这些贡献分数对非负的辅助内在奖励进行重分配，并保持奖励总质量不变，从而惩罚冗余访问并鼓励互补覆盖。

Result: 在受控环境、7 个公开离散状态基准以及原 PGPSE 协议的 Room 与 Maze 设置中，MCC-PGPSE 在归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度上均相对 Entropy 基线取得正向的最终窗口增益；受控任务比较与固定公开套件的聚合结果显著，五种子原协议比较方向一致。消融与信用对齐对照表明增益主要来自 leave-one-policy-out 覆盖度量，而非非均匀加权、错配信用或单纯的新颖性奖励。

Conclusion: 贡献条件化的辅助奖励分配是一种可解释且有效的方法，可在离散状态空间中改善并行策略间的互补覆盖，为多策略并行探索的信用分配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [141] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型后训练量化工作流中因"源精度验证-部署"脱节而产生的结构性风险，形式化定义了量化行为等价类（QBECs）并证明其成员关系不蕴含行为等价性，据此提出一种三阶段对抗微调的量化触发后门攻击，在翻译与立场分类任务上验证了压缩后触发恶意行为的威胁，并提出将部署配置纳入行为认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化常被视为语义中立的优化，但源检查点在全精度下被评估、量化在下游进行且不重新评估的工作流形成了结构性的验证-部署鸿沟：量子化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度认证无法保证部署配置下的行为等价性，这为量化触发的后门攻击提供了理论空间。

Method: 形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBEC）并证明其成员关系不蕴含行为等价性；构建三阶段对抗微调框架在满足源精度检查的模型中嵌入潜在恶意载荷，使模型在INT8或4-bit压缩时激活目标对抗行为；并在两个实际场景中将攻击从仅解码器因果语言模型扩展至多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型；进行跨量化器迁移性分析。

Result: 被后门化的翻译模型在修复FP16下测得的友敌腐化为零，量化后反转率最高达85.02%；成对立场分类器在压缩后意识形态偏移最高达ΔBias=0.33；跨量化器迁移性分析表明攻击持续性由量化方案与模型架构共同决定，而非仅由名义位宽决定。

Conclusion: 源精度审计本身无法排除量化触发的行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证，方能保障可信边缘AI的安全性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [142] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 GDN/KDA 等线性注意力模型中以固定大小循环状态替代 KV 缓存的做法，本文提出 DAMP——一种基于离线标定的循环状态后训练量化方法。通过量化误差能量与衰减持久性识别高风险通道并给予更高精度，其余通道用 INT8 存储，在 9.9 bits/状态值的精度下接近 FP32 基线准确率，同时减少 69.1% 的循环状态存储、加速更新内核最高 2.01 倍并降低全模型 TPOT 最多 10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax 注意力需为每个历史 token 存储键值向量，推理内存随序列长度增长。GDN 与 KDA 等模型用固定大小的循环状态替代大部分层中的 KV 缓存以降低存储成本，但这些循环状态通常以 FP32 存储、占用大量显存，且其更新受内存带宽限制、显著增加解码延迟。作者是首个研究 GDN/KDA 模型循环状态后训练量化的工作，并发现均匀量化在精度-存储上表现不佳：INT8/FP8 已令复杂推理任务精度下降，INT4/NVFP4 则几乎摧毁精度；进一步发现量化误差能量集中于少数通道，且状态通道的相对衰减强度在不同提示与任务间保持稳定。

Method: DAMP 在离线标定阶段同时利用量化误差能量与基于衰减的持久性识别高风险通道，将这些通道以更高精度存储，其余通道量化为 INT8。该方法利用通道级量化误差能量分布集中与衰减强度跨任务稳定的两个发现，做出混合精度分配决策。

Result: 在 Qwen3.6-35B 与 Kimi-Linear-48B 上，覆盖数学推理、通用推理与代码生成共六项基准进行评测。在每状态值 9.9 bits 下，DAMP 的平均准确率接近 FP32 基线；循环状态存储减少 69.1%，循环状态更新内核加速最高 2.01 倍，全模型 TPOT 降低最高 10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP 表明通过离线标定与混合精度分配，可以有效对 GDN/KDA 模型的循环状态进行后训练量化，在近乎保持 FP32 准确率的前提下显著节省显存并降低解码延迟，为该类线性注意力模型部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [143] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究块稀疏特征化器（BSF，一种以方向块/子空间为原子单元、类似稀疏自编码器SAE的表示学习方法）的优势与局限，发现它仍存在特征分裂（feature splitting）与组合（composition）等经典SAE失败模式，并提出多项架构改进——包括Tournament Top-K选择规则以显著减少特征分裂——同时将块范式扩展至crosscoder。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF针对视觉中常见的生活在低维流形上的特征而设计，其原子单元是子空间（方向块）而非单个方向，相比SAE更适配此类结构。然而，作者需要系统评估BSF是否真正克服了SAE的经典失败模式（特征分裂与组合），并据此改进其架构。

Method: 作者对BSF进行实证分析以定位其优点与弱点，并在此基础上提出多项架构修改：引入Tournament Top-K选择规则来替代原有选择机制，从而显著降低特征分裂；同时将块（block）范式推广到crosscoder，使跨模型/跨层表示也能受益于子空间型特征单元。

Result: 研究发现BSF虽然以子空间为单元，但仍部分受限于特征分裂与组合等SAE传统问题；提出的Tournament Top-K选择规则能显著减少特征分裂，且块范式被成功扩展到crosscoder。

Conclusion: 通过架构级改进（Tournament Top-K）和向crosscoder的扩展，BSF在保留子空间建模优势的同时，缓解了经典SAE失败模式，为视觉等领域基于流形结构的可解释表示学习提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [144] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示持续学习中的灾难性遗忘与模型合并中的权重解纠缠误差本质上是同一现象——"任务干扰"（task interference），将其形式化为逐层的Frobenius内积 <ΔW_ℓ, J_ℓ(x)>_F，并证明该量的上界可由优化器控制的谱范数 ‖ΔW_ℓ‖_2 决定。据此识别出Muon优化器按构造调节这一谱范数因子，从而同时收紧持续学习与模型合并的干扰界，替代AdamW后可带来高达+5.02个点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习（CL）与模型合并（MM）都旨在获得一个在多个任务上表现良好的单一模型，但分别受限于灾难性遗忘与权重解纠缠误差。文献中这两类困难通常被分开处理，而基础优化器诱导的几何性质仅被视为实现细节。作者认为这两个困难实为同一现象的两种体现：对一个任务有用的参数更新会改变模型在另一任务上的输出，即"任务干扰"，并希望透过优化器视角统一地理解与缓解该问题。

Method: 作者将两个任务中的困难统一形式化为"任务干扰"，归结为逐层的Frobenius内积 <ΔW_ℓ, J_ℓ(x)>_F；理论上推导出隔离优化器可控因子谱范数 ‖ΔW_ℓ‖_2 的上界，并通过逐模式（per-mode）分析证明该上界能追踪经验干扰的主导部分。在此基础上，识别出近期提出的Muon优化器能通过构造调节该谱范数因子，从而收紧CL与MM的干扰界。

Result: 在八个任务的模型合并基准上，用Muon替代AdamW在三种CLIP骨干上最高提升+5.02个准确率点；在持续学习方面，Muon在十个类增量协议、三个任务增量协议以及11任务的MTIL基准上均带来一致的正向增益。理论分析与实验结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 任务干扰是持续学习与模型合并共同的根本机制，而优化器（尤其是其谱范数控制能力）在其中扮演核心角色。Muon作为一种原理驱动的、以优化器为中心的方案，能够与现有方法互补，为两类问题提供统一的增效途径。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [145] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Dandelion，一种球面版本的 warp 型神经偏微分方程求解器（源自 Flower）：每个网络层预测切平面位移并沿大圆传输特征，全程在球谐域进行层级池化以形成 U-Net 结构，完全摒弃卷积；同时发布一套原生球面 PDD 基准数据集。实验表明 Dandelion 在所有数据集上取得最优或次优性能，且分辨率越高优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量动力学过程发生在球面上，而默认科学机器学习架构是欧几里得式的。直接用于规则经纬网格会带来问题：笛卡尔卷积在高纬度扭曲、傅里叶神经算子中的 2D FFT 错误假设双重周期性、ViT 的笛卡尔位置编码扭曲球面测地距离。虽有球面卷积、球面傅里叶神经算子（SFNO）和测地线注意力等工作，但仍有改进空间，且缺少规模合适、原生球面的 PDE 基准数据集。

Method: 提出 Dandelion：球面版本的 Flower，一种基于 warp 的神经 PDE 求解器。其层用于预测切平面位移并将特征沿大圆传输（即球面坐标变换实现空间混合，无卷积）；通过在球谐域中实现完全层级池化来构造类似 U-Net 的结构。同时发布一套不断演进的、具有挑战性的原生球面 PDE 基准（含改进的 Galewsky 急流、异常链式湍流、Cahn-Hilliard 分解、球面 Riemann 激波、Held-Suarez 干大气输运与全球海洋动力学），填补现有球面数据集（要么过小过于风格化，要么如 ERA5 过大不利于迭代）的空白。

Result: Dandelion 在每个数据集上均取得最优或次优性能；与非线性 warp 的基线相比，其优势随分辨率提高而扩大；在 256×512 分辨率下，Dandelion 与 Flower2D 同时占据单步预测和 rollout 的前两名。

Conclusion: Dandelion 证明了 warp 式架构（无卷积、仅靠球面坐标变换）在球面动力系统建模上的有效性，与现有球面卷积/SFNO/注意力方法相比具有竞争力；所发布的基准数据集也为该领域的模型开发与比较提供了标准化的测试平台。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [146] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型端到端自可解释多标签图神经网络（SEMGNN），在统一框架与训练目标下联合学习多标签节点分类器与稀疏边掩码解释器，并利用标签-标签相关性同时提升分类性能与单个标签的解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签图学习旨在刻画真实世界中一个样本关联多个类别或包含多个对象的复杂场景，但现有方法均不具备训练时的解释能力。已有的事后（post-hoc）图解释器未在多标签图学习器中显式建模标签依赖的证据共享，尤其在标签对呈弱相关或负相关时，可能无法正确判断证据应在不同标签间共享还是分离，导致解释不佳。

Method: SEMGNN在一个统一框架和训练目标中联合学习预测器与稀疏边掩码解释器（而非事后解释）。它利用标签-标签相关性改进多标签节点分类，并增强对单个标签的解释，使同一节点的不同标签可由不同但连贯的结构性/相关性证据支持。

Result: 在社交网络、娱乐与生命科学等领域的合成及真实多标签网络上进行实验，SEMGNN在取得具有竞争力或更优预测性能的同时，提供了更忠实、更紧凑的标签条件化解释。

Conclusion: 端到端自解释框架能够弥补事后解释方法在多标签场景中证据共享建模的不足；将标签相关性融入预测与解释的统一学习中，可在不牺牲预测精度的前提下获得更忠实、紧凑的标签条件化解释。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [147] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CARSANN，一种基于局部曲率感知半径收缩的自适应最近邻分类框架。它利用 TwoNN 估计本征维数、PCA 构建本征表示，并通过基于形状算子的公式估计局部平均曲率来控制邻域空间范围：高曲率区域半径收缩更强，近似平坦区域保持更大邻域。在 70 多个 OpenML 数据集上的实验表明其一致优于标准 k-NN，且在相同邻域基数下于 40/45 个数据集上取得更高平衡准确率（均值从 0.6506 提升至 0.7528），Friedman 与 Nemenyi 检验证实改进具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规 k-NN 在整个特征空间中使用固定的邻域基数（即相同数量的邻居），这对局部几何结构沿数据流形剧烈变化的数据并不合适。现有自适应方法大多只调整邻居数量或局部度量，而没有显式地根据局部几何复杂度调整空间支持范围，因此需要一种能够按局部曲率调节邻域空间范围的几何驱动框架。

Method: CARSANN 首先使用 TwoNN 估计本征维数，并通过主成分分析（PCA）构建本征表示；随后利用基于形状算子（shape-operator）的公式估计局部平均曲率，并用该曲率控制邻域尺度：高曲率区域施加更强的半径收缩，近似平坦区域保留更宽的空间支持。与仅修改邻居数量或局部度量的方法不同，CARSANN 显式地调整局部证据（邻域）的空间范围。

Result: 在超过 70 个真实 OpenML 数据集上的实验表明，CARSANN 一致优于标准 k-NN，并与现有自适应最近邻方法相当。在相同基础邻域基数的受控比较中，CARSANN 在 45 个数据集中的 40 个上取得更高平衡准确率，平均平衡准确率从 0.6506 提升到 0.7528；相对于固定 k=5 的 k-NN 优势依然保持。Friedman 和 Nemenyi 检验证实改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可以作为自适应邻域支持的有效几何控制变量，为基于基数（cardinality）的最近邻自适应提供了一种互补的新范式，证明几何感知的空间邻域调整能够显著提升分类性能。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [148] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，无监督表征对齐在面对具有对称性（自同构群）的刺激几何设计时存在根本性的退化问题，且现有诊断指标（最廉价非恒等重标号）因密集采样产生近重复样本而失效并给出错误排序。作者提出一种可在实验设计阶段、在采集任何数据之前执行的设计时诊断与干预方法：仅依据该诊断筛选刺激（如颜色）子集，即可推动所有模型表征远离退化点，将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%，且模型、层数、N和求解器均保持不变。该风险广泛存在于规则设计与其候选几何等距群重合的情形（如均匀间隔的方向、音调、运动方向），而该检查只需一次函数调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐仅凭几何结构恢复刺激间的对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群在数据存在之前就限制了任何对齐方法所能识别的内容，造成不可辨识的退化问题。然而，已有的直观诊断（最廉价的非恒等重标号）在排序两个已发表设计时给出错误结论，因为密集采样产生了近重复样本，使其置换几乎零代价。因此需要一种可靠的、能在设计阶段就检测并干预这种结构退化的诊断方法。

Method: 作者将Demetci等人(2024)已知的置换不变性转化为一个设计阶段（data-free）的诊断与干预工具。在颜色刺激中，候选几何具有闭式表达，可通过穷举/重启测试验证诊断：用64倍的随机重启预算仍无法移动对称设计，而非对称的同等规模（N相同）集合每次都能恢复。在模型判别（区分表征模型）与对应关系恢复这两个目标上，对3000个子集计算显示二者几乎不相关（r=-0.02）。作者仅依据该诊断选择九个颜色（完全不参考任何学习到的表征）来进行干预。

Result: 该诊断正确识别出对称设计的内在退化（即使重启预算增加64倍仍无法恢复），而非对称集合总能成功恢复。仅用该诊断选择的九个颜色，使全部93个模型表征都远离退化点，并在模型、层数、N和求解器全部固定的情况下，把灾难性对齐失败率从75%降到2%。同时证实模型判别与对应恢复是两个几乎无关的目标（r=-0.02）。

Conclusion: 只要规则化的刺激设计与其候选几何的等距群重合——例如均匀间隔的方向、音调或运动方向——同样的退化风险就会出现。作者提出的诊断非常廉价，在数据采集前只需一次函数调用即可完成，因此应作为所有无监督表征对齐实验的标准设计前检查，用以诊断并规避自同构群导致的不可辨识性。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [149] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生长自组织映射（GSOM）的无监督生成式持续学习框架，通过每单元维护的均值、方差和协方差等分布统计量生成合成样本（分布统计记忆）实现免样本回放，在编码器-解码器配置中经祖先采样解码回输入空间进行训练。该方法不依赖任务边界或类别标签，在多个基准上达到与监督式基于记忆的SOTA方法相当的性能，并一致优于无记忆方法，尤其在单类增量场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习面临灾难性遗忘问题，现有方法常需存储原始数据（基于记忆的回放）或依赖监督信号/任务边界，存储开销大且不灵活。作者希望设计一种无需存储原始数据、完全无监督、可扩展的持续学习框架，以克服这些局限。

Method: 框架基于生长自组织映射（GSOM），每个GSOM单元学习并维护自身的均值、方差和协方差统计量，形成分布统计记忆；训练时通过从这些分布中采样生成合成样本（免样本回放），在编码器-解码器配置下再经祖先采样将样本解码回输入空间用于后续训练。整个训练过程不依赖显式任务边界或类别标签，具有自适应拓扑结构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该无监督方法性能与有监督的基于记忆的SOTA方法相当，且一致优于无记忆方法；在具有挑战性的单类增量场景中，框架匹配或超越现有基线。此外，还为单类增量TinyImageNet和MiniImageNet提供了基线参考结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，无监督、自适应、拓扑驱动的神经统计回放（分布统计记忆）是一种可扩展且灵活的持续学习途径，通过消除原始数据存储和标签/任务边界依赖，为持续学习提供了高效且具有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [150] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SegBench-GC，一个用于离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）的受控分段不变性压力测试基准。研究发现，当轨迹因管理原因而被人工切分（而非因终止）时，多步目标学习会严重退化：在 PointMaze 中，同等切分下将切点视为吸收态的成功率从无切分的 50.5% 降至 19.1%，而使用延续有效目标（CVT，即人工切分点仍从存储后继引导）则降至 39.1%；在 Puzzle-4x5 的 n=25 基线中，朴素处理甚至只有 0.27% 的成功率。作者通过目标级和学得评论家诊断验证了失败源于人工分段被误当作终止，并证明 CVT（标准的延续引导，而非新 Bellman 规则）可作为分段一致的对照，从而将行政性分段对多步离线 GCRL 的重大影响进行了隔离与跨学习器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习常利用轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步目标计算，但收集到的轨迹可能因行政管理原因被切分成段，这些切分边界并不对应真实的终止（episode termination）。这种人为分段若被朴素地当作终止来处理，可能导致多步目标与引导（bootstrapping）失效，从而严重影响算法性能。作者希望系统地检验并量化这一"分段不变性"问题。

Method: 作者引入 SegBench-GC 受控压力测试：在固定转移、源轨迹、目标采样、优化设置与评估的条件下，仅改变人工备份边界（artificial backup boundaries）是否以及如何保留延续价值。其中延续有效目标（CVT）作为分段一致的对照——奖励累积在人工切点停止，但目标从存储的后继状态继续引导。研究在 PointMaze 上进行匹配数量的实验（35,000 个人工切分、三种分段实现、三种优化种子），并复现 Decoupled Q-Chunking 代码库中公开的 n=25 基线用于 Puzzle-4x5；同时设计了目标级诊断（验证解析目标差异到数值精度）和学得评论家诊断（对比朴素处理与 CVT 下的评论家偏移）。

Result: PointMaze 中每任务 50 集的最终成功率为：无切分 50.5%、CVT 39.1%、相同切分按吸收态处理 19.1%；跨分段实现下朴素平均成功率在 4.8% 到 31.9% 之间剧烈波动。Puzzle-4x5 的 n-step 基线（n=25）同样失败：无切分 47.2%、CVT 58.5%、朴素仅 0.27%（跨三种优化种子）。目标级诊断验证解析目标差异到数值精度；学得评论家诊断显示朴素处理下评论家出现大幅乐观偏移，而 CVT 与无切分评论家大致对齐。

Conclusion: 行政性分段会显著改变多步离线 GCRL 的结果，朴素地将人工切点当作终止会带来严重性能损失与评论家乐观偏移；CVT 采用标准的延续引导（而非新的 Bellman 规则）即可保持分段一致性。本文的主要贡献在于提供了一个受控基准、对失败原因的隔离，以及跨学习器（跨算法/设置）的证据，提醒研究者在离线 GCRL 中必须区分真实终止与行政切分边界。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor. In a matched-count PointMaze study with 35,000 artificial cuts, three segmentation realizations, and three optimization seeds, final 50-episode-per-task success is 50.5% uncut, 39.1% with CVT, and 19.1% when the same cuts are treated as absorbing; across segmentation realizations, naive mean success ranges from 4.8% to 31.9%. An independent published n-step baseline (n=25) from the Decoupled Q-Chunking codebase shows the same failure on Puzzle-4x5: 47.2% uncut, 58.5% CVT, and 0.27% naive across three optimization seeds. A target-level diagnostic verifies the analytic target difference to numerical precision, and learned-critic diagnostics show a large optimistic shift under naive handling while CVT remains approximately aligned with the uncut critic. CVT applies standard continuation bootstrapping rather than a new Bellman rule; the contribution is the controlled benchmark, failure isolation, and cross-learner evidence that administrative segmentation can materially change multi-step offline GCRL.

</details>


### [151] [SafeStep: An Interactive Demonstration of Semantic Communication for Pedestrian Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27688)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Jeremiah Yang,Sirin Chakraborty,Samuel Chamoun,Travis Ross,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SafeStep，一个基于浏览器的实时语义通信平台，用于实时行人安全监控。平台从四路交通摄像头提取行人信息，经语义通信收发机在 AWGN 信道上传输，并向用户渲染位置、轨迹和风险标签。系统采用紧凑的 Meta-VIB 语义收发机（仅 416 万参数），可在变化的 SNR、码长和 AoI 下无需在线重训练而泛化。实验表明 Meta-VIB 较五种基线平均任务损失最多降低 92.1%；单台高端 GPU 服务器可支撑 20 个用户维持 5 帧/秒，100 个并发用户时无请求失败、平均响应低于 1 秒，但帧率降至约 1 帧/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信已展示出高效率和稳健性的潜力，但现有工作大多停留在仿真层面，缺乏可交互、可实时观测的演示平台；同时 AoI 驱动的下游任务性能退化难以在真实监控应用中直接观察。SafeStep 旨在填补这一空白，提供一个端到端的、可在浏览器中实时运行的语义通信演示平台。

Method: 构建一个浏览器交互式语义通信平台：从四路实时交通摄像头提取行人信息，经过语义通信收发机在 AWGN 信道上传输，并将特定用户的位置、轨迹和风险标签渲染到各浏览器。平台允许独立选择收发机、SNR、码长和 AoI，并通过实时行人安全监控展示所选配置的性能。核心收发机采用近期提出的 Meta-VIB（紧凑神经模型，约 416 万参数），可在不同 SNR、码长和 AoI 下泛化而无需在线重训练，并与五种基线收发机对比。

Result: 实验结果显示：Meta-VIB 相对基线收发机平均任务损失最多降低 92.1%；在单台高端 GPU 服务器上，集成式并发访问工作负载在 20 个用户时保持目标 5 帧/秒；100 个用户各自请求不同配置时，无请求失败、平均应用响应时间低于 1 秒，但平均每浏览器帧率降至约 1 帧/秒。

Conclusion: SafeStep 据作者所知是首个可在实时监控应用中直接观察 AoI 引起的下游性能退化的实时语义通信平台，验证了 Meta-VIB 在真实端到端场景中的有效性与可扩展性，并揭示了并发用户数增加时系统吞吐与帧率的权衡边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop SafeStep, an interactive browser-based semantic communication platform for live pedestrian safety monitoring. SafeStep extracts pedestrian information from four live traffic-camera feeds, transmits it through a semantic communication transceiver over an Additive White Gaussian Noise (AWGN) channel, and renders user-specific positions, trajectories, and risk labels. The platform allows to independently select the transceiver, Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), codelength, and Age of Information (AoI), and demonstrates the transceiver performance of the selected configuration through live pedestrian safety monitoring to each browser. SafeStep compares a recently proposed semantic communication design called Meta-VIB with five baseline transceivers. Meta-VIB uses a compact neural model with only $4.16$ million parameters to generalize across varying SNR, codelength, and AoI values without online retraining. Experimental results show that Meta-VIB achieves mean task-loss reductions of up to $92.1\%$. On one high-end GPU server, the integrated concurrent-access workload maintains the target $5$ frames/s through $20$ users. At $100$ users, each requesting a distinct configuration, SafeStep records no request failures and a mean application response time below $1$ s, but its mean per-browser frame rate falls to approximately $1$ frame/s. To our knowledge, SafeStep is the first real-time semantic communication platform to make AoI-induced downstream degradation directly observable in live monitoring applications.

</details>


### [152] [RiskBlend: A Multi-Signal Framework for Test Input Prioritization in Machine Learning Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27704)
*Madhusudan Srinivasan,Namith Nishal Raphae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RiskBlend，一个分类器无关的测试输入优先级排序框架，通过融合四种互补的风险信号（历史失败模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移、邻域变化），并在验证集上学习 APFD 平方加权，以在有限验证预算内最大化回归故障的检出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分类器重训练后，先前模型版本能正确分类的输入可能被新版模型误分类，产生回归故障。由于验证预测需要人工标注、专家审查或昂贵仿真而非廉价推理，检测成本高昂。现有测试输入优先级排序方法主要依赖单模型置信度分数，未利用预测、决策边界和局部邻域在模型版本间的变化，因此效果受限。

Method: 提出 RiskBlend，一种分类器无关的优先级排序框架，组合四种互补风险信号：历史失败模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移和邻域变化。这些信号通过验证集上学习的 APFD 平方加权进行融合；实验覆盖 4 个数据集、5 个分类器、4 种回归更新场景和 15 个随机种子，共 1,200 个实验配置。

Result: 在全部 80 个数据集-分类器-场景组合中，RiskBlend 均取得最高平均 APFD，相对最强基线最高提升 0.32 APFD。基于置信度的方法仅在稀疏类别特征的线性分类器上保持竞争力，这归因于特征空间的几何特性。

Conclusion: 跨版本行为信号为机器学习系统的回归故障优先级排序提供了重要的互补信息，RiskBlend 验证了融合多源跨版本信号相较于仅依赖单一模型置信度方法的显著优势。

Abstract: When machine learning classifiers are retrained, inputs correctly classified by the previous model version may be misclassified by the updated version, creating regression faults that are costly to detect because verifying predictions against ground truth may require human annotation, expert review, or expensive simulation rather than inexpensive model inference. Test input prioritization addresses this problem by ranking inputs so that a limited verification budget reveals as many regression faults as possible. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single-model confidence scores and do not exploit how predictions, decision boundaries, and local neighborhoods change between model versions. We propose RiskBlend, a classifier-agnostic prioritization framework that combines four complementary risk signals: historical failure patterns, prediction shift, decision-boundary shift, and neighborhood change. These signals are combined using validation-learned APFD-squared weighting. Across four datasets, five classifiers, four regression-update scenarios, and 15 random seeds, totaling 1,200 experimental configurations, RiskBlend achieves the highest average APFD in all 80 dataset-classifier-scenario combinations, with improvements of up to 0.32 APFD over the strongest baseline. Confidence-based methods remain competitive primarily for linear classifiers on sparse categorical features, which we attribute to feature-space geometry. The results show that cross-version behavioral signals provide important complementary information for prioritizing regression faults in machine learning systems.

</details>


### [153] [DART-FL: Burst-Aware Multitask Federated Learning under Dynamic Inference Demand at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27713)
*Yiming Xie,Pinrui Yu,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DART-FL，一个面向资源受限边缘设备的 SLO 感知、需求驱动的多任务联邦学习框架，联合优化推理-训练资源分配与任务级训练侧重，使推理突发期的高需求任务获得更早、更强的训练权重，从而在不牺牲长期多任务性能的前提下提升突发期的模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统需要在资源受限设备上同时进行模型训练和在线推理，而不同任务的推理需求随时间波动。这带来两个耦合挑战：一方面要为推理预留足够计算资源以满足服务级目标（SLO），另一方面剩余训练容量应随任务需求自适应调整，使高频请求的任务能在训练早期更快提升。

Method: 在每个调度间隔，DART-FL 依据推理积压量和已测服务容量确定推理所需的最小资源分配，然后将剩余训练容量通过一个基于队列感知的 DPP 启发的调度器分配给各任务，并将得到的任务分配映射为动态损失权重。客户端训练共享主干加任务特定头的多任务模型，通过 FedAvg 聚合。

Result: 在 Stanford Cars 和 Oxford Flowers 102 数据集上，结合合成负载与真实阿里云 trace 负载进行评估，结果表明 DART-FL 能动态调整推理-训练资源分配以适应时变推理需求，将高需求任务的学习进度集中于突发期，在高频请求时段显著提升模型精度，同时保持可比的长期多任务性能。

Conclusion: DART-FL 验证了在边缘多任务联邦学习中将推理 SLO 与训练需求联合调度的可行性与有效性，为资源受限设备上训练与推理共存场景提供了需求感知的资源分配方案。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems increasingly require model training and online inference to coexist on resource-constrained devices, while inference demand can vary substantially across tasks over time. This creates two coupled challenges: sufficient computation must be reserved for inference to maintain service-level objectives (SLOs), while the remaining training capacity should adapt to task-specific demand so that frequently requested tasks can improve earlier during training.
  We propose an SLO-aware, demand-driven multitask federated learning framework (DART-FL) that jointly adapts the inference-training resource split and task-level training emphasis. At each scheduling interval, DART-FL uses the inference backlog and profiled service capacity to determine the minimum resource allocation required for inference. The remaining training capacity is then distributed across tasks using a queue-aware DPP-inspired scheduler, and the resulting task allocations are mapped to dynamic loss weights. This allows tasks experiencing higher inference demand to receive greater training emphasis in earlier communication rounds. Clients train a shared backbone with task-specific heads, and the complete multitask model is aggregated through FedAvg.
  We evaluate DART-FL using Stanford Cars and Oxford Flowers 102 under both synthetic and real Alibaba trace-derived workloads. Results show that DART-FL dynamically adapts the inference-training resource split to time-varying inference demand and shifts the learning progress of high-demand tasks toward their burst periods, improving model accuracy when those tasks are frequently requested while maintaining comparable long-term multitask performance.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Non-IID: Learner--Client Distribution Mismatch in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27715)
*Yiming Xie,Lili Su,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态的、影响力感知的客户端选择框架（用于联邦学习），在客户端数据分布与学习者的目标分布不一致的情况下，利用学习者保留的小型代理数据集上计算的代理影响力信号，识别并优先选择对各轮训练最有信息量的客户端，同时控制统计噪声和数据异质性的负面影响，从而加快收敛并提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习实践大多隐含假设聚合后的客户端数据分布能代表学习者的目标分布，或所有客户端对学习者均匀有益，但这一假设在现实中常常不成立。传统客户端选择策略忽略了这种"学习者-客户端群体错配"，而多数多源迁移学习工作要么需要直接访问本地数据，要么采用一次性的模型/特征聚合。因此作者希望理解和缓解这种错配带来的影响。

Method: 在学习者持有一小份代理数据集的实用设定下，作者观察到客户端贡献在不同训练轮次间差异显著，而传统技术难以在多源迁移多样性下识别有益来源。为此，他们提出动态、影响力感知的客户端选择框架：通过学习者专属代理集上的代理影响力信号估计每个客户端对学习者优化目标的潜在效用，并利用留一法（leave-one-out）评估来优先选择最具信息量的知识来源，同时控制统计噪声和数据异质性带来的负面影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-10异构数据划分下的实验表明，所提方法一致优于静态和动态基线，实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 作者开创性地针对学习者与客户端群体之间的分布错配问题，证明了基于代理数据集的影响力感知动态客户端选择能够更有效地利用异构客户端知识，显著提升联邦学习在目标分布上的性能。

Abstract: Federated learning systems are increasingly deployed to facilitate collaborative model training across a heterogeneous client population. Existing practice mostly implicitly assumes that the aggregated client data distribution is representative of the learner's target distribution or that learning from all available clients is uniformly beneficial for the learner distribution. However, such an assumption often does not hold in reality. Traditional client selection strategies in FL literature largely overlook such misalignment, while most existing work on multi-source transfer learning either requires direct access to local data or uses one-shot model/feature aggregation.
  In this paper, we take the initiative to understand and mitigate the impacts of such learner-client population misalignment. In particular, we consider the practical setting where the learner keeps a small proxy dataset. We observe that client contributions vary significantly across training rounds, and traditional technology is insufficient to identify beneficial sources under multi-source transfer diversity. Then, we propose a dynamic, influence-aware client selection framework that estimates each client's potential utility to the learner's optimization objective using proxy influence signals on a learner-specific proxy set. Via using leave-one-out evaluations, we prioritize the most informative sources of knowledge while controlling the negative impacts of statistical noise and data heterogeneity. Experiments on CIFAR-10 under heterogeneous data partitions demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms static and dynamic baselines, achieving faster convergence and higher accuracy.

</details>


### [155] [Leveraging a Foundation Model for the EEG-Based Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27719)
*Maggie Lin,Chung-Lin Hou,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大规模脑电预训练模型 LaBraM 与非线性随机森林分类器相结合的阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断框架，在仅使用 8 秒 EEG 片段、受试者独立 5 折交叉验证下，实现 ROC-AUC 89.36%、PR AUC 81.45%、平衡准确率 82.44%，优于频带功率与 FOOOF 等传统频谱基线，并通过遮挡分析与神经生理学对齐验证了其捕捉的枕-额 Alpha/Theta 节律退化等临床生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD 的生物学异质性给诊断带来重大挑战，传统线性方法无法捕捉大脑中非线性的神经动力学特征，因而难以从噪声 EEG 信号中稳健地识别疾病标志物，需要一种能够提取高维非线性表征的诊断方案。

Method: 采用在大规模 EEG 数据（超过 2500 小时）上预训练的大型脑模型 LaBraM 提取高维潜在嵌入，并将其与非线性随机森林分类器相结合；在严格的受试者独立 5 折交叉验证协议下进行脑电痴呆分类，并辅以遮挡分析（occlusion analysis）和神经生理学对齐分析验证模型所学习特征的临床意义。

Result: 在区分痴呆患者与健康对照组时，该方法仅使用 8 秒 EEG 片段即达到 ROC-AUC 89.36%±3.49%、PR AUC 81.45%±4.43%、平衡准确率 82.44%±4.34%，超越频带功率与参数化振荡特征（FOOOF）等传统频谱基线；遮挡分析确认模型捕捉到枕-额 Alpha 与 Theta 节律退化等临床验证生物标志物；LaBraM 预测的痴呆概率与更差的认知表现、更高的临床严重程度、增加的 Theta/Alpha 相对功率及更高的无周期指数显著相关。

Conclusion: 深度潜在表征能够从噪声信号中提取临床相关的脑电特征，实现精确、快速且数据高效的 AD 诊断，验证了基于大规模预训练脑模型的方法在识别 AD 异质性生物标志物方面的有效性与临床价值。

Abstract: Biological heterogeneity in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) poses a critical diagnostic challenge, particularly for traditional linear methods that fail to capture non-linear neural dynamics. To address this, we propose a diagnostic framework utilizing the Large Brain Model (LaBraM), pretrained on over 2,500 hours of EEG data. By integrating these high-dimensional latent embeddings with a non-linear Random Forest classifier, our approach effectively isolates robust disease markers. Under a rigorous subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation protocol, the method achieves an ROC-AUC of 89.36% +/- 3.49%, PR AUC of 81.45% +/- 4.43%, and Balanced Accuracy of 82.44% +/- 4.34% in distinguishing dementia patients from healthy controls. Notably, this performance uses only 8-second EEG segments, surpassing traditional spectral baselines, including band-power and parameterized oscillatory features (FOOOF). Post-hoc occlusion analysis confirms the model captures clinically validated biomarkers, specifically occipital-frontal Alpha and Theta rhythm degradation. Additional neurophysiological alignment analysis demonstrated that higher LaBraM-predicted dementia probability significantly correlated with worse cognitive performance, greater clinical severity, increased theta and alpha relative power, and higher aperiodic exponent. These findings demonstrate that deep latent representations extract clinically relevant signatures from noisy signals, enabling precise, rapid, and data-efficient diagnosis.

</details>


### [156] [Diffusion Distillation for Efficient Weather Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27728)
*Yiming Yang,Valentin Brekke,James Briant,Serge Guillas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有监督的能量距离蒸馏方法，将多步扩散模型教师压缩为单步学生模型，在天气预报和台风路径预测中优于现有蒸馏方法，且仅需一次神经函数评估即可匹配甚至超越教师模型的精度，并能保持极端事件的预报技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能生成高质量的气象集合预报，但其迭代采样过程计算成本高昂，难以满足业务化预报对时效性的要求，因此需要一种蒸馏方法将多步采样压缩为单步推理。

Method: 提出有监督的能量距离蒸馏（supervised energy-distance distillation）：通过最小化学生预测与教师采样样本、以及真实观测之间的能量距离，将多步扩散教师蒸馏为单步学生模型，学生仅需一次神经函数评估即可完成每个自回归步的预测。

Result: 在全局天气预报和台风路径预测实验中，学生模型优于现有蒸馏方法；在极端事件的技巧保持上表现良好，且在所有关键指标上达到或超过教师模型，同时将每步推理成本降至单次神经函数评估。

Conclusion: 有监督的能量距离蒸馏能有效在保持预报质量（包括极端事件）的前提下大幅降低扩散模型采样成本，为高效气象集合预报提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate skillful weather ensembles but require costly iterative sampling. We introduce a supervised energy-distance distillation method that compresses a multi-step diffusion teacher into a single-step student by aligning student forecasts with teacher samples and ground-truth observations. Experiments on global forecasting and typhoon-track prediction show that our student outperforms existing distillation methods and preserves skill for extreme events. It matches or surpasses the teacher across key metrics using only one neural function evaluation per autoregressive step.

</details>


### [157] [The Calls are Coming from Inside the Model: Investigating Probe-based Detection of Tool-Calling Errors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27750)
*Eric Yeats,Brendan Kennedy,Loc Truong,John Buckheit,Jung Lee,Jesse Friedbaum,John Emanuello,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究用线性探针（linear probes）从大语言模型的隐藏状态中检测错误的工具调用（tool-call）。作者在 Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard 上对18个工具调用型LLM进行了系统评估，发现探针能有效捕获多种工具调用错误（包括“类型正确但值错误”这类难以被常规日志记录的问题），且探针性能受模型规模、探针层位置和模型后训练类型影响，并具备对新型错误的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的系统越来越多地与外部世界交互（调用工具），检测错误或不恰当的工具使用变得日益重要。LLM的隐藏状态包含难以从输入/输出中直接提取的知识与行为信息，这为从内部状态入手检测工具调用错误提供了可能，但目前对此研究不足。

Method: 采用线性探针方法对LLM隐藏状态进行分类，以检测错误的工具调用。实验在18个工具调用LLM上进行，评测基准为Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard；系统评估了探针在不同错误类型、模型大小、探针层及模型后训练类型下的表现，并考察探针对新型（未见过的）错误类型的泛化能力。

Result: 线性探针能有效捕获一系列不同的工具调用错误，包括“参数类型正确但取值错误”的错误（这类错误通常不会被标准日志框架记录）。影响探针成功率的关键因素包括模型规模、探针所在层以及模型的后期训练类型。此外，探针能够泛化到新型错误上，这对实际部署至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究表明基于隐藏状态的线性探测是检测LLM工具调用错误的有效手段，能够捕获常规日志难以发现的隐式错误，并具备面向新型错误的泛化性，可作为实际部署中工具使用监控与安全防护的有力补充。

Abstract: The hidden states of large language models (LLMs) are known to capture rich information relating to model knowledge and behavior that can be hard to extract from examination of input and output alone. As LLM-based systems increasingly interface with the external world, one area of concern is detecting incorrect or improper use of tools. Motivated by this, we study the effectiveness of using linear probes to detect incorrect tool-calls, measuring probe efficacy across 18 tool-calling LLMs evaluated on the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard. Overall, we find that probing is an effective means to catch a range of different tool-calling errors, including errors arising from using an argument that has the wrong value but the correct type, which might not be recorded by standard logging frameworks. Important factors in success include model size, probing layer, and model post-training type. We also show that probes are capable of generalizing to novel types of errors, which is critical in real world deployments.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Search-Imitation: Prior-Directed Exploration for Searchless Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27757)
*Szymon Miłosz,Piotr Duch,Szymon Grabowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过模仿Lc0的Chessformer进行单次前向传播的搜索less国际象棋网络已达到人类大师水平，但模仿搜索并不能很好地替代无搜索对弈。本文用自对弈强化学习对单次前向传播强度进行微调，将原本的熵奖励（对均匀分布的逆向KL）替换为网络自身MCTS先验的前向质量覆盖KL（先验引导探索），并搭配由价值头不确定性驱动的熵自适应采样温度。约2000步内将谜题准确率从93.9%提升到94.9%、四步杀准确率从77%提升到81%，同时保持搜索less强度不降。关键发现是战术准确率与对弈强度会分离：只做谜题微调的对照组取得最大战术提升却损失约260 Elo，说明更好的解谜者并不等同于更强的棋手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最强的搜索less网络（Lc0的Chessformer）通过蒸馏AlphaZero式MCTS的访问计数来获得单次前向传播的棋力；但模仿搜索并非“不搜索就能下好棋”的良好代理，因为搜索的多步前瞻过程无法被单次前向传播直接替代。因此需要对单次前向传播强度进行自对弈强化学习微调，并改进探索机制。

Method: 用自对弈强化学习对搜索less网络进行微调。具体创新包括：1）将常规的熵奖励（对均匀分布的逆向KL）替换为对网络自身MCTS先验的前向质量覆盖KL（先验引导探索），使探索集中在先验认为有希望的着法上；2）引入熵自适应采样温度，由价值头对结果的不确定性设定，当局面已定时温度变尖锐；3）通过匹配计算量的对比实验、谜题套件、对局等级分和分布级测量来系统评估。

Result: 约2000步微调将10万谜题套件的准确率从93.9%提升至94.9%，四步杀准确率从77%提升至81%，且搜索less强度保持或略高于基线。但战术准确率与对弈强度分离：准确率提升落在一个百分点区间内而等级分围绕基线摆动；仅用谜题微调的对照组取得最大的战术提升却损失约260 Elo。分布级测量显示，没有正则化时自对弈坍缩到单一着法序列，新解出的谜题都是先验仍保留胜着“差一点”的题；前向KL先验在等级分榜居首，与一个集中两倍、丢失质量覆盖先验所保留的最难解的逆向KL锚点在统计上持平。

Conclusion: 前向KL先验引导的探索是搜索less国际象棋网络自对弈微调的有效方法，能在不损失对弈强度的前提下提升战术能力。然而更好的解谜者并不必然是更强的棋手（战术准确率与棋力会分离），这也说明仅凭可解谜题率的指标不足以衡量搜索less棋力，需要同时考察对弈强度与分布级行为。

Abstract: Searchless chess networks reach human master strength from a single forward pass by imitating a stronger teacher: the strongest, Leela Chess Zero's (Lc0) released Chessformer, distills the visit counts of an AlphaZero-style Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). Imitating a search is a poor proxy for playing without one, so we fine-tune for single-pass strength with self-play reinforcement learning (RL). Its exploration is usually supplied by an entropy bonus, the reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence to uniform. We replace it with a forward, mass-covering KL toward the network's own MCTS prior (prior-directed exploration), so exploration covers the moves the prior judges promising, and pair it with an entropy-adaptive sampling temperature, set by the value head's outcome uncertainty, that sharpens once a position is decided. In about two thousand steps it raises puzzle accuracy from 93.9% to 94.9% on a 100,000-puzzle suite and mate-in-four accuracy from 77% to 81% while holding searchless strength at or slightly above the base. Measuring tactical accuracy and playing strength together across a matched-compute sweep, we find the two dissociate: accuracy gains fall in a one-point band while ratings straddle the base, and a control fine-tuned on puzzles alone posts the study's largest tactical gains while shedding roughly 260 Elo; a better puzzle-solver is not thereby a stronger player. Distribution-level measurements show what anchoring buys: without a regularizer self-play collapses onto a single line of play, and the puzzles newly solved are the near misses whose winning move the prior kept alive. The forward-KL prior tops the rating ladder, statistically tied with a reverse-KL anchor that concentrates twice as hard and drops the hardest solutions the mass-covering prior keeps in support.

</details>


### [159] [Fast Weight Attention for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27763)
*Yifan Zhang,Steve Ta,Jasper Zhang,Jichen Feng,Shuzhen Li,Yongxin Zhang,Yifeng Liu,Huizhuo Yuan,Mengdi Wang,Quanquan Gu,Andrew Chi-Chih Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在"先写后读"自回归语义下研究循环快权重记忆与选择性状态空间模型的状态转移学习规则，发现前缀预测目标要求训练样本为前缀对齐对(φ(k_{t-1}), v_t)，而非常用的同一步关联(φ(k_t), v_t)。作者据此推导了基于平方误差回归和负内积目标的归一化一阶更新，得到 Falcon-1/2/3 系列（回归族）及 Falcon-1A/2A/3A（内积族）变体，并提供循环、掩码并行与分块并行形式及数值稳定的正衰减重归一化。代表性变体在语言建模中保持竞争力，并能改进变长数字加法的长度外推，为循环序列模型提供了时间对齐、可塑性、遗忘与有界重演的分离框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环快权重记忆与选择性状态空间模型将不断扩展的上下文压缩为固定大小的循环状态，其状态转移本质上是一种在线学习规则。然而该规则在"读后写"（read-after-write）自回归语义下的正确形式尚未被厘清——尤其是训练样本的时间对齐方式：常见做法使用同一步关联(φ(k_t), v_t)，但它优化的其实是另一种内部目标，而非前缀预测目标。

Method: 作者明确"前缀预测"目标下的局部快记忆样本应为前缀对齐对(x_t, y_t)=(φ(k_{t-1}), v_t)，并针对平方误差回归与负内积两种目标推导归一化一阶更新。回归族给出 Falcon-1（标量 NLMS）、Falcon-2（逐列扩展）、Falcon-3（滑动窗口小批量）；对应内积变体为 Falcon-1A/2A/3A。同时提供循环、掩码并行、分块并行三种计算形式，以及数值稳定的正衰减重归一化技巧。

Result: 代表性 Falcon 变体在语言建模任务上与既有方法保持竞争力，并在变长数字加法任务上显著改善长度外推能力，验证了前缀对齐样本设计与传统同步关联假设之间的差异。

Conclusion: 该工作作为一个统一框架，将循环序列模型中的四个关键维度——时间对齐（temporal alignment）、可塑性（plasticity）、遗忘（forgetting）与有界重演（bounded rehearsal）——在理论上与算法上加以分离和刻画，为后续设计可解释、可外推的循环记忆模型提供了基础。

Abstract: Recurrent fast-weight memories and selective state-space models compress an expanding context into a fixed-size recurrent state, making the state transition an online learning rule. We study this rule under read-after-write autoregressive semantics. For the prefix-prediction objective considered here, the local fast-memory example revealed at step $t$ is the prefix-aligned pair $(\mathbf{x}_t,\mathbf{y}_t)=(φ(\mathbf{k}_{t-1}),\mathbf{v}_t)$. The common same-step association $(φ(\mathbf{k}_t),\mathbf{v}_t)$ remains causal, but optimizes a different internal objective. We derive normalized first-order updates for squared-error regression and negative inner-product objectives. The regression family comprises Falcon-1 (a scalar NLMS update), Falcon-2 (its per-column extension), and Falcon-3 (a sliding-window mini-batch update); Falcon-1A/Falcon-2A/Falcon-3A are the corresponding inner-product variants. We provide recurrent, masked-parallel, and chunk-parallel forms, together with numerically stable positive-decay renormalization. Representative variants remain competitive in language modeling and improve length extrapolation on variable-digit addition. This framework separates temporal alignment, plasticity, forgetting, and bounded rehearsal in recurrent sequence models.

</details>


### [160] [Initialization Is Critical: Advancing Federated Short-Term Load Forecasting under Load Heterogeneity via Model Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27791)
*Jianing Chen,Vajiheh Farhadi,Yan Li,Thomas La Porta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习在短期负荷预测（STLF）中面临的客户端数据结构化异质性（对外生因素响应不同、时序负荷形态各异）问题，从模型初始化的角度提出了两种策略：基于公开辅助数据的全局预训练初始化策略，以及本地顺序初始化策略SLIAvg。两种策略仅修改初始化过程，可兼容现有联邦学习框架与隐私增强技术。在真实智能电表数据上的实验表明，所提策略能有效减少客户端漂移、改善收敛行为并降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期负荷预测对现代电力系统至关重要，但准确预测常依赖分布式用户的细粒度智能电表数据，引发数据隐私担忧。联邦学习因此成为有前景的隐私保护范式。然而本文揭示出客户端负荷数据存在结构化异质性：不同客户端对外生因素（如天气、节假日等）的响应不同，且具有不同的时序负荷形态，这会降低联邦学习的预测性能。因此需要缓解这种异质性带来的负面影响。

Method: 本文研究模型初始化在联邦短期负荷预测中的作用，提出两种初始化策略：1）全局视角：当存在辅助公共负荷数据时，采用预训练初始化策略，在联邦训练前初始化全局模型，以减少训练过程中的客户端漂移；2）局部视角：提出SLIAvg（顺序本地初始化策略），在每个通信轮内允许参与客户端从逐步适配的模型开始训练，从而促进更一致的训练过程。由于仅修改初始化环节，两种策略与大多数现有FL框架和隐私增强技术兼容。

Result: 在真实智能电表数据上，使用两种代表性预测架构（两种神经网络模型）进行实验，结果表明所提策略有效提升了预测性能，具体表现为客户端漂移减少、收敛行为改善以及预测误差降低。

Conclusion: 模型初始化是缓解联邦短期负荷预测中客户端结构化异质性问题的有效切入点。全局预训练初始化与本地顺序初始化SLIAvg两种策略均能提升联邦STLF的预测精度与训练稳定性，且具有良好通用性，为联邦学习下的负荷预测提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting (STLF) provides essential information for numerous applications in modern power systems. However, accurate STLF often relies on fine-grained smart-meter data from distributed users, raising increasing concerns about data privacy. Federated learning (FL) has therefore emerged as a promising privacy-preserving paradigm for STLF. Nevertheless, this paper reveals structured heterogeneity in clients' load data. Specifically, clients exhibit different responses to exogenous factors and distinct temporal load profiles, which can degrade forecasting performance in FL. To mitigate these issues, this paper studies the role of model initialization in federated STLF, and proposes two initialization strategies from global and local perspectives. For global model initialization, when auxiliary public load data are available, a pretrained initialization strategy is developed to initialize the global model before federated training, thereby reducing client drift during the training process. For local model initialization, we propose SLIAvg, a sequential local initialization strategy that promotes a more consistent training process by allowing participating clients to start from progressively adapted models within each communication round. Since the proposed strategies only modify the initialization process, they are compatible with most existing FL frameworks and privacy-enhancing techniques. Experiments on real smart-meter data with two representative forecasting architectures demonstrate that the proposed strategies effectively improve forecasting performance, as evidenced by reduced client drift, improved convergence behavior, and lower forecasting errors.

</details>


### [161] [Node-wise Feature Encoding for Neural Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27794)
*Matthew Grenier,William Hammer,Andrew Heuer,Nikhil Krishna,Yi Wang,Ramtin Zand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FeatureFormer，一种用于边缘设备神经网络性能预测的模型，通过门控图注意力架构中显式的节点级计算量编码（FLOPs、参数量、内存代理）来提升延迟和能耗预测精度；同时提出大规模能耗数据集NNEQ，实验表明在两个指标上均达到SOTA，包括困难的域外场景，且该编码方式可通用地改进现有预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN和Transformer的性能预测器虽取得较好结果，但大多忽略节点级计算成本，难以准确建模对性能至关重要的算子操作（如特殊算子、内存受限操作），从而限制了在资源受限边缘设备上进行高效神经架构搜索时的延迟与能耗预测精度。

Method: 1) 引入FeatureFormer：在门控图注意力架构中融入显式的节点级编码，包括FLOPs、参数量及内存代理特征；2) 提出NNEQ，一个大规模能耗数据集，支持延迟与能耗预测的统一评估。

Result: FeatureFormer在延迟和能耗两个指标上均达到最先进水平，在具有挑战性的域外（out-of-domain）设置下依然表现优异；提出的节点级编码方式可广泛适用，能几乎无开销地一致提升现有预测器性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模节点级计算成本能够显著提升神经网络性能预测的准确性与泛化能力，为资源受限边缘设备上的神经架构搜索提供了更可靠的性能预估工具，同时验证了该编码方案的通用性和轻量性。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed on resource constrained edge devices, accurate prediction of latency and energy is critical for efficient neural architecture search. Existing GNN and transformer based predictors achieve strong results but largely ignore node-level computational cost, limiting their ability to model performance critical operations. To address this, we introduce FeatureFormer, a neural performance predictor that incorporates explicit node-wise encodings of FLOPs, parameter counts, and memory proxies within a gated graph attention architecture. We also present NNEQ, a new large-scale energy consumption dataset that enables unified evaluation of latency and energy prediction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FeatureFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance across both metrics, including challenging out-of-domain settings. Finally, we show that the proposed encoding is broadly applicable and consistently improves existing predictors with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [162] [Actionable CBFI: Integrating Structural Decomposition and Causal Counterfactual Recourse for Tabular Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27821)
*Sejong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A-CBFI（可操作的基于案例的特征重要性），一种面向表格机器学习、集诊断与处方于一体的可解释AI框架。它基于结构因果模型（SCM）识别协同交互瓶颈并释放抑制性结构锁，将干预努力集中在诊断出的根因上，在金融与医疗领域的实证中，将主动人类干预负担降低76.9%，同时保持与穷举因果基线相当的全局追索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可操作的反事实追索方法面临三大挑战：因果无效性、认知负担过重和预测失败。穷举式因果搜索需要修改多个属性，而基于加性归因的方法（如SHAP）忽略高阶特征协同效应，在XGBoost等复杂非线性模型中导致预测动量欠佳和干预效果分散。因此需要一个既能诊断瓶颈又能给出精准干预方案的一体化框架。

Method: A-CBFI基于结构因果模型，先诊断并隔离协同交互瓶颈、释放抑制性结构锁，将其转化为目标干预措施；在数学上将用户的主动干预空间（L_active）与下游效应分离，并将超过98.3%的干预努力集中于诊断出的根因。整体是一个诊断-处方集成框架，适用于表格机器学习（如XGBoost）。

Result: 在金融和医疗领域的实证评估中，A-CBFI将主动人类干预负担降低76.9%，同时全局追索成本与穷举因果基线相当；在保持因果有效性的前提下，对所有因果可行实例实现完全相对收敛。

Conclusion: 通过优先处理经过诊断的因果瓶颈，A-CBFI能够提供高针对性、可操作的追索建议，同时保持因果有效性并实现完整收敛，有效弥合了加性归因方法与穷举因果搜索之间的差距。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) increasingly calls for actionable counterfactual recourse, yet current methodologies face challenges related to causal invalidity, excessive cognitive burden, and predictive failure. Exhaustive causal search algorithms often require modifications to multiple attributes, whereas additive attribution-guided methods, such as SHAP, ignore higher-order feature synergies, leading to suboptimal predictive momentum and diffuse intervention effort in complex nonlinear models, such as XGBoost. To bridge this gap, we introduce actionable case-based feature importance (A-CBFI), a diagnosis-prescription integrated framework for tabular machine learning. Grounded in structural causal models (SCMs), A-CBFI isolates synergistic interaction bottlenecks and releases suppressive structural locks, translating them into targeted interventions. By mathematically separating the active user intervention space (L_{\mathrm{active}}) from downstream effects and concentrating over 98.3% of the intervention effort on diagnosed root causes, A-CBFI enables highly targeted interventions. Empirical evaluations across the financial and healthcare domains demonstrate that A-CBFI reduces the active human intervention burden by 76.9% while maintaining comparable global recourse cost to exhaustive causal baselines. By prioritizing the diagnosed causal bottlenecks, A-CBFI provides targeted and actionable recourse while maintaining causal validity and achieving full relative convergence across all causally feasible instances.

</details>


### [163] [FedEHR-Agents: Federated Agentic Optimization for Automated EHR Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27856)
*Jun Bai,Ruilin Wang,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedEHR-Agents 提出了一种以“经验”为中心、而非以“参数”为中心的联邦式智能体优化框架，让各医院部署的临床 EHR 智能体在本地完成数据处理与模型开发并沉淀建模经验，再通过联邦服务器进行证据引导的经验聚合与全局元提示蒸馏，最终在真实多医院 EHR 基准上优于本地与联邦基线，展现出联邦自主临床智能的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的自主临床智能体可在各医院内部执行复杂的 EHR 建模工作流，但单个医院受限于机构专属的数据与建模环境；跨医院直接协作又因患者级 EHR 数据敏感而受限。现有联邦学习虽然支持隐私保护协作，但主要停留在模型中心视角（只联邦预测模型或其更新），忽略了自主智能体在建模过程中积累的丰富“经验”这一更具价值的协作对象。

Method: 每个医院部署一个自主临床 EHR 智能体，负责数据预处理和模型开发，并通过历史记忆、任务专属评估以及 TextGrad 提示词优化来不断精炼本地临床建模经验；联邦服务器采用证据引导的经验聚合机制，整合异质医院间的可靠且互补的经验，并将聚合结果蒸馏为全局元提示（meta-prompts），再下发到各医院进行后续本地微调与迭代。

Result: 在真实世界多医院 EHR 基准上的大量实验表明，FedEHR-Agents 在多种临床预测任务上一致优于本地与联邦基线，并且在不同联邦规模和不同 LLM 主干下均保持稳健的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该工作证明“临床建模经验”可以成为超越传统参数中心联邦学习的有效协作对象，开辟了以经验为中心、由联邦机制支撑的自主临床智能方向，为跨医院隐私保护下的协作式 EHR 建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models are enabling autonomous clinical agents to perform increasingly complex electronic health record (EHR) modeling workflows. However, agents deployed at individual hospitals remain constrained by institution-specific data and modeling environments, while direct cross-hospital collaboration is restricted by the sensitivity of patient-level EHR data. Although federated learning (FL) provides a natural foundation for privacy-preserving collaboration, existing approaches remain predominantly model-centric, limiting federation to prediction models or their updates while overlooking the richer modeling experience accumulated by autonomous agents. To address this limitation, we propose FedEHR-Agents, an experience-centric federated agentic optimization framework for automated EHR modeling. Each hospital deploys an autonomous clinical EHR agent that performs data preprocessing and model development while refining local clinical modeling experience through historical memory, task-specific evaluation, and TextGrad-based prompt refinement. The federated server performs evidence-guided experience aggregation to integrate reliable and complementary modeling experience across heterogeneous hospitals and distills the aggregated experience into global meta-prompts for subsequent local refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world multi-hospital EHR benchmarks demonstrate that FedEHR-Agents consistently outperforms local and federated baselines across diverse clinical prediction tasks and remains robust across different federation scales and LLM backbones. These results establish clinical modeling experience as a promising collaborative object beyond conventional parameter-centric FL and point toward federated autonomous clinical intelligence.

</details>


### [164] [SOMTab: Set-Order Mamba for Efficient Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27882)
*Hao Wang,Siyu Zhang,Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SOMTab（Set-Order Mamba）架构，用于高效的表格数据上下文学习。其核心思想是将表征构建与查询条件检索解耦：行/列表征使用 Mamba 状态空间混合构建紧凑表示，最终预测阶段保留注意力机制进行上下文检索，并辅以 DCH-TailMix 合成先验增强依赖结构多样性，在接近强 Transformer 基座模型精度的同时实现更快推理与更低显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格基础模型多基于上下文学习，但当前性能前沿几乎完全由注意力密集型架构主导，注意力被贯穿于建模全流程。这引出一个自然问题：表格上下文学习是否在每个阶段都需要注意力？作者旨在探索更高效的架构，减少对全流程注意力的依赖，同时保持竞争力。

Method: 提出 SOMTab 混合架构，将表征构建与查询条件检索分离：(1) 行/列表征阶段：将无序表格 token 映射到稳定的潜在槽位，并用基于 Mamba 的状态空间混合构建紧凑表示；(2) 最终预测阶段：保留基于注意力的上下文学习以维持对标注示例的查询条件检索。此外引入 DCH-TailMix 合成先验，将度校正图异质性与混合重尾机制结合，以多样化合成依赖结构。

Result: 在多个表格基准上，SOMTab 在性能上接近强 Transformer 表格基础模型，同时实现更快的推理速度和更低的 GPU 显存占用，取得有利的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 注意力并非在表格上下文学习的每个阶段都不可或缺：在表征构建阶段可用 Mamba 状态空间模型替代注意力以提升效率，而在查询条件检索阶段保留注意力可维持精度。该类混合设计为构建高效表格基础模型提供了可行且可扩展的方向。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models based on in-context learning have recently emerged as strong alternatives to task-specific model fitting. However, the current performance frontier remains dominated by attention-heavy architectures, where attention is used throughout the modeling pipeline. This raises a natural question: is attention necessary at every stage of tabular in-context learning? We introduce SOMTab, a Set-Order Mamba architecture for efficient tabular in-context learning. SOMTab separates representation construction from query-conditioned retrieval. For row and column representations, it maps unordered table tokens into stable latent slots and applies Mamba-based state-space mixing to construct compact representations. For final prediction, it retains attention-based in-context learning to preserve query-conditioned retrieval from labeled context examples. We further introduce DCH-TailMix, a synthetic prior that combines degree-corrected graph heterogeneity with mixed heavy-tailed regimes to diversify synthetic dependency structures.
