Finally, we characterize the dependence of loss on model capacity and dataset size, evaluating recently proposed scaling forms that explicitly model their interaction. We find these approaches particularly effective at capturing both undertraining and overtraining regimes across our experiments. This study establishes a first baseline and scaling procedure for the development of future OpenEuroLLM models. We open-source the complete collection of pretraining runs used in this study.

</details>


### [197] [SymboLLM-FE: LLM-Accelerated Symbolic Regression for Automated Feature Engineering on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28408)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Zi-Yi Jia,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SymboLLM-FE，一种结合符号回归与大语言模型的自动特征工程方法：先用符号回归从数据中提取与目标强相关、富有数学表达力的公式以提升模型性能，再由具备先验知识的 LLM 进行精炼以保证可解释性，并以统计先验引导和极少的 LLM 调用次数解决传统 AutoFE 可解释性差、LLM 式 AutoFE 迭代成本高及偏差/幻觉风险的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据常在特征信息量不足时缺乏判别力，影响建模性能。AutoFE 虽能自动生成和选择特征，但传统方法依赖盲目的数学变换导致特征可解释性差；基于 LLM 的 AutoFE 虽能利用先验知识，却需要成本高昂的多轮迭代，且存在偏差与幻觉风险。因此需要一个兼顾性能、可解释性与效率的特征工程方案。

Method: SymboLLM-FE 采用两阶段流水线：(1) 通过符号回归提取与目标变量强相关且具有数学表达力的公式作为候选特征，以增强模型性能；(2) 利用具备丰富先验知识的 LLM 对这些公式进行精炼，并结合基于统计先验的引导机制确保结果的可解释性，整个过程仅需一位数的 LLM 调用次数。

Result: 在六个真实数据集和四个 Kaggle 竞赛上的实验表明，SymboLLM-FE 在性能上优于现有 AutoFE 方法，同时有效应对了可解释性差与迭代次数多这两大挑战。

Conclusion: 符号回归与 LLM 的结合能够互补优势：符号回归提供高性能且可优化的数学特征，LLM 提供语义可解释性；该方案以极低迭代开销实现了性能与可解释性的双赢，为 AutoFE 提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tabular data, as a core data format in machine learning, often lacks the discriminative power needed for high-performance modeling due to insufficient feature informativeness. Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) overcomes this by automating feature generation and selection, ensuring both model performance and operational efficiency. However, traditional AutoFE often yield features with poor interpretability because they rely on blind mathematical transformations, while large language models (LLM)-based AutoFE faces challenges in requiring costly multi-round iterations to generate high-utility features to effectively enhance model performance, compounded by inherent risks of bias and hallucination. In this paper, we combine symbolic regression with LLMs for feature engineering (SymboLLM-FE) to solve these challenges. We extract mathematically expressive formulas strongly correlated with the target via symbolic regression, which can enhance model performance, then refine them by LLMs with rich prior knowledge to ensure interpretability. Empirical results on six real-world datasets and four Kaggle competitions demonstrate that SymboLLM-FE outperforms existing AutoFE. SymboLLM-FE also addresses the dual challenges of poor interpretability and numerous iterations by employing a statistical prior-grounded LLM refinement mechanism and single-digit LLM calls.

</details>


### [198] [Euclidean Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28425)
*Nathanael Bosch,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FNO虽与网格分辨率无关，但其离散谱权重依赖周期域（波矢），换域后隐式表示不同算子。本文提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO），将谱核参数化为物理波矢的连续函数，使其在不同形状和大小的周期域上保持一致行为，并在热方程与材料科学（交换关联势）任务中验证了对未见网格与域的良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO虽然在构造上与训练/评估的网格分辨率无关，但并非独立于其所作用的周期域：其离散谱权重按整数傅里叶模式数索引，对应特定物理波矢。当应用于不同域时，同一组权重作用于不同波矢，FNO会静默地表示一个不同的算子，因而无法胜任需要跨域迁移的任务。

Method: 提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO）：将谱核参数化为物理波矢的连续函数，而非离散的模式索引，从而使算子能够在不同形状和大小的周期域上保持一致的作用。

Result: 在简单热方程以及具有实际意义的学习不同晶体结构间交换关联势的材料科学任务上评估，EFNO能够泛化到未见过的网格尺寸与域。

Conclusion: EFNO作为FNO的域无关替代方案，通过连续化谱核克服了FNO依赖周期域的局限，从而支持跨域迁移任务。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide an efficient framework for learning mappings between function spaces as they are, by construction, independent of the grid resolution at which they are trained and evaluated. However, FNOs are not independent of the periodic domain they are applied to: their discrete spectral weights are indexed by integer Fourier mode numbers, which correspond to physical wavevectors. When applied to a different domain, the same trained weights act at different wavevectors, and the FNO silently represents a different operator. This makes FNOs unsuitable for tasks where transfer across domains is crucial. We propose Euclidean Fourier neural operators~(EFNOs) as a domain-independent alternative to FNOs. By parameterizing the spectral kernel as a continuous function of the physical wavevector, the EFNO can learn operators that act consistently across periodic domains of varying shape and size. We evaluate the EFNO on a simple heat equation and on a practically relevant materials science task of learning exchange-correlation potentials across different crystal structures, and demonstrate that the EFNO is able to generalize to unseen grid sizes and domains.

</details>


### [199] [Curvature-Conditioned Multiscale Momentum with Sphere Constraints for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28442)
*Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Mingze Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种曲率条件化的多尺度动量方法（结合球面约束），仅沿平坦方向（小特征值特征方向）应用该动量以加速LLM预训练，在0.12B–2.3B参数规模的稠密与MoE架构上显著加速Muon优化器，并给出理论验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练在LLM训练总计算成本中占比很大，但噪声主导的梯度和高度病态的损失景观带来严峻挑战。现有自适应优化器（如AdamW、Muon）虽成功，但其依赖梯度归一化，对病态曲率的缓解有限；沿平坦方向（小特征值方向）的进展——主导最终损失下降的部分——仍然较慢，需要增强沿平坦方向的训练动力学。

Method: 提出曲率条件化的多尺度动量方法，并配合球面约束技术。仅在平坦方向应用多尺度动量：慢衰减分量用于降噪，快衰减分量用于快速适应曲率，二者互补。球面约束用于防止朴素组合导致的参数膨胀和有效学习率过快的衰减。理论部分验证了加速效果并阐明平坦方向多尺度动量的设计原理。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种架构（稠密、MoE）和模型规模（0.12B–2.3B参数）上显著加速Muon优化器；理论上也验证了加速效应并提供了设计原理上的洞见。

Conclusion: 作者提出的方法为LLM预训练带来稳定且持续的加速，通过沿平坦方向的曲率条件化多尺度动量与球面约束相结合，优于现有自适应优化器，并具有理论支撑，表明该设计原则是一种有效的大规模预训练加速策略。

Abstract: Pretraining accounts for a large fraction of the total computational cost in LLM training. However, noise-dominant gradients and the highly ill-conditioned loss landscape bring severe challenges. Although modern adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Muon have achieved great success in large-scale pretraining, their reliance on gradient normalization offers limited mitigation of the ill-conditioned curvature. The progress along flat directions (eigen-directions of small eigenvalues), which dominates the final loss reduction, remains relatively slow. To enhance training dynamics along flat directions, we propose a curvature-conditioned multiscale momentum method with sphere constraints, delivering steady acceleration in LLM pretraining. This multiscale momentum, applied only along flat directions, pairs a slow-decay component for noise reduction with a fast-decay component for rapid curvature adaptation, harnessing their complementary strengths. Crucially, we employ a sphere constraint technique to prevent parameter inflation and excessively rapid effective learning rate decay that would otherwise arise from a naive combination. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method significantly accelerates Muon across diverse architectures (dense, MoE) and model sizes (0.12B--2.3B parameters). Theoretically, we verify the acceleration effect and provide insight into the design principles underlying the flat-direction multiscale momentum.

</details>


### [200] [How Proper Scoring Rules Shape LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28482)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim,Ville A. Satopää,Philip E. Tetlock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比五种"适当评分规则"（proper scoring rules）作为训练目标对LLM二分类预测器（binary forecaster）的影响，发现尽管这些评分规则理论上都激励真实的概率报告，但训练出的模型在校准度、概率使用以及偏差/信息/噪声的组成结构上存在显著差异，而在总体准确性与判别力上差异较小。Brier规则训练的模型取得最低Brier分数和最高AUC-ROC，对数规则训练的模型取得最高对数分数和最低校准误差；相似总体性能可由不同的偏差-信息-噪声组合实现，说明奖励选择不仅影响预测水平，还影响预测误差的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设理论上等价的适当评分规则可互换地作为LLM预测器的训练目标，但缺乏实证检验：奖励函数的具体选择究竟如何影响预测模型的性能与行为，尤其是校准、概率使用及误差结构，仍不清楚。

Method: 作者将五种适当的评分规则分别作为二元事件预测任务的强化学习训练目标，在已解决的真实世界事件上训练LLM预测器，并对各模型的校准误差、概率使用模式、偏差/信息/噪声分解以及总体准确性和判别力（Brier分数、对数分数、AUC-ROC）进行系统比较。

Result: 五种评分规则训练出的模型在校准度、概率使用及偏差-信息-噪声组成上存在差异，但总体准确性和判别力差异较小；Brier训练模型获得最低Brier分数和最高AUC-ROC，对数训练模型获得最高对数分数和最低校准误差。总体性能相近的模型可能通过不同的偏倚-信息-噪声组合实现该性能。

Conclusion: 适当评分规则作为训练目标时并非可互换，奖励函数的选择会同时影响LLM预测的水平和误差结构。由于每种条件仅使用单个随机种子，部分差异可能来自训练随机性，因此结论需要谨慎解读并值得进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper evaluates how reward function choice shapes the performance and behavior of LLM forecasters. We compare five proper scoring rules as training objectives for binary forecasts of resolved real-world events. Although the rules share the same theoretical incentive for truthful probability reporting, the resulting models differ in calibration, probability use, and estimated profiles of bias, information, and noise, with smaller differences in aggregate accuracy and discrimination. The Brier-trained model has the lowest observed Brier score and highest AUC-ROC, while the log-trained model has the highest observed log score and lowest calibration error. Models with similar aggregate performance also reach that performance through different combinations of bias, information, and noise. Proper scoring rules therefore need not behave interchangeably as training objectives. Reward choice may shape not only how well an LLM forecasts, but how its forecasting errors are structured. Each condition uses a single seed, so some differences may reflect training stochasticity.

</details>


### [201] [REPLICANT: Learning Policies for Evading and Hardening Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28499)
*Shae McFadden,Ilias Tsingenopoulos,Mario D'Onghia,Alexander Herzog,Myles Foley,Chris Hicks,Lorenzo Cavallaro,Fabio Pierazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Replicant，一个基于深度强化学习的框架，在严格的仅标签黑盒威胁模型下学习现实中的规避任务，可跨样本、检测器和特征空间迁移，并展现出更强的攻击性能与更好的防御加固效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的恶意软件检测在实际环境中的效能评估必须考虑强对抗者的鲁棒性，但最先进的攻击方法往往假设攻击者能访问训练数据、特征空间或置信度分数等特权信息，无法有效建模现实中更具限制性的对抗者。

Method: 提出 Replicant，一种深度强化学习框架，学习在严格的仅标签黑盒威胁模型下的规避任务：学习一个可复用的策略来决定如何修改恶意软件样本以及何时查询目标检测器，该策略可跨样本、检测器及特征空间迁移。

Result: 在七个 Android 恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间上，Replicant 是最强且查询效率最高的方法，平均攻击成功率达 78.8%，相对最先进方法提升 20.9%–39.2%；用于对抗训练时，其产出的检测器具有更可泛化的鲁棒性，同样优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 学习规避任务不仅能带来更强的攻击性能，更重要的是能为加固恶意软件检测器提供更好的信号，从而证明现实的对抗学习对提升检测器鲁棒性的价值。

Abstract: To determine the real-world effectiveness of machine learning based malware detection, it is vital to evaluate its robustness against highly capable adversaries. However, state-of-the-art attacks do not effectively model realistic adversaries, as they often assume access to privileged information such as the training data, feature space, or confidence scores of the target. In this work, we present Replicant, a deep reinforcement learning framework that learns the realistic task of evasion under a strict label-only black-box threat model. Replicant learns a reusable policy on how to modify a malware sample and when to query the target, which transfers across samples, detectors, and feature spaces. Across seven Android malware detectors and three feature spaces, Replicant is the strongest and most query-efficient approach achieving a mean attack success rate of 78.8%, a relative improvement of 20.9%-39.2% over the state-of-the-art. Furthermore, when used for adversarial training, Replicant also outperforms the state-of-the art by producing detectors with more generalizable robustness. With Replicant we demonstrate that learning the task of evasion not only results in stronger attack performance but, crucially, provides a better signal for hardening malware detectors.

</details>


### [202] [An Enclosed Mode Is a Gauge Choice: Topology Relative to Reach in Certified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28541)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"经由采样门验收的代码世界模型"的认证边界：在可达查询集上模型可被精确确定，而在可达域之外其错误可任意严重（此部分为"规范"自由度）。作者用一个最小环状仪器证明极端情形（错误拓扑的实心圆盘伪影无法被任何采样门证伪，且在游戏中位级无害），并借助三个模型族的LLM合成实验，测量单一控制旋钮（宽度为伽马的通道）如何在"不可证伪且无害—可证伪且代价高昂—立即被证伪"三种状态间迁移，最后给出基于持续同调、传感器分辨极限与维度匹配的惩罚/认证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于：被采样门（sampling gate）验收的世界模型可能在门可见的所有内容上精确无误，但在门不可见之处任意错误，因此需要刻画"被认证模型究竟能知道什么、其错误代价有多大"，尤其当遗漏是一个包住不可达内域的环形冻结模态（annular freeze mode）时。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 用"门商"（gate quotient）使问题精确化——"带确定性的验收"在可达查询集上精确决定模型，可达之外属于规范（gauge）自由度；(2) 在最小环状仪器上构造错误拓扑的实心圆盘伪影，并证明其不可被任何采样门证伪、在游戏中位级无害（极端情形）；(3) 用LLM合成（跨三个模型族）测量宽度为伽马的通道这一单一旋钮如何驱动伪影经历三种状态；(4) 提出三原则：危险由拓扑相对可达性决定、修复受参数与传感器限制（持续同调摘要给出错误的beta_1，代表具有几何分辨极限的传感器）、缓解须匹配错误的维度与方向（点围栏失效、维度匹配的持续围栏使利用崩溃、双自由度证书对称地使虚构模态失败坍塌）；(5) 在n维中论证壳层结构使误识别近乎必然而危险仍完全可利用。

Result: 实验结果：(1) 通道若被规划器可用，会击垮盲模型的利用（在伽马约0.1的膝点处，博弈代价从1.09降至约0）；而同样第一贝蒂数的隐藏通道则使其保持满强度（1.12）。(2) 没有模型族能从外部证据恢复该区域；从内部，模型能摆出正确拓扑却无法锁定参数，且摆出的拓扑跟随持续同调摘要错误的beta_1（而非真值）。(3) 点围栏对一维边界无效；维度匹配的持续围栏把利用从0.999压至0.058（两课时瞬态）；双自由度证书把虚构模态失效从1.769压至0.029。(4) n维中壳层使误识别近乎必然，而危险仍完全可利用，两条轴相互独立。

Conclusion: 结论：可达性界定了认证权力的边界——"接受即确定"仅覆盖可达查询集，超出部分天然是规范自由度，可被任意错误且无法被外部观测证伪。安全的认证与缓解必须(a)以可达性相对拓扑判定危险、(b)承认修复受参数与传感器分辨极限约束（正确拓扑≠正确参数）、(c)使缓解手段与错误的维度/方向匹配，否则错误拓扑伪影以"不可证伪且无害"形态潜伏，并可在单个旋钮下转入"可证伪且代价高昂"乃至n维下"误识别近必然且危险完全可利用"的形态。

Abstract: A code world model accepted by a sampling gate can be exactly right on everything the gate can see and arbitrarily wrong beyond it. We characterize what a certified model can know, and what its errors can cost, when the omission is an annular freeze mode enclosing an unreachable interior. The gate quotient makes the question precise: acceptance-with-certainty determines the model exactly on the reachable query set; beyond reach is gauge. On a minimal ring instrument we prove the extreme case (a wrong-topology filled-disc artifact unfalsifiable by any sampling gate and bitwise harmless at play) and measure, with LLM synthesis across three model families, how one knob (a channel of width gamma) walks the same artifact through three regimes: unfalsifiable-and-harmless, falsifiable-and-costly, and instantly falsified. Three principles organize the empirics. First, danger is topology relative to reach: a channel the planner can use collapses the blind model's exploitation (play cost 1.09 to ~0 over a knee at gamma ~ 0.1), while a hidden channel with the same first Betti number keeps it at full strength (1.12). Second, repair is parameter-bound and sensor-bound: no family recovers the region from outside evidence; from inside, models pose the right topology but cannot pin its parameters, and the posed topology tracks the guiding persistent-homology summary's wrong beta_1 (a sensor with a measured geometric resolution limit), not the truth. Third, mitigation must match the error's dimension and direction: point fences fail against the one-dimensional boundary, a dimension-matched persisted fence collapses exploitation to a two-lesson transient (0.999 to 0.058), and the dual freedom certificate collapses the invented-mode failure symmetrically (1.769 to 0.029). In n dimensions the shell makes misidentification near-certain while the danger stays fully exploitable: the two axes are independent.

</details>


### [203] [DARTS: Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28547)
*Aaryan Ajay Sharma,Sai Nishanth Padala,Seganrasan Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DARTS，一种面向解码器LLM的表示偏差修正方法。模型合并虽能在无需训练的情况下组合多个任务专用模型，但合并后的模型存在表示偏差（与各源模型隐藏状态的系统性漂移）。不同于编码器模型，解码器因自回归特性存在两个新挑战：因果注意力掩码导致偏差随token位置累积（需位置相关修正），以及并非所有位置的token同等重要（高熵决策关键位置远重要于低熵位置）。DARTS采用熵加权L1损失来上调高熵位置的修正权重，并引入逐位置加性偏置以无过度参数化地捕获位置相关误差。在Llama-2-7B上的代码生成（HumanEval）、数学推理（GSM8K）和指令跟随（AlpacaEval）三个领域实验中，DARTS相较标准surgery方法取得了显著改进，且仅增加0.1%的参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可将多个任务微调的LLM无训练地组合为多任务模型，但合并模型存在表示偏差（与各源模型隐藏状态间的系统性漂移）。先前工作（Yang et al., 2024a）仅针对基于编码器的视觉模型，用L1损失的轻量修正模块缓解该偏差，而解码器模型由于自回归性质尚未被研究。解码器引入了编码器中不存在的两个挑战：(1) 因果注意力掩码使偏差随token位置累积，需要位置相关修正；(2) 并非所有token位置同等重要，高熵（决策关键）位置远比低熵位置更影响生成质量。

Method: 提出解码器感知的表示调整外科手术方法DARTS（Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery）。其核心包括：(1) 新颖的熵加权L1损失，用于上调高熵位置（错误最影响生成质量处）的修正权重；(2) 逐位置加性偏置，以不过度参数化的方式捕获位置相关的累积误差。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B模型上对三个领域进行广泛评估：代码生成（HumanEval）、数学推理（GSM8K）和指令跟随（AlpacaEval）。结果表明DARTS相较标准surgery方法取得显著改进，且仅增加极小参数（约占模型总参数的0.1%）。

Conclusion: DARTS有效解决了解码器模型中表示偏差的位置累积与位置重要性不均问题，仅以0.1%的参数开销即在三个领域显著超越标准surgery方法，表明熵加权与逐位置修正机制对解码器合并模型的有效性。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific fine-tuned LLMs into a single multi-task model without additional training. However, merged models are known to suffer from representation bias: systematic drift between the merged model's hidden states and those of each individual source model. Prior work (Yang et al., 2024a) study and mitigate this bias for encoder-based vision models using a lightweight correction module trained with L1 loss. However, such bias is not studied for decoder models due to their autoregressive nature. We analyze the problem of representation bias in decoder models, and show two challenges absent in encoders: (1) the causal attention mask causes bias to accumulate across token positions, requiring position-dependent correction; and (2) not all token positions are equally important, i.e., high-entropy (decision-critical) positions matter far more than low-entropy ones. To address these challenges, we propose Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery (DARTS). DARTS employs a novel entropy-weighted L1 loss to upweight correction at high-entropy positions where errors most affect generation quality, and a per-position additive bias that captures position-dependent error without overparameterization. We perform extensive evaluation on three domains: code generation (HumanEval), mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), and instruction following (AlpacaEval) on Llama-2-7B models, and show DARTS achieves significant improvement over the standard surgery approach while adding negligible parameters ($0.1\%$ of total parameters).

</details>


### [204] [Advancing Interaction-Sensitive Feature Selection: Novel Relief-Based Algorithms, Expanded Comparisons, and Recommendations for Biomedical Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28552)
*Kia Kazemi-Nia,Harsh Bandhey,Philip J. Freda,Ryan J. Urbanowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究重构并优化了 scikit-rebate 特征选择软件包，纳入现有与新提出的 Relief 类算法（RBA）变体，并通过多样化的基因组模拟基准比较各算法的特征排序能力与运行时间；结果显示多数 RBA 能有效检测噪声数据中的两两交互，MultiSWRFDB 综合表现最优，重构后运行时间显著缩短（10-35 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维生物医学数据建模前，可靠的特征选择能够降低计算开销、提升建模性能并得到更简单可解释的模型。过滤式特征选择方法大多难以检测特征交互，而包装式或嵌入式的计算方法开销大。Relief 类算法（RBA）作为过滤方法，既能对特征交互敏感，又规避了上述限制，因此值得扩展与系统评估。

Method: (1) 重构、优化并扩展 scikit-rebate Python 包，纳入已有及新提出的 RBA 变体，包括 SWRF*、mu-Relief，以及 5 种采用替代邻居选择与特征评分策略的新 RBA 变体；(2) 在样本量、特征数、遗传力与潜在关联类型（主效应与交互）各异的模拟基因组数据集上，对所有 RBA 进行预测性特征排序与运行时间的严格基准比较。

Result: 除 mu-Relief 外，所有 RBA 在噪声数据中均能有效检测两两交互；采用“far”评分的 RBA 对两两交互检测最佳（MultiSWRFDB* 最优），但对主效应敏感性较低。SWRF、MultiSWRF、MultiSURF 与 MultiSWRFDB 在主效应及两两交互数据集上表现领先，且 MultiSWRFDB 在考虑三阶交互时表现最佳。scikit-rebate 重构使 RBA 运行时间缩短 10 至 35 倍。

Conclusion: 新引入的 RBA 变体位居最强性能之列，由于能稳健地同时保留主效应与两两上位性交互信号，这些算法可为下游建模维持预测信号，从而在特征交互敏感性与计算效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: As a precursor to high-dimensional biomedical data modeling, reliable feature selection can reduce computational expense, improve modeling performance, and yield simpler, more interpretable models. However, most filter-based feature selection methods struggle to detect feature interactions, while wrapper or embedded feature selection methods are computationally expensive. Relief-based algorithms (RBAs) are filter methods that are sensitive to feature interactions while mitigating these other limitations. This study (1) refactors, optimizes, and expands the scikit-rebate Python package with existing and newly proposed RBA variants and (2) conducts rigorous RBA benchmark comparisons across diverse genomic simulations. We expand scikit-rebate to include SWRF*, mu-Relief, and 5 novel RBA variants implementing alternative strategies for neighbor selection and feature scoring. All RBAs were evaluated to compare predictive feature ranking and runtime across simulated genomic datasets varying in sample size, number of features, heritability, and underlying association type (e.g. main effects and interactions). All RBAs, except mu-Relief, were proficient in detecting 2-way interactions in noisy data. RBAs utilizing 'far' scoring were best at detecting 2-way interactions - with MultiSWRFDB* top-performing - but were far less sensitive to main effects. SWRF, MultiSWRF, MultiSURF, and MultiSWRFDB yielded top performance across main effect and 2-way interaction datasets with MultiSWRFDB performing best when also considering 3-way interactions. Refactoring of scikit-rebate resulted in 10 to 35-fold reductions in RBA runtimes. The newly introduced RBAs were among the strongest performing, and by robustly retaining both main effects and 2-way epistatic interactions, these algorithms preserve predictive signals for downstream modeling.

</details>


### [205] [Blog: Survey of Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28557)
*Ruoran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于2025-2026年神经网络优化方法的综述，提出将优化器与训练优化方法沿四个相互独立的轴（时间估计、更新几何、时间跨度管理、表示与系统）进行组织，并通过实证结论指出矩阵感知方法虽有真实进展，但不存在可脱离上下文替代AdamW的优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络优化领域已不再能用“一系列新的Adam变体”来简单描述：设计空间从坐标扩展到矩阵与层、从固定训练时域扩展到随时间变化的策略、从纯数学更新规则扩展到必须在分片（sharding）和低精度计算下存活的参数状态表示。因此需要对这一扩展后的设计空间进行系统梳理与组织。

Method: 采用综述方法，沿四个大致独立的轴组织近期优化器与训练优化方法：时间估计、更新几何、时间跨度管理、表示与系统；并连接了Muon的谱归一化、Shampoo与SOAP的历史矩阵统计、自适应与混合矩阵方法、内存高效优化器、免调度（schedule-free）训练、小批量修正以及量化优化器状态等具体技术。

Result: 核心实证结论刻意非颂扬式：矩阵感知方法代表了真实的进展，但不存在独立于上下文的AdamW替代品。各优化器的排名会随模型规模、数据与参数之比、批大小、学习率调度、参数划分、调参预算以及目标指标（tokens、FLOPs、墙钟时间或内存）而变化。

Conclusion: 实际后果是应采用组合式的优化器设计观，并遵循更严格的优化器宣称评估协议，即在特定上下文条件下进行验证，而非寻求普适的最优优化器。

Abstract: Neural-network optimization in 2025-2026 is no longer well described as a succession of new Adam variants. The design space has expanded from coordinates to matrices and layers, from fixed training horizons to policies over time, and from mathematical update rules to state representations that must survive sharding and low-precision computation. This survey organizes recent optimizers and training optimization methods along four largely independent axes: temporal estimation, update geometry, horizon management, and representation and systems. It connects the spectral normalization of Muon, the historical matrix statistics of Shampoo and SOAP, adaptive and hybrid matrix methods, memory-efficient optimizers, schedule-free training, small-batch corrections, and quantized optimizer states. The central empirical conclusion is deliberately non-triumphal: matrix-aware methods represent a genuine advance, but there is no context-independent replacement for AdamW. Rankings change with model scale, data-to-parameter ratio, batch size, schedule, parameter partition, tuning budget, and whether the target metric is tokens, FLOPs, wall-clock time, or memory. The practical consequence is a compositional view of optimizer design and a stricter protocol for evaluating optimizer claims.

</details>


### [206] [QGPINNs: A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Nonlocal Differential Equations on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28589)
*Vaibhav Mehandiratta,Saket Ramchandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QGPINNs，一个基于 PyTorch 的物理信息神经网络框架，用于在量子图上的非局部微分方程（多阶分数阶椭圆问题和时间分数阶演化方程）的数值求解；它通过每条边上的神经网络近似解、统一的基于图的损失函数以及多种图自适应学习策略来耦合边上的解为全局解，并可扩展到逆问题。实验在基准图结构、IEEE 14 节点系统和农业排水网络中验证了其精度、效率与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子图上的非局部（分数阶）微分方程缺乏通用、可扩展的数值计算框架，且需要兼顾各边解之间的耦合以及顶点传输条件、边界条件和方程本身，同时还要处理弱奇异性解，因此需要一个能将图结构与物理信息神经网络有机结合并统一处理正、逆问题的实现方案。

Method: 提出 QGPINNs 框架：在图的每条边上用神经网络近似解，并设计统一的基于图的损失函数，把控制方程、初始条件、Dirichlet 边界条件以及连续性、Kirchhoff-Neumann 顶点条件同时纳入学习过程，将局部边上的解耦合成图的全局解。面向两类代表性非线性模型（多阶分数阶椭圆问题、时间分数阶演化方程），集成了软/硬约束施加、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入和用于弱奇异解的可学习奇异性捕捉特征等图自适应策略，并自然扩展到通过含噪观测数据识别分数阶算子阶数及物理参数的逆问题。

Result: 通过在基准图结构以及真实网络（IEEE 14 节点电力系统、明渠农业排水网络）上的数值实验，验证了 QGPINNs 在精度、计算效率和物理一致性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: QGPINNs 是一个通用、准确且高效的量子图非局部微分方程求解框架，能够同时处理正问题与参数/阶数识别等逆问题，为图结构上分数阶与非线性偏微分方程的物理信息求解提供了统一方案。

Abstract: We propose QGPINNs, a physics-informed neural network framework developed in PyTorch for the numerical solution of nonlocal differential equations on quantum graphs. The framework is designed as a general computational implementation in which the solution on each edge of the graph is approximated by a neural network, while a unified graph-based loss function enforces the governing equations together with initial, boundary, and vertex transmission conditions. In particular, the formulation incorporates standard continuity and Kirchhoff-Neumann vertex conditions and Dirichlet boundary conditions into the learning process to couple the local edge-wise neural approximations into a global solution on the graph. The framework is developed for two representative classes of nonlinear models: multi-order fractional elliptic problems and time-fractional evolution equations on quantum graphs. To improve accuracy and training stability, QGPINNs integrates several graph-adapted learning strategies, including soft and hard constraint enforcement, dynamic loss balancing, Fourier feature embeddings, and a learnable singularity-capturing feature for weakly singular solutions arising in the considered problems. The framework also extends naturally to inverse problems, including the identification of the orders of fractional operators and physical parameters from noisy observational data. We validate the accuracy, computational efficiency, and physical consistency of the proposed framework through numerical experiments on benchmark graph structures and real-world networks, including the IEEE 14-bus system and an open-channel agricultural drainage network.

</details>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Visual Cue Interactions in AR-Guided Needle Insertion: A Prostate Biopsy-Inspired Phantom Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27620)
*Xinrui Zou,Mingxu Liu,Thomas T. Jones,Braden Millan,Sandeep Gurram,Peter A. Pinto,Raisa Z. Freidlin,Alejandro Martin-Gomez*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文针对经皮穿刺手术中医生依赖超声图像导致认知负荷高、进针角度控制困难的问题，研究增强现实（AR）辅助进针对齐的可视化技术，提出了三种可视化方案（局部聚焦-上下文窗口、基于颜色的接近度编码、显式轨迹叠加），并通过包含7名临床专家在内的26名受试者、基于前列腺活检仿真的用户研究进行评价。结果表明提示效果取决于周围提示配置与用户经验：显式轨迹叠加能提升新手的目标精度并减少退针行为，但单独呈现时耗时更长；聚焦-上下文窗口和颜色接近度编码对专家分别缩短了完成时间、减少了退针事件。研究建议AR引导策略应考虑用户专业水平与视觉上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经皮针穿刺操作看似简单，但临床医生无法直接看到内部解剖结构，只能依赖超声图像，这会显著增加认知负荷，导致工具方向操控困难。AR有望通过在医生视野中提供相关视觉信息来辅助任务，但若仅仅提供视觉信息而忽略有意义的视觉线索，反而会干扰深度感知与空间理解。因此需要设计并评估能够支持进针对齐的有效AR可视化技术。

Method: 与医学专家进行设计研讨，提出三种进针对齐可视化技术：局部聚焦-上下文窗口（localized focus-and-context window）、基于颜色的接近度编码（color-based proximity encoding）和显式轨迹叠加（explicit trajectory overlay）。随后在26名受试者（含7名临床专家）中，使用前列腺活检启发式仿真模型（prostate-biopsy-inspired phantom）开展用户研究，对三种技术在不同视觉提示配置下的效果进行对比评价。

Result: 提示效果依赖周围的提示配置与用户专业水平。对新手而言，显式轨迹叠加提高了目标定位精度并减少退针行为，但其完成时间效果因配置而异——当叠加单独呈现时表现较慢。对专家而言，聚焦-上下文窗口缩短了完成时间并减少退针事件，颜色接近度叠加则缩短了完成时间。主观评价方面，颜色提示常被认为有帮助，但对其精度的实际效果并不一致。

Conclusion: AR引导策略对经皮穿刺介入的效果会受到用户专业水平和视觉上下文配置的显著影响，因此设计时应针对不同用户经验（新手与专家）及不同提示呈现方式加以差异化考虑，不应采用一刀切的通用方案。

Abstract: Despite the apparent simplicity of the motor action involved during percutaneous needle procedures, manipulating the tool's direction becomes challenging when clinicians cannot directly visualize internal anatomy and must rely on ultrasound images, which increase cognitive demand. Augmented reality (AR) offers the promise to assist with these tasks by providing pertinent visual information in the clinician's field of view. However, simply providing visual information that ignores meaningful visual cues can complicate depth perception and spatial understanding. In this work, we introduce and evaluate three visualization techniques for needle alignment developed during design sessions with medical experts: a localized focus-and-context window, a color-based proximity encoding, and an explicit trajectory overlay. These techniques were evaluated in a user study (n=26) including clinical experts (n=7) using a prostate-biopsy-inspired phantom. Results from this study suggest that cue effects depended on the surrounding cue configuration and user expertise. For novices, explicit trajectory overlay improved targeting accuracy and reduced retreat behavior, but its effect on completion time varied across cue configurations, with slower performance when the overlay was presented alone. For experts, the focus-and-context window reduced completion time and retreat events, while color-based proximity overlay improved completion time. Subjectively, color cues were often perceived as helpful even when their effects on accuracy were not consistent. These results suggest that AR guidance strategies for percutaneous interventions should consider user expertise and visual context.

</details>


### [208] [The Role of Mixed and Augmented Reality in Medical Visualization: Literature Review and A Context-Aware Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27644)
*Xinrui Zou,Mingxu Liu,Hongchao Shu,Ruixing Liang,Mathias Unberath,Alejandro Martin-Gomez*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结构化的医学增强/混合现实（AR/MR）可视化策略分类体系，该分类法基于图像模态、数据维度、显示技术和临床应用四个核心设计组件，并引入可视化锚定与感知觉意识两个常被忽视的关键维度，为不同临床场景下选择适当的可视化技术提供了综合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像依赖复杂的物理过程和对比机制形成图像，单纯成像不足以让人类充分利用信息；传统方法又用二维显示呈现三维解剖结构，存在空间理解局限。AR/MR技术为医学影像可视化提供了更直观的空间交互范式，但若缺乏精心设计，可能产生感知不一致，损害实用性和有效性。因此需要更清晰的理解可视化设计如何在不同临床用例中变化。

Method: 作者构建了一套结构化的医学AR/MR可视化策略分类法（taxonomy），按四个核心设计组件组织技术：图像模态、数据维度、显示技术和临床应用；同时引入两个文献中常被忽视的关键维度——可视化锚定（虚拟内容与物理世界之间的空间关系）和感知觉意识（利用视觉线索支持空间解释），共同构成全面的分类框架。

Result: 该分类法将各种AR/MR可视化技术系统化组织起来，涵盖图像模态、数据维度、显示技术、临床应用以及可视化锚定和感知觉意识两个补充维度，形成一个详细的框架，用于指导医学应用中选择合适的可视化技术。

Conclusion: 所提出的综合分类框架为医学应用中选择合适的AR/MR可视化技术提供了详细而系统的指导，有助于提升医学影像信息利用的直观性和有效性。

Abstract: The discovery and evolution of medical imaging technologies have enabled non-invasive visualization of internal anatomy that has be- come essential for supporting diagnosis, monitoring, and treatment. However, because medical imaging relies on complex physical processes and contrast mechanisms for image formation, imaging alone is not sufficient to enable humans to leverage the resulting in- formation fully. In addition, traditional methods to visualize the resulting information use two-dimensional displays to present three-dimensional anatomical structures. The introduction of Augmented and Mixed Reality (AR/MR) technologies offers an opportunity to provide valuable paradigms for medical imaging visualization, allowing users to observe, explore, and interact with anatomical infor- mation in more spatially intuitive ways. However, naive implementation without careful design considerations can lead to perceptual inconsistencies, potentially compromising utility and effectiveness. In this paper, we present a structured taxonomy of medical AR/MR visualization strategies aimed at providing clearer insight into how visualization design varies across clinical use cases. The taxonomy organizes techniques based on four core design components: image modality, data dimensionality, display technology, and clinical application. In addition, we introduce two critical dimensions that are often overlooked in the literature: visualization anchoring (the spatial relationship between virtual content and the physical world), and perceptual awareness (the use of visual cues to support spatial interpretation). Together, these components form a comprehensive taxonomy, offering a detailed framework for selecting appropriate visualization techniques in medical applications.

</details>


### [209] [What Will This Copper Look Like Later? Forecasting Surface Appearance and Rendering It as a PBR Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28102)
*Teejuta Sriwaranon,Borworntat Dendumrongkul,Tanapat Chamted,Pizzanu Kanongchaiyos*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对铜表面氧化的外观预测管线：给定固定相机观测，预测10个加速老化单位后的外观，并转换为渲染器所需的反照率、法线、粗糙度和金属度贴图。在严格的留出样本协议下（用一件样本训练、在另一件未见样本上测试），所有有参学习的时空预测器均不如“复制最后一帧”，唯一能跨样本迁移的是无参的闭式全局颜色外推，其在水平10时相对复制末帧提升16.7%和55.5%。受控实验（光度漂移校正、滑动块自举）验证了这一结论，并揭示学习到的“易腐蚀图”仅对训练样本有效、误导新样本，而全局颜色轨迹才是样本间共享的规律。最终管线对未见样本使用闭式预测器，仅对已观测样本沿用学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字设计需要预先知道金属表面在氧化过程中将如何呈现，但目前缺少能够跨样本泛化的铜表面外观预测方法；作者希望构建一条从观测到渲染器所需材质贴图的完整预测管线，并用严格的未见样本协议检验其泛化能力。

Method: 在固定相机观测下预测10个加速单位后的外观并转成反照率、法线、粗糙度、金属度贴图；比较了带单调氧化态约束的时空学习模型、三种其他训练架构、复制末帧基线以及无参的闭式全局颜色外推；采用留出整件样本的协议（一件训练用于选择检查点、另一件在不同日期与条件下录制作测试），并通过非氧化参考区域的光度漂移校正和滑动块自举进行双重控制验证。

Result: 单一记录内最准的学习模型及其余三种训练架构在未见样本上都不如复制末帧；唯一可迁移的是闭式全局颜色外推，比复制末帧提升13.4%和50.6%，且随水平增大至t+10的+16.7%与+55.5%。漂移校正后增益不变（排除曝光失控），滑动块自举使较大增益显著、较小增益未达单独显著。机制上，学习的易腐蚀图在训练样本上编码腐蚀起始位置但在新样本上产生误导，而全局颜色轨迹才是样本共享的规律。

Conclusion: 有参空格学习模型不跨样本泛化，闭式全局颜色外推是唯一可靠的选择；因此面向未见样本（如数字设计中的新物体）应部署闭式预测器，学习模型则仅用于继续预测已观测过的样本。作者并公开了代码、数据划分、协议与泄漏审计。

Abstract: Digital design requires predicting how a metal surface will look later in its oxidation; this paper presents such a pipeline for copper. Given a fixed-camera observation, the system forecasts appearance 10 accelerated units ahead and converts it into the albedo, normal, roughness and metallic maps a renderer consumes. Forecasting is evaluated as an authoring tool would use it, on a copper specimen the system has not observed: an entire recording is held out, so training and checkpoint selection use one specimen and the test set is the whole of a second, recorded on a different day and condition. Under this protocol a learned spatio-temporal model with a monotone oxidation state, the most accurate forecaster within a single recording, is less accurate than copying the last observed frame on an unseen specimen, in both directions, as are three further trained architectures. The only forecaster that transfers is a closed-form global color extrapolation with no trained parameters, improving on copy-last-frame by 13.4% and 50.6%, with a margin that increases with horizon to +16.7% and +55.5% at t+10. Two controls qualify this: correcting every frame for the photometric drift measured on a non-oxidizing reference region leaves both margins intact, ruling out uncontrolled exposure as their source, and a moving-block bootstrap over the 6 independent windows each recording contains separates the larger margin from zero but leaves the smaller one not individually significant. The mechanism is measured: a learned susceptibility map encodes where corrosion begins on the training specimen and misleads on a new one, whereas the global color trajectory is what specimens share. The pipeline therefore deploys the closed-form forecaster for unseen specimens and the learned model only for continuing one already observed. Code, splits, protocol and leakage audit are released.

</details>


### [210] [Transparency Rendering in Computer-Aided Design: Methodologies, Trade-offs, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28310)
*Grigoris Tsopouridis,Ioannis Fudos*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了CAD环境中透明度渲染的现状，涵盖工业系统中的实际部署与底层算法，从应用场景、近似与精确技术权衡、基准与评测方法、内部零件强调技术以及透明CSG渲染挑战等多个维度进行了梳理，并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明度渲染在CAD中对于设计检查、沟通和决策至关重要，但现有文献缺乏对CAD领域透明度渲染技术的系统综述；同时，复杂装配体和CSG对象对透明渲染的准确性与性能提出了特殊要求，需要对该领域的技术现状、开放问题与评测方法进行系统化梳理。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法：1) 回顾CAD环境中透明度渲染的现有方法与典型应用场景；2) 比较近似与精确透明度技术在桌面和移动平台上的性能与质量权衡，并讨论方法选择标准；3) 分析CAD专用可视化任务所需的基准、质量指标与评测方法论；4) 考察重要性驱动透明度、基于轮廓的方法等用于突出内部关键零件的技术；5) 探讨透明CSG对象渲染中的鲁棒性、正确性及与现代渲染管线集成等独特挑战。

Result: 该综述刻画了CAD透明度渲染的当前能力，系统归纳了现有的开放问题（如评测基准缺失、CSG透明渲染的鲁棒性与正确性、管线集成等），并梳理了近似与精确方法在桌面/移动平台上的性能-质量权衡规律，为方法选型提供了参考依据。

Conclusion: 作者认为，CAD透明度渲染在技术选择上存在近似与精确方法的权衡，当前亟需面向CAD特定可视化任务的基准、质量指标与评测方法；未来研究方向应包括改进复杂装配体的空间理解技术（如重要性驱动与轮廓方法）以及解决透明CSG渲染的鲁棒性、正确性和现代渲染管线集成问题。

Abstract: This paper surveys the state of transparency rendering in Computer-Aided Design (CAD), with a focus on both practical deployment in industrial systems and the underlying algorithms. We first review current approaches to transparency rendering in CAD environments and outline application scenarios in which accurate and performant transparency is critical for design inspection, communication, and decision-making. We then analyze the trade-offs between approximate and exact transparency techniques, comparing their performance-quality balance on desktop and mobile platforms and discussing criteria for selecting appropriate methods. The survey further identifies the need for robust benchmarks, quality metrics, and evaluation methodologies tailored to CAD-specific visualization tasks. We examine techniques for emphasizing important interior components such as importance-driven transparency, silhouette-based methods, and related approaches to support effective spatial understanding in complex assemblies. Finally, we discuss the unique challenges of rendering transparent constructive solid geometry (CSG) objects, including robustness, correctness, and integration with modern rendering pipelines. Collectively, these contributions characterize current capabilities, systematize open problems, and outline future research directions for transparency rendering in CAD visualization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Time Capsule of Testable Human Knowledge: 41 Years of Jeopardy! in a Single Free Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27459)
*David Noever,Forrest McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了2011年IBM Watson与当代开源大语言模型在完整Jeopardy!题库上的表现：一个9GB的开源模型（Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit）在529,939条线索上以严格强制应答协议取得67.0%的正确率（事实类类别超85%），而Watson受限于构建时固化的精选语料库，无法回答任何训练截止日期之后的线索；相比之下，本地模型对截止后线索仍保持65%正确率，Claude Opus 4.8达95%。该能力谱系已将“文化知识快照”从服务器机房缩小为可封装进时间胶囊的文件，且不再冻结于自身时代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望说明：曾经像“密封胶囊”一样的IBM Watson所代表的时代性可查询知识快照，如今已变得便携且几乎零成本；同时检验现代模型在全量Jeopardy!语料上的真实知识覆盖能力，并以“训练数据暴露”这一双方共有的属性重新审视Watson与当代LLM的差异。

Method: 使用单个9 GB开源权重模型（Qwen2.5-14B，4-bit量化），在1984至2025年41个播出季的完整开源Jeopardy!线索数据集（529,939条）上进行评估，采用严格强制应答协议，结合精确匹配与模糊匹配计分；并针对训练截止日期之后出现的线索进行测试，与Claude Opus 4.8及Watson进行对比。

Result: 模型在全部线索上回答正确率为67.0%，在事实类类别上超过85%；对训练截止后的线索本地模型保持65%，Claude Opus 4.8达到95%，而Watson按构造得分为零。据作者所知，这是首次有模型在全量语料上运行。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：Watson的情况反而是更极端的——其语料被构建为包含Jeopardy答案并针对过往线索调优，且无法回答其精选分布之外的任何内容；而现代模型虽然在训练数据暴露上与Watson无异，但该能力在规模化后并未冻结于自身时刻，成功从服务器机房迁移到便携文件，具有可封装进时间胶囊的知识快照属性。

Abstract: In 2011, IBM's Watson was something like a sealed capsule of its era's queryable knowledge. Its DeepQA system defeated the strongest human Jeopardy! champions, but the knowledge that let it do so lived in a curated billion-document corpus running on a cluster of POWER7 servers, frozen at build time and impossible to move or copy. We show that the same kind of artifact, a snapshot of what a culture can answer, is now portable and essentially free. We evaluate a single 9 GB open-weight model (Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit) against the complete open Jeopardy! clue dataset, 529,939 clues across all 41 broadcast seasons from 1984 to 2025. To our knowledge this is the first time a model has been run over the full corpus. The 41 years mark only how long the questions were collected. What they test is far older and broader: the accumulated body of human general knowledge a culture considers worth knowing, from ancient history and dead languages to science, literature, and geography, with a verified answer for every item. The model answers 67.0% of all clues under a strict forced-response protocol with exact and fuzzy matching, and exceeds 85% on factoid categories. We treat training-data exposure as something both systems share rather than a flaw unique to language models. Watson's case is in fact the more extreme one. Its corpus was assembled to contain Jeopardy answers and it was tuned on past clues, and it could not answer anything outside that curated distribution. The decisive test is whether a model can answer clues that did not exist when it was built. On clues aired after its training cutoff, the local model holds 65% and Claude Opus 4.8 holds 95%, while Watson by construction scores zero. The capability survives the move from a server room to a file you could seal in a time capsule, and unlike Watson it is not frozen to its own moment.

</details>


### [212] [Rating the Raters: Rasch Measurement Theory for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27463)
*Pratik S. Sachdeva,Nathan Boudol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证将Rasch测量理论（RMT）引入大语言模型（LLM）评估体系。通过将LLM作为评阅者（rater）对仇恨言论语料进行标注的案例研究，使用多层面Rasch模型分析9个LLM的标注数据，发现LLM与人类评分者在严重度、条目校准、问题顺序鲁棒性、目标身份敏感性和评分量表使用等方面存在系统性差异，而这些差异在标准评估实践中会被掩盖。作者主张RMT应纳入LLM评估（作为被试、裁判、评阅者）的工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM如今在评估中扮演多重角色（被测试的被试、评判其他模型输出的裁判、对人类内容打分的评阅者），每种范式都可视为测量问题。但标准评估实践往往忽视仪器（如基准）、被试与评阅者等核心组成部分对最终结果的各自贡献，限制了我们对"究竟在测量什么"的理解。Rasch测量理论天然适合此类问题，能将序数评分分解到共同量表上的可分离层面，并提供识别校准不良与评阅者偏倚的诊断工具。

Method: 以"LLM作为评阅者"范式为例进行案例研究，数据来自Measuring Hate Speech语料库（该语料的构念本身即基于RMT构建）。作者对来自不同家族和能力水平的9个LLM的标注数据拟合了一系列多层面Rasch模型（many-facet Rasch models），以分离并诊断评阅者严重度、条目校准、问题顺序鲁棒性、目标身份敏感性和评分量表使用等方面的特征。

Result: 分析表明，LLM与人类评阅者在多个维度上存在系统性差异：评分严重度（severity）、条目层面的校准、对问题顺序的鲁棒性、对目标身份（target identity）的敏感性以及评分量表的使用方式。这些差异在标准评估实践下均会被掩盖而不可见。

Conclusion: 作者主张Rasch测量理论应当被纳入LLM评估（LLM-as-examinee、-judge、-rater三种范式）的标准工具箱，以更好地揭示测量中各组成部分的贡献、识别评阅者偏倚，从而提升评估的有效性与可解释性。

Abstract: LLMs now sit on every side of evaluation: as examinees scored on benchmarks, judges of other models' outputs, and raters of human-generated content. Each paradigm can be viewed as a measurement problem, where a latent property of an object is probed with items from an instrument (e.g., benchmark) by raters. Standard evaluation practices often neglect the contributions of each core component to the end result, limiting our understanding of what is being measured. Rasch measurement theory (RMT) is well-suited to this kind of problem. RMT decomposes ordinal ratings into separable facets on a common scale. It further provides a battery of diagnostics that can identify miscalibrated measurements and rater biases. We present a case study of RMT applied to the LLM-as-rater paradigm using the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, whose construct was itself built under RMT. We fit a series of many-facet Rasch models to annotations from nine LLMs spanning families and capability levels. Our analyses show that LLMs systematically differ from human raters in severity, item-level calibration, question-order robustness, target-identity sensitivity, and rating scale use, which all would be obscured by standard evaluation practice. Overall, we argue that RMT belongs in the toolkit for evaluating LLM-as-examinee, -judge, and -rater paradigms.

</details>


### [213] [Not All Explanations Are Sought: Information-Seeking Psychology for Human-Centered XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27464)
*Andrea Beretta,Salvatore Rinzivillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将信息寻求心理学引入以人为中心的可解释人工智能（HCXAI）。基于Sharot与Sunstein的信息寻求动机框架，提出人们根据工具性、享乐性和认知性三类预期效用来决定是否参与解释，且这些效用评估受多种认知偏差影响，可能导致过度或不足的信息寻求两种失败模式，在智能体AI系统中尤为严峻。文章呼吁从"让解释可用"转向"让解释被寻求"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCXAI往往假设只要解释是可用的（available），用户就会主动寻求并有效利用，却忽视了一个关键问题：用户实际上"何时、为何"想要获取解释。理解这一点需要借鉴信息寻求心理学，因为用户是否接触解释取决于其对解释的预期效用评估，而这些评估会受到系统性认知偏差的扭曲。

Method: 采用理论综合与概念论证的方法：以Sharot和Sunstein的信息寻求动机框架为核心，将其三类预期效用（工具性、享乐性、认知性）映射到用户对解释的参与决策上；结合多种认知偏差（控制错觉、自动化偏差、不切实际的乐观、影响偏差、过度自信、确认偏差）分析效用估计的偏差来源；并据此归纳两种失败模式，且将其应用于智能体AI系统的特殊挑战情境。

Result: 识别出两种由认知偏差导致的失败模式：过度信息寻求（碎片化注意力、未必提升决策）与不足信息寻求（遗漏关键风险和误解）。同时指出该问题在智能体AI系统中尤为尖锐——解释不仅要支撑对单一输出的理解，还需支持对级联行为的前瞻、风险评估以及干预时机的判断。

Conclusion: 应将信息寻求心理学整合进HCXAI设计，实现范式转变：从让解释"可获得"（available）转向让解释"被寻求"（sought），即设计能考虑用户实际求知欲望及其时机（when and why）的系统，以此提升人机交互中解释的有效性与决策质量。

Abstract: This position paper argues that human-centered explainable AI (HCXAI) should incorporate insights from the psychology of information seeking. Drawing on Sharot and Sunstein's framework of information-seeking motives, we propose that people evaluate whether to engage with explanations based on three types of expected utility: instrumental (will it help me act better?), hedonic (will it make me feel better?), and cognitive (will it improve my understanding?). Each utility is estimated through a lens shaped by well-documented cognitive biases, including illusion of control, automation bias, unrealistic optimism, impact bias, overconfidence, and confirmation bias. These biases can lead to two failure modes: excessive information-seeking that fragments attention without improving decisions, and insufficient information-seeking that leaves critical risks and misunderstandings unexamined. This challenge is particularly acute for agentic AI systems, where explanations must support not just understanding a single output but anticipating cascading actions, assessing risks, and deciding when to intervene. By integrating information-seeking psychology into HCXAI, we advocate for a shift from making explanations available to making them sought: designing systems that account for when and why users actually want to know.

</details>


### [214] [Retrieving Relations, Detecting Fallacies: A RAG Approach to Political Debate Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27471)
*Deborah Dore,Greta Damo,Elena Cabrio,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受论证关系引导的检索增强方法（guided retrieval-augmented methodology），利用论证语篇中支持与攻击关系动态引导相关文档的检索，以补充谬误检测与分类所需的背景知识与语境信息。在ElecDeb60to20基准上的42种检索配置和14个模型实验中，检测与分类的macro-F1分别最高达到0.864和0.725，显著优于非检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谬误检测不能仅依赖表面文本，而需要结合论述主题的世界知识与语篇中论证间的关系等语境信息。已有工作表明论证语篇结构有助于提升分类性能，但通常只是将其编码为静态分类器特征，灵活性有限，难以动态利用外部知识。

Method: 提出一种由论证关系（支持与攻击）引导的检索增强方法：在检索过程中利用论证语篇结构动态引导相关文档的抽取，而非将其作为静态特征；构建15GB政治相关文档知识库，并在ElecDeb60to20基准上，对42种检索配置和14个模型进行系统评估。

Result: 该方法在谬误检测任务上macro-F1最高达0.864，在分类任务上最高达0.725，均优于非检索基线；结果表明在检索过程受论证关系引导时，外部知识的引入能显著提升谬误检测与分类性能。

Conclusion: 将论证语篇结构融入检索过程（而非静态特征）是提升谬误分析的有效途径；外部知识结合论证引导的检索可带来显著的性能增益，为高敏感场景（如政治辩论）中的自动谬误检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fallacies are arguments that employ invalid reasoning, making their automatic detection critical in sensitive contexts such as high-stakes political debates, where public opinion is shaped. Spotting a fallacious argument requires contextual knowledge beyond its pure surface text. This entails world knowledge pertaining to the subject matter under discussion, as well as knowledge of the relationships that exist between arguments within the argumentative discourse. Prior work on fallacy analysis has shown that argumentative discourse structure can beneficially improve classification performance. However, such structure is typically encoded only as static classifier features, limiting its flexibility. Building on this intuition while addressing this limitation, we introduce a guided retrieval-augmented methodology for fallacy detection and classification that leverages argumentative relations of support and attack to dynamically steer the extraction of relevant documents. We evaluate our approach on the ElecDeb60to20 benchmark across 42 retrieval configurations and 14 models, performing retrieval over a 15GB knowledge base of collected political-related documents. Our approach improves macro-F1 up to 0.864 for fallacy detection and up to 0.725 for classification over non-retrieval baselines. These results show that incorporating external knowledge significantly enhances fallacy detection and classification when retrieval is argumentatively guided.

</details>


### [215] [LLM-Augmented Causal Discovery: Probabilistic Fusion of Edge Existence and Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27472)
*Neville K. Kitson,Anthony Constantinou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率依赖图（PDG）表示法，将贝叶斯网络结构学习（BNSL）和大型语言模型（LLM）这两种互补的因果知识来源统一为对边存在性和方向的不确定性分布，并通过加权平均进行融合，从而提升因果图结构学习的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNSL 从观测数据中学习贝叶斯网络结构时存在方向可识别性不足的问题（难以正确确定边的方向）；而 LLM 具备广泛但往往不可靠的因果知识。二者能力互补但各自存在缺陷，因此作者希望将这两种信息来源结合起来，以提升因果图结构的整体准确性。

Method: 提出一种新的表示形式——概率依赖图（PDG）：图中每条边关联一个关于有向、无向和不存在三种状态的分布，从而通过加权平均实现两种来源的融合。实验在26个基准网络上进行，组合了三种BNSL算法（FGES、Tabu、PC）与三种LLM（Gemini、Claude、GPT），并在多种提示和随机种子下进行评估。

Result: 简单的50/50融合在26个网络中的22个上优于单一来源中较优者，平均F1显著提升0.056（p<0.001）。分析表明两种来源作用互补：BNSL提供高召回率的边骨架（80%对LLM的60%），而LLM提供高准确率的边方向判定（96%对BNSL的77%）。

Conclusion: 将两种来源均表示为关于边存在性和方向性的概率不确定性，是一种实用且有效的提升因果图准确性的方法，验证了概率化融合思路的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Bayesian network structure learning (BNSL) from observational data struggles with orientation identifiability, while large language models (LLMs) offer broad but often unreliable causal knowledge. We propose combining these complementary sources through a novel representation, termed Probabilistic Dependency Graphs (PDGs). In a PDG, each edge is associated with a distribution over directed, undirected, and absent states, enabling fusion via weighted averaging. We evaluate this approach on 26 benchmark networks, combining ensembles of three BNSL algorithms (FGES, Tabu, PC) with three LLMs (Gemini, Claude, GPT) across multiple prompts and random seeds. A simple 50/50 fusion improves F1 over the better of either source alone in 22 of 26 networks, with a statistically significant mean improvement of $0.056$ $(p<0.001)$. Analysis reveals that the two sources play complementary roles: BNSL contributes a high-recall edge skeleton (80\% vs 60\% for LLM), while LLM contributes accurate edge orientation (96\% vs 77\% for BNSL). Our results show that representing both sources as probabilistic uncertainty over edge existence and orientation is a practical and effective way to improve causal graph accuracy.

</details>


### [216] [Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine: A Scientific Agent for PDE Discovery with Unknown Spatial Coefficient Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27475)
*YuJie Huang,WenWu He,ZhuoEr Lin,Congcong Liu,Dong Liang,Zhuo-Xu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HER-PDE，一个科学智能体框架，用于在异构介质中联合识别偏微分方程（PDE）的控制算子与非参数、时不变的系数场。该智能体分析由不同激励生成的两条带噪轨迹，提出完整的表达式树假设，并通过双向交叉激励迁移的评估接口与局部候选细化相结合来恢复生成算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构介质中发现PDE需要同时识别控制算子和参数化它的未知空间场，这两项任务相互耦合：改变场的位置会改变微分律，而足够灵活的场又可能掩盖单一轨迹上的结构误差。现有方法需要预设参数形式，难以处理这种联合识别问题。

Method: HER-PDE采用科学智能体框架，包含：1) 分析两个由不同激励生成、含噪声的轨迹；2) 提出完整的表达式树结构假设，将创造性结构探索与局部候选细化相结合；3) 通过假设评估接口（HEI）仅估计每个假设中显式声明的场，不添加缺失项；4) 使用双向交叉激励迁移对结构进行评分；5) 在密封的时间间隔上对所选定律进行审计。

Result: 在五个受控二维系统（含5%相对高斯状态噪声）上，智能体在所有五种情况下都恢复了生成算子，包括等价的符号场和乘积规则参数化；在九个未知系数场上，恢复的场达到约0.85的中位皮尔逊相关性和约0.28的中位相对L2误差。

Conclusion: 实验表明，智能体引导的假设细化可以在不预设空间系数参数形式的情况下恢复异构控制定律，为异构介质中的PDE发现提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Discovering PDEs in heterogeneous media requires jointly identifying the governing operator and the unknown spatial fields that parameterize it. These tasks are coupled: changing field placement changes the differential law, while a sufficiently flexible field can conceal structural error on a single trajectory. We present Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine for PDE Discovery (HER-PDE), a scientific-agent framework that discovers compositional PDE structure together with nonparametric, time-invariant coefficient fields. The Agent analyzes two noisy trajectories generated by different excitations, proposes complete expression-tree hypotheses, and combines creative structural exploration with local candidate refinement. Its Hypothesis Evaluation Interface (HEI) estimates only the fields explicitly declared in each hypothesis, never adds missing terms, and scores structures by bidirectional cross-excitation transfer. The selected law is subsequently audited on a sealed temporal interval. Across five controlled two-dimensional systems observed with 5 percent relative Gaussian state noise, the Agent recovers the generating operator in all five cases, including equivalent signed-field and product-rule parameterizations. Across nine unknown coefficient fields, the recovered fields attain a median Pearson correlation of approximately 0.85 and a median relative L2 error of approximately 0.28. These results show that agent-guided hypothesis refinement can recover heterogeneous governing laws without prescribing a parametric form for their spatial coefficients.

</details>


### [217] [AI Alignment through a Game-theoretic Lens: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27910)
*Yanan Cai,Zhongrui Zhao,Zhigang Lu,Ickjai Lee,Wei Emma Zhang,Minhui Xue,Yihong Zhang,Shuchao Pang,Wei Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，从博弈论的视角梳理大语言模型与AI智能体的对齐（alignment）研究，围绕偏好多样性、对齐优先级和时序动态三个挑战，评估现有对齐方法的适用性与开放性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法虽能提升有用性、无害性和可控性，但难以捕捉现实世界中依赖情境、非传递且由动态多方互动塑造的复杂人类偏好，因此需要新的理论框架来理解和应对这一挑战。

Method: 采用博弈论视角开展系统性综述：以关键博弈论要素为组织框架，将近期文献按偏好多样性、对齐优先级及时序动态三大挑战进行分类归纳，并对各对齐方法在博弈论框架下的契合程度进行评估。

Result: 厘清了哪些对齐方法确实能从博弈论分析中受益、哪些框架对应关系较松散，并指出了构建鲁棒、自适应、可验证AI系统所面临的剩余挑战。

Conclusion: 博弈论为AI对齐提供了有价值的分析视角，能够帮助澄清方法适用边界，但该框架对部分问题仍较松散，未来需在鲁棒性、适应性与可验证性方面继续攻关。

Abstract: As large language models and increasingly capable AI agents are deployed in high-risk settings, aligning them with complex human values has become a central challenge. Existing alignment methods, while effective in improving helpfulness, harmlessness, and controllability, often struggle to capture real-world preferences that are context-dependent, non-transitive, and shaped by dynamic multi-party interactions. This survey reviews AI alignment through a game-theoretic lens. Specifically, it organizes recent progress around key game-theoretic elements and synthesizes the literature along three challenges: preference diversity, alignment priority, and temporal dynamics. This perspective clarifies where current alignment methods genuinely benefit from game-theoretic analysis, where the framework is looser, and what challenges remain in building robust, adaptive, and verifiable AI systems.

</details>


### [218] [Class-Based Heuristic Selection for Solving the Flying Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27476)
*Sanyar Ahmadi,Pedram Asadzadeh,Amanj Khorramian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出类基启发式A*（CBHA*）算法，通过广义移动约束、七类运动学分类及类条件平局打破机制，在双柱飞块难题这一NP完全空间规划基准上，将成功率从标准A*的39%提升至93.4%，节点扩展量减少87.98%，有效分支因子维持在约3，证明类触发自适应启发式是高效空间规划的可行机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用启发式搜索在自主系统（仓储物流、机器人导航等）中虽广泛用于规划，但无法利用受限空间域的结构约束，导致在困难实例上性能急剧退化。本文旨在通过NP完全的空间规划微世界——双柱飞块难题（其瓶颈几何结构与多智能体路径搜索、自动驾驶导航和块重定位系统中的余隙-尺寸约束相似），研究如何利用结构约束设计更高效的启发式搜索方法。

Method: 提出CBHA*算法，包含三个核心组件：（1）广义移动约束，用于在空单元稀缺时捕获最小位移成本；（2）形式化运动学分类法，将状态空间划分为七个互斥类别，并基于空位率与目标块几何推导每类可采纳的启发式；（3）类条件平局打破机制，在深度优先与垂直距离排序之间动态切换以克服f值平台。算法在146个基准实例上进行验证。

Result: 在146个基准实例上，CBHA*成功率93.4%，显著优于Depth-Prioritized A*（64%）、Standard A*（39%）和BFS（17%）；相较Standard A*节点扩展减少87.98%，平均有效分支因子约为3，同时保持启发式可采纳性与最优性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，类触发的自适应启发式是高效空间规划的原则性机制，不仅能显著提升搜索性能，还能在结构上泛化到更广泛的物理约束系统（如多智能体路径搜索、自动驾驶导航、块重定位等），验证了将领域结构纳入启发式设计的研究路径。

Abstract: Heuristic search underlies planning in autonomous systems ranging from warehouse logistics to robotic navigation, yet generic heuristics fail to exploit the structural constraints that govern constrained spatial domains, causing search performance to degrade catastrophically on harder instances. We study this problem through the two-column Flying Block Puzzle, a rigorously NP-complete spatial planning microworld whose bottleneck geometry mirrors clearance-to-size constraints encountered in multi-agent path finding, autonomous vehicle navigation, and block relocation systems. We introduce the Class-Based Heuristic A* (CBHA*) algorithm, which integrates a General Move Constraint to capture minimum displacement costs when vacant units are scarce, a formal kinematic taxonomy partitioning the state space into seven mutually exclusive classes with provably admissible heuristics based on vacancy ratio and goal-piece geometry, and a class-conditional tie-breaking mechanism that dynamically switches between depth-priority and vertical-distance ordering to overcome f-value plateaus. Over 146 benchmark instances, CBHA* achieves a 93.4% success rate against 64% for Depth-Prioritized A*, 39% for Standard A*, and 17% for BFS, while reducing node expansions by 87.98% relative to Standard A* and sustaining an average effective branching factor of approximately 3, demonstrating that class-triggered adaptive heuristics constitute a principled mechanism for efficient spatial planning that generalizes structurally to physical constraint systems.

</details>


### [219] [Benchmarking General Mobile Assistants in Challenging Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27477)
*Yiqi Zhu,Feiyu Gao,Jiaxing Fan,Jiahui Zeng,Minggang Wu,Chenliang Li,Haiyang Xu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GMA是一个用于评估通用移动助手在真实挑战场景中表现的基准，涵盖7个开源应用、300个跨四个难度层级的任务；实验表明当前智能体性能随任务复杂度增加而显著下降，且harness设计（如上下文保留与显式状态追踪）能有效提升复杂工作流中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形用户界面已成为评估自主AI智能体在多模态交互任务上表现的重要环境，但现有基准（如AndroidWorld和MobileWorld）在应用覆盖范围和任务设计上尚未充分反映真实移动使用的多样性与复杂性，因此需要更贴近现实的评测基准。

Method: 提出GMA基准：基于开源项目构建7个应用（涵盖生活方式分享、旅行规划等领域），设计了300个跨四个难度层级（从原子动作到复杂多步工作流）的任务；评测8个前沿模型，并在共享环境、模型设置和任务分类下对harness设计选择（包括上下文保留和显式状态追踪）进行受控消融研究。

Result: 随着任务复杂度增加，所有模型的性能均显著下降，当前智能体距离可靠处理真实用户需求仍有很大差距；适当的harness设计能有效提升性能，尤其在复杂工作流上，但具体设计的效果会因基础模型不同而有差异。

Conclusion: GMA是对现有基准的补充，通过扩大应用覆盖范围和任务复杂度，为评估移动智能体、研究harness设计如何支撑复杂移动工作流中的可靠执行提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Graphical user interfaces have emerged as an important environment for evaluating autonomous AI agents on multimodal interactive tasks. Existing benchmarks such as AndroidWorld and MobileWorld provide strong foundations for mobile agent evaluation, but their application coverage and task design do not yet fully capture the diversity and complexity of realistic mobile use. We present GMA, a benchmark for evaluating general mobile assistants in challenging real-world scenarios. GMA introduces seven applications based on open-source projects, spanning domains such as lifestyle sharing and travel planning, and 300 tasks across four difficulty tiers, from atomic actions to complex multi-step workflows. We evaluate eight frontier models and find that performance declines substantially as task complexity increases, with current agents remaining far from reliably handling realistic user requirements. We further conduct controlled ablation studies of agent harness choices, including context retention and explicit state tracking, under a shared environment, model setting, and task taxonomy. Results show that appropriate harness design can meaningfully improve performance, particularly on demanding workflows, while the effectiveness of specific designs can vary across foundation models. Overall, GMA complements existing benchmarks by expanding application coverage and task complexity, providing a challenging testbed for evaluating mobile agents and studying how harness design supports reliable execution in complex mobile workflows.

</details>


### [220] [Effectiveness of IoT and Deep Learning for Detection and Severity Assessment of Postelectrotermes militaris in Tea Plantations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27480)
*D. K. C. Senevirathna,A. A. E. Nanayakkara,H. M. C. K. Kulathunga,J. K. D. P. Nadula,R. M. Mapatuna,Malithi Nawarathne,Jaliya L. Wijayaraja,P. D. Senanayake,Samitha Vidhanaarachchi,Kalpani Manathunga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合物联网（IoT）与深度学习的声学监测框架，用于茶园高地活木白蚁（ULWT）侵害的早期检测与严重程度评估。通过在 Raspberry Pi 设备上使用高灵敏度麦克风非侵入式采集茶树音频，利用傅里叶变换生成的语谱图训练 CNN 进行分类与侵染概率估计，并结合声学振幅、邻近染病植株和地理空间映射构建加权严重程度模型。田间试验中 CNN 在测试集上达到 81.5% 的准确率，验证了该框架在真实噪声环境下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 茶园极易遭受高地活木白蚁（Postelectrotermes militaris）的侵害，当侵染未被及时发现时会造成严重损害。现有检测手段难以实现早期、非侵入式的监测，因此需要一种能够在真实环境噪声下进行早期检测和严重程度评估的自动化框架，以支持茶园管理者及时采取防控措施。

Method: 利用连接在 Raspberry Pi 物联网设备上的高灵敏度麦克风，非侵入式地从茶树茎干捕获音频信号并记录地理坐标；经过修剪、重采样和分割后获得 2,000 个十秒样本（1,000 个健康、1,000 个受侵染），划分为 1,600 个训练、200 个验证和 200 个测试样本；使用傅里叶变换得到的语谱图训练 CNN 进行侵染分类与概率估计；构建加权严重程度模型，综合 CNN 概率、平均声学振幅和 5 米内存活的邻近侵染植株，并通过地理空间映射可视化侵染分布。

Result: 在斯里兰卡 Pundaluoya 一个受 ULWT 影响的茶园中开展的田间试验证明了该框架在现实环境噪声下的可行性。在留出测试集上，CNN 实现了 81.5% 的准确率、80.6% 的精确率、83.0% 的召回率、81.8% 的 F1 分数和 0.819 的 ROC-AUC。除二元侵染检测外，框架还引入了基于侵染概率、声学振幅和邻近侵染植株的定量严重程度评估，并生成严重程度与地理空间输出。

Conclusion: 该 IoT 声学监测与深度学习框架能够实现 ULWT 侵染的早期检测和严重程度量化评估，其空间可视化输出可帮助茶园管理者识别高风险区域、优先安排田间巡检，并实施更及时、更具针对性的防控措施，具有实际推广应用价值。所用数据集已在 Kaggle 公开（Senevirathna et al. 2026）。

Abstract: Tea plantations are vulnerable to Postelectrotermes militaris, commonly known as the Upcountry Live Wood Termite (ULWT), which can cause substantial damage when infestations remain undetected. This study proposes an IoT-enabled acoustic monitoring framework integrated with deep learning for early detection and severity assessment of ULWT infestations in tea plantations.
  Research Method: Audio signals were captured non-invasively from tea trunks using a high-sensitivity microphone connected to a Raspberry Pi-based IoT device, with geographic coordinates recorded for spatial tracking. After trimming, resampling, and segmentation, 2,000 ten-second samples were obtained, comprising 1,000 healthy and 1,000 infested samples, and divided into 1,600 training, 200 validation, and 200 test samples. The dataset used in this study is publicly available on Kaggle (Senevirathna et al. 2026). Fourier-derived spectrograms trained a CNN for infestation classification and probability estimation. A weighted severity model combined CNN probability, mean acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants within 5 m, with geospatial mapping used to visualize infestation distribution.
  Findings and Values: Field trials in a ULWT-affected tea plantation in Pundaluoya demonstrated feasibility under realistic environmental noise. On the held-out test set, the CNN achieved 81.5% accuracy, 80.6% precision, 83.0% recall, 81.8% F1-score, and 0.819 ROC-AUC. Beyond binary infestation detection, the framework introduced quantitative severity assessment using infestation probability, acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants. The resulting severity and geospatial outputs can support plantation managers in identifying high-risk areas, prioritizing field inspections, and implementing more timely and targeted control measures.

</details>


### [221] [Context Localization for Generalized Level-Based Evaluation in Knowledge-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27482)
*Ondrej Hutník,Natália Puškárová*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于知识的系统中广义水平评估的上下文局部化问题。作者建立了一个框架，其中定义在事实、规则、案例、准则或证据单元上的结构化非负分数通过可容许知识上下文上的条件聚合测试进行评估，广义水平测度在所有聚合支持达到规定水平的上下文上最大化单调集函数。核心定理刻画了“按上下文B过滤分数”等价于“按与B的交集局部化可容许上下文”这一一致性的充要条件：对所有单调集函数成立当且仅当满足两个结构条件——关于上下文的单调性，以及排除B外部正局部化支持的约简性质。文章进一步分析了产生该约简性质的逐点与块生成机制，将结果推广到参数化系统，并将其解释为上下文依赖证据选择、非加性支持评估与水平基知识聚合的稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在知识库系统中，证据、规则或事实往往带有非负分数，需要通过可容许上下文上的条件聚合测试进行水平化评估。一个自然且重要的建模问题是：当分数被某上下文B过滤后，广义水平测度（在聚合支持达到规定水平的上下文中最大化单调集函数）是否可通过将可容许上下文与B求交来“局部化”实现。作者旨在严格刻画这种过滤-局部化一致性何时成立，为知识系统评估提供可验证的结构条件。

Method: 本文采用公理化方法：首先定义建立在结构化非负分数与条件聚合测试之上的广义水平测度，然后形式化“上下文过滤”与“上下文局部化”两种操作的一致性概念。作者证明了主定理，表明对所有单调集函数该一致性成立当且仅当满足两个结构条件：上下文方向的单调性以及排除B外部正局部化支持的约简性质；随后分别从逐点机制与块生成机制两个角度给出产生约简性质的充分结构，并将核心结论推广到参数化系统，最后将其解释为稳定性判据。

Result: 主定理给出了过滤-局部化一致性的充要刻画：对所有单调集函数而言，该性质成立当且仅当满足上下文单调性与排除B外部正局部化支持的约简性质。文章还给出了逐点和块生成两类机制以构造满足约简性质的系统，并成功将结果扩展至参数化系统，从而得到具有普遍性的稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 论文确立了知识系统中上下文局部化与水平化评估相一致的精确边界条件，指出上下文单调性与约简性质是这一稳定性成立的“最经济”的结构前提。所得结果不仅统一了上下文过滤与局部化两种视角，还为证据选择、非加性支持聚合和水平基知识评估提供了可操作的理论判据，并可通过参数化系统加以推广应用。

Abstract: We study context localization for generalized level-based evaluation in knowledge-based systems. The framework models situations where a structured nonnegative score, defined on facts, rules, cases, criteria or evidence units, is evaluated through conditional aggregation tests on admissible knowledge contexts. The generalized level measure maximizes a monotone set function over all contexts whose aggregated support reaches a prescribed level. We characterize when filtering the score by a context $B$ is equivalent to localizing the admissible contexts by intersection with $B$. The main theorem shows that this consistency holds for all monotone set functions if and only if two structural conditions are satisfied: monotonicity with respect to contexts and a reduction property excluding positive localized support outside $B$. We analyze pointwise and block-generated mechanisms producing the reduction property, extend the result to parameterized systems, and interpret it as a stability criterion for context-dependent evidence selection, non-additive support evaluation and level-based knowledge aggregation.

</details>


### [222] [CareGraph: An Auditable Hybrid AI Framework for Evidence-Grounded Personalized Longitudinal Health Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27484)
*Pratik Ghawate,Tanvi Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CareGraph 是一个可审计的混合 AI 框架，将异构医疗记录转化为带优先级的趋势、缺失背景提示、有边界的下一步行动、讨论问题及可溯源解释，并通过确定性分析、上下文检测、图构建、受约束的语言模型合成、证据验证、安全控制与发布门控等流水线，在不进行诊断、预测、治疗选择或自主临床决策的前提下，为个性化健康系统提供安全有界的坚实基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在变革个性化医疗，但临床、自我报告和可穿戴设备等多源证据碎片化，难以解释和追踪，导致现有系统缺乏可审计性与安全边界。

Method: 提出 CareGraph 框架，采用流水线设计：确定性分析、缺失上下文检测、图构建、受约束的 LM 合成、证据验证、安全控制与发布门控。它将异构记录组织为优先级趋势、缺失信息指示、有边界建议、讨论问题及带溯源解释，明确不进行诊断、结果预测、治疗选择或自主决策。评估采用合成队列（开发/验证/留出各 400 例），并引入冻结 OLS 趋势规则+充分性门控、安全规则集 v1.2、图检索审计以及与 GPT 5.6 的对比。

Result: 留出集上，OLS 趋势规则+充分性门控取得 0.827 准确率、0.837 宏 F1（95% CI 0.819–0.854）与 0.974 的不足数据 F1；缺失上下文检测严格微 F1 为 0.815，远超旧检测器的 0.318；安全规则集 v1.2 达到 1.000 精确率、0.950 召回率与 0.974 F1。80 名患者的图审计中，79 份合成与 78 份展示未回退，1 个输出被阻断、1 个因无效证据键安全失败。相较 GPT 5.6（56 名配对患者），CareGraph 更快（40.15 秒 vs 49.62 秒）、更短（661 vs 1163 词），探索性词汇对齐更优；而基线 token 更少、引用原始证据更多。

Conclusion: CareGraph 通过可验证溯源与确定性检索证明了审计能力，为智能个性化健康系统提供了安全有界、透明可审计的底座；图增量对生成的影响仍需配对评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming personalized healthcare, yet fragmented clinical, self reported, and wearable evidence remains difficult to interpret and trace. We present CareGraph, an auditable hybrid AI framework that converts heterogeneous records into prioritized trends, missing context indicators, bounded next steps, discussion questions, and provenance linked explanations. CareGraph organizes evidence without diagnosing, predicting outcomes, selecting treatment, or making autonomous clinical decisions. Its pipeline covers deterministic analysis, context detection, graph construction, constrained language model synthesis, evidence validation, safety controls, and release gating. Tests used synthetic cohorts of 400 patients each for development, validation, and holdout. On holdout data, a frozen ordinary least squares trend rule with a sufficiency gate achieved 0.827 accuracy, 0.837 macro F1 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.819 to 0.854, and 0.974 insufficient data F1. Missing context detection achieved 0.815 strict micro F1 versus 0.318 for the legacy detector. On an authored holdout benchmark, safety ruleset version 1.2 achieved 1.000 precision, 0.950 recall, and 0.974 F1. An audit requiring graph retrieval across 80 patients yielded 79 syntheses and 78 presentations without fallback; one output was blocked and one failed closed because of an invalid evidence key. Against monolithic GPT 5.6 on 56 matched patients, CareGraph was faster at 40.15 versus 49.62 seconds, shorter at 661 versus 1,163 words, and showed better exploratory lexical alignment with longitudinal targets; the baseline used fewer tokens and cited more raw evidence. Graph auditing verified provenance and deterministic retrieval; incremental graph effects on generation require paired evaluation. CareGraph offers a safety bounded foundation for intelligent personalized health systems.

</details>


### [223] [Thinking Costs Tokens: When More Structure is Worth the Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27506)
*Thomas Nolasque,John Grey,Calista Pham,Ankit Vani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理结构（如搜索、验证、修正）是否存在一个 token 预算阈值：低于该阈值时规划与验证的开销会损害性能，高于该阈值时则会带来收益。作者在 FinQA 和 TAT-QA 金融推理任务上、使用 GPT-5.4 mini、在 14 个预算档位（250 到 42,000 个输出等价 token）下对比了单次调用的"单体"系统与带规划、无标签检查及修复的"验证搜索"系统，共运行 1,000 个案例、28,000 个完整单元格。结果表明交叉点出现在 1,000–1,500 个输出等价 token 之间：在 1,000 token 处单体准确率约 18% 而验证搜索接近 0%；从 1,500 token 起验证搜索持续超越单体，最高档位约 44% 对 40%，并由严格的交并检验（两端 p ≤ 0.001）证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语言模型添加推理结构（搜索、验证、修订）会消耗用于解决问题的预算，因此需要探究是否存在一个 token 预算阈值，阈值之下规划与验证的开销反而损害性能、阈值之上才能带来收益，从而为推理结构的使用时机提供依据。

Method: 在 FinQA 和 TAT-QA 两个金融推理任务上，使用 GPT-5.4 mini，设置 14 个预算档位（250 至 42,000 个输出等价 token），对比两种系统：一是单次 LLM 调用的"单体"系统，二是增加规划、无标签（label-blind）检查与修复能力的"验证搜索"架构。共运行 1,000 个案例，产生 28,000 个完整单元格，并用严格的交并检验（intersection-union test）确认交叉区间。

Result: 两个系统在两个最低档位均得到 0% 准确率（无法容纳完整提示）。在 1,000 token 处单体达到约 18%，而验证搜索接近 0%（规划开销挤占了答案空间）。从 1,500 token 起验证搜索超越单体并保持稳定优势，最高档位约为 44%，单体约为 40%。交叉点位于 1,000–1,500 个输出等价 token 之间，交并检验两端 p ≤ 0.001。

Conclusion: 存在一个 token 预算阈值：在约 1,000–1,500 个输出等价 token 之间发生了性能交叉，低于该阈值时推理结构的规划与验证开销会降低性能（验证搜索甚至不如单体），高于该阈值时验证搜索能够稳定超越单体并带来收益。这为推理结构应在充足预算下才使用提供了量化证据。

Abstract: Adding inference structure to a language model lets it search, verify, and revise, but these actions consume the very budget they are supposed to use well. In this paper, we investigate whether there exists a token-budget threshold, below which the overhead of planning and verification hurts performance and above which it helps. We evaluate two systems on FinQA and TAT-QA financial reasoning tasks, using GPT-5.4 mini across 14 budget tiers ranging from 250 to 42,000 output-equivalent tokens. The first system is a monolith, which is a single LLM call. The second is a verified search architecture that adds planning, label-blind checking, and repair capabilities. We run 1,000 cases for a total of 28,000 completed cells. Both systems score 0% at the two lowest tiers, where neither can fit a complete prompt. At 1,000 tokens, the monolith reaches 18% accuracy while verified search scores near 0%, since the planning overhead leaves no room for an answer. From 1,500 tokens onward, verified search surpasses the monolith and maintains a consistent advantage, reaching approximately 44% at the highest tiers while the monolith reaches approximately 40%. The crossover occurs between 1,000 and 1,500 output-equivalent tokens, confirmed by a strict intersection-union test ($p \le 0.001$ at both endpoints).

</details>


### [224] [WM-R1: Training GUI Agents to Reason and leverage World Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27508)
*Yu Han,Tianwen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WM-R1 是首个利用世界模型而非真实环境来训练移动端 GUI 智能体的强化学习框架。它用世界模型替代真实 Android 环境进行所有 rollouts，并将世界模型嵌入智能体的推理过程以预判动作后果，同时引入多维规则奖励，在无需真实环境交互的情况下显著优于 GRPO-only 基线和推理时仿真方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的 GUI 智能体虽然在移动平台上展现了强大的环境学习能力，但 RL 通常需要大量真实环境交互，导致资源成本高且训练不稳定，这在 GUI 场景中尤为突出。因此需要一种减少真实环境依赖的 RL 训练方案。

Method: 提出 WM-R1 框架：(1) 以世界模型作为所有 rollouts 的状态转移来源，完全替代训练循环中的真实 Android 环境；(2) 将世界模型直接嵌入智能体的思考过程，使其在执行最终动作前先推理候选动作的后果；(3) 支持基于世界模型的、大规模并行且可按步骤粒度生成的轨迹；(4) 引入多维规则化奖励，联合优化任务成功率、轨迹效率和世界模型利用率；(5) 构建了包含 2000 个高难度任务的高质量训练数据集。

Result: 在 Android 移动端基准上的实验表明，WM-R1 训练的智能体显著优于仅使用 GRPO 的基线方法，也优于推理时仿真方法。

Conclusion: WM-R1 验证了世界模型可以完全替代真实环境用于移动 GUI 智能体的强化学习训练，在消除真实环境交互需求的同时提升性能，为低成本、稳定且高效的 GUI 智能体 RL 训练提供了新范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: GUI agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) have showcased strong environment learning capabilities on mobile platforms. However, RL typically demands extensive real-environment interactions, leading to high resource costs and instability, especially in GUI scenarios. To address these, we propose WM-R1, the first reinforcement learning framework that trains mobile GUI agents with world models instead of real environments. Specifically, world models serve as the source of state transitions during all rollouts, replacing the real Android environment within the training loop. WM-R1 also embeds world models directly into the thinking process, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of candidate actions before committing to the final action. Crucially, WM-R1 eliminates the need for real-environment interaction, supports massively parallelized and step-level granularized trajectory generation grounded in world models, and introduces a multi-dimensional rule-based reward that jointly optimizes task success, trajectory efficiency, and world model utilization. For efficient training, we curate a high-quality dataset of 2000 challenging tasks. Experiments on Android mobile benchmarks demonstrate that WM-R1-trained agents significantly outperform GRPO-only baselines and inference-time simulation methods. Code is available at https://github.com/genalyu/WM-R1 .

</details>


### [225] [SETU: An Agentic Ecosystem for Multilingual, Persona-Aware Communication Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27524)
*Jonnalagadda Maruthi Tejas,Uponika Barman Roy,Tilottama Goswami,Samir Goswami,Mousita Dhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SETU 是一个面向企业沟通教练场景的多智能体（agentic）生态系统，将视频、音频语音、文本相关性、评分、通知与报告等分析任务分解为专门智能体，并通过“信任感知编排”进行协调，为多语言环境下的招聘面试与销售演讲提供可审计、逐模态归因的教练报告，且保留人工评审的最终判断权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业培训团队需要可扩展且可解释的工具来提升多语言环境下的员工沟通能力，而现有系统要么孤立地对文本、音频或视频分别评分，要么产生难以审计的黑盒输出，无法满足教练辅导场景中对过程透明、可追责的要求。

Method: 论文提出 SETU 智能体生态系统，将沟通分析分解为视频、音频语音、文本相关性、评分、通知和报告等专门智能体，由信任感知编排机制协调协作；系统为两个场景设计（招聘者-候选人资格与兴趣电话、面向目标受众调整的销售演讲），并生成带模态归因的教练报告以支持形成性训练，最终判断保留给人工评审者。

Result: 受限于评测资源，论文仅在第二个场景（销售演讲的目标受众适配）上报告实验结果；SETU（多种印度语言中意为“桥梁”）旨在弥合区域语言与受众期望之间的沟通鸿沟。

Conclusion: SETU 通过模块化智能体分解与信任感知编排，在可解释性、审计性和规模化之间取得平衡，为多语言企业沟通教练提供了一种可落地的范式；未来仍需在更多场景（含招聘者-候选人场景）中扩展验证。

Abstract: Corporate training teams need scalable and explainable tools to improve workforce communication in multilingual settings. Existing systems often score text, audio, or video in isolation, or produce black-box outputs that are difficult to audit for coaching use. This paper presents SETU, an agentic ecosystem for corporate communication coaching aimed at recruiters, frontline sales professionals and training units who prepare for audience specific conversations. SETU is designed for two scoped scenarios: (i) recruiter-candidate eligibility-and-interest calls with persona context and (ii) sales pitches with target-audience adaptation; owing to limited evaluation resources, this paper reports results on scenario (ii) only. The ecosystem decomposes analysis into specialized video, audio-speech, text-relevance, scoring, notification and reporting agents coordinated through trust-aware orchestration. It generates modality-attributed coaching reports for formative training, with human reviewers retaining final judgment. The name SETU (bridge in several Indic languages) reflects the goal of bridging communication gaps across regional languages and audience expectations.

</details>


### [226] [Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator: A Compact Multimodal, Multilingual, and Reasoning Enabled Content Safety Moderator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27548)
*Varun Singh,Anuj Doshi,Makesh Narsimhan Sreedhar,Shaona Ghosh,Katherine Luna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Nemotron 3.5 内容安全审核器（Nemotron 3.5 CS），一个紧凑的 4B 参数视觉-语言安全审核模型，可跨 12 种语言联合分类用户提示、图像与助手回复，同时支持低延迟的安全标签输出与可选的推理轨迹（遵循自定义策略并标识违规类别），并配套发布多模态多语言安全数据集。实验表明它在增加图像条件和策略条件审核的同时，与专用审核模型保持广泛竞争力，可作为可部署的前线安全组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署 AI 应用的安全审核正从纯文本提示扩展到图像、文档、截图及生成回复，且策略因领域而异。现有护栏通常只覆盖该场景的一部分，难以同时兼顾广泛覆盖、自定义策略控制和低计算成本。

Method: 提出 Nemotron 3.5 CS，一个紧凑的 4B 视觉-语言安全审核模型。它联合分类用户提示、图像和助手回复，覆盖 12 种语言；对于延迟敏感场景直接返回安全标签，在需要推理时可生成简洁的推理轨迹以应用给定的自定义策略并标识违规类别。同时发布了多模态多语言安全训练数据集，涵盖人工标注的真实图像审核、良性视觉-语言与文档任务、合成罕见风险与越狱案例、自定义策略示例。

Result: 在多模态安全、文本审核、多语言鲁棒性、自定义策略遵循、良性误报和延迟等多项评估中，Nemotron 3.5 CS 表现出实用的覆盖权衡：在增加图像条件与策略条件下的审核能力的同时，仍与专用审核模型保持在广泛任务上的竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑的视觉-语言审核模型可以作为可部署的前线安全组件，而推理能力可选择性用于审计与策略审查等场景。

Abstract: Safety moderation for deployed AI applications is moving beyond text-only prompts: systems increasingly need to judge images, documents, screenshots, and generated responses under policies that vary across domains. Existing guardrails usually cover only part of this setting, making it difficult to combine broad coverage, custom policy control, and low compute cost. We present Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator, also referred to as Nemotron 3.5 CS in this paper for brevity, a compact 4B vision-language safety moderator that jointly classifies user prompts, images, and assistant responses across 12 languages. Nemotron 3.5 CS returns safety labels for latency-sensitive moderation and can additionally produce concise reasoning traces that apply supplied custom policies and identify violated categories when reasoning is requested. We also release a multimodal and multilingual safety dataset for guard training, spanning human-labeled real-image moderation, benign vision-language and document tasks, synthetic rare-risk and jailbreak cases, and custom-policy examples. Across evaluations spanning multimodal safety, text moderation, multilingual robustness, custom-policy following, benign false positives, and latency, Nemotron 3.5 CS demonstrates a practical coverage tradeoff: it adds image-conditioned and policy-conditioned moderation while remaining broadly competitive with specialized guard models. These results suggest that compact vision-language moderators can serve as deployable front-line safety components, with reasoning used selectively for audit and policy review.

</details>


### [227] [LongGuard: Mechanistic Analysis and Training-Free Mitigation of Long-Context Failure in Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27580)
*Ziyang Chen,Xing Wu,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LongGuard，一个用于评估、机制分析与缓解大语言模型安全护栏在长上下文中失效问题的框架。通过 Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack（SafetyNIAH）任务在 0.25k–32k 长度网格上测试 15 种主流护栏，发现不安全内容召回率平均单调下降超过 50%，并将失败归因于不安全"针"的比例稀释而非绝对长度。通过三层注意力-逻辑值-行为分析定位机制：注意力质量被稀释、不安全/安全逻辑值差被压缩、检测行为随之崩溃。基于机制分析提出两种免训练缓解方法（Chunked Detection 与 Attention-Head Sharpening）及部署协议 CAHR，在五个基准上平均提升 22% 与 13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全护栏是抵御大语言模型有害输入与输出的最后防线，但现有护栏几乎仅在短文本上训练和评估，导致其在长上下文场景下系统性失效。本文的核心动机是系统性地评估、理解并缓解这种长上下文护栏失效问题，填补安全评估在长文本维度上的空白。

Method: 1) 提出 Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack（SafetyNIAH）任务，在 0.25k–32k 长度网格上评估 15 种护栏；2) 设计 Benign-Fill 与 Needle-Repeat 配对实验，区分"比例稀释"与"绝对长度"两种假设；3) 对六个护栏进行注意力-逻辑值-行为三层机制分析，并隔离出一组护栏特化的检索头；4) 基于机制提出两种免训练缓解方法：Chunked Detection（CD，分块检测）与 Attention-Head Sharpening（AHS，注意力头锐化）；5) 提出部署协议 Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing（CAHR），按上下文长度与审计侧选择配置。

Result: 在 15 种主流护栏上，不安全召回率随长度单调下降平均超 50%；配对实验表明失败源于不安全"针"的比例稀释。六护栏上注意力质量被稀释、不安全/安全逻辑值差同步压缩、检测行为随之崩溃，且该链条在控制长度后仍一致。CAHR-CD 与 CAHR-AHS 在涵盖合成数据、长上下文攻击与推理模型输出的五个基准上，将六护栏平均性能分别提升 22% 与 13%。

Conclusion: 长上下文护栏失效是一种可机制化解释的系统性缺陷，其根源在于不安全信号随上下文增长被比例性地稀释。通过机制层面的理解（注意力-逻辑值-行为链条与护栏特化检索头），免训练的输入侧（分块）与模型侧（注意力头锐化）干预以及按长度自适应的部署协议，可以有效缓解该问题，为长上下文安全评估与护栏加固提供了可复现的框架。

Abstract: Safety guardrails serve as the last line of defense against harmful inputs and outputs of large language models (LLMs), yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on short text. We present LongGuard, a framework that evaluates, mechanistically analyzes, and mitigates long-context guardrail failure. We formulate the task as Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack (SafetyNIAH) over a 0.25k-32k length grid; across 15 mainstream guardrails, unsafe recall drops monotonically by more than 50% on average, and a paired Benign-Fill vs. Needle-Repeat design attributes the failure to proportional dilution of the unsafe needle rather than to absolute length. A three-layer attention-logit-behavior analysis on six guardrails locates the mechanism: attention mass on the unsafe needle is diluted, the unsafe-over-safe logit margin is compressed in lockstep, and the detection decision collapses accordingly, with this attention->logit->behavior chain remaining consistent after partialling out length. We further isolate a sparse set of guard-specialized retrieval heads that exhibit partial specificity relative to their base models. Building on the analysis, we propose two training-free mitigations - Chunked Detection (CD) and Attention-Head Sharpening (AHS) - and a deployment protocol, Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing (CAHR), that selects configurations by context length and audit side. Across five benchmarks spanning synthetic data, long-context attacks, and reasoning-model outputs, CAHR-CD and CAHR-AHS improve the six-guardrail average by 22% and 13%, respectively. Code and data are available online.

</details>


### [228] [Generative AI Expands the Intellectual Reach of Course Based Undergraduate Research Experiences (CUREs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27638)
*Aditi Babar,Kristin J. Davin,Alex Dornburg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 三学期生物信息学与基因组学CURE课程的纵向质性研究表明，将生成式人工智能嵌入课程式本科科研体验，可通过个性化按需支架、分布式认知参与和保留学科判断三种机制，扩展本科初学者的研究范围、协作方式与责任承担水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
