AGENT-O provides a reusable ontology, semantic Agent Card profile, and reporting-completeness workflow for structured reporting and gap identification, but does not assess agent quality or deployment readiness.

</details>


### [290] [Propagating construction-time knowledge quality into medical question answering: A framework grounded in clinical guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28360)
*Jie Hu,Junjie Wang,Shan Lu,Yifang Hu,Gong Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个质量感知框架，将知识图谱构建阶段产生的三元组质量信号（结构符合性SchemaConf与证据支持EvidScore融合为Q(t)）作为持久属性，传播到下游检索与证据提示中，在中文糖尿病临床指南上的图谱问答中显著降低知识遗漏与冲突输出，并提升临床医生的证据可信度评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽已用于从临床指南构建知识图谱，但抽取的三元组在结构有效性和证据支持上参差不齐；而基于图谱增强的问答系统在检索时通常只优化查询相关性，很少复用图谱构建阶段产生的质量信息。这导致构建期质量控制与推理期证据使用脱节，作者因而探究构建期三元组质量能否作为持久信号服务于下游证据选择与呈现。

Method: 提出质量感知框架：将结构符合性（SchemaConf）与证据支持（EvidScore）作为互补维度建模，融合为逐三元组质量信号Q(t)。不同于仅将质量用于过滤，框架将Q(t)及衍生的质量分级作为图谱属性保留，并传播到质量加权子图检索与分级条件证据提示中，同时保留段落级出处信息。实验在中文糖尿病临床指南上进行，并针对跨版本与跨模型漂移场景评估及跨生成器泛化测试。

Result: 质量信号效用具有分布依赖性。在跨版本与跨模型漂移下，融合的Q(t)三元组质量判别能力优于单一维度（AUC 0.748，对比EvidScore 0.703与SchemaConf 0.645）；在指南问答中，传播构建期质量将所需知识遗漏率从16.3%降至5.3%，冲突输出从16.3%降至2.7%，证据落地精度达81.6%且无效引用几乎为零。盲法临床医生评分中，完整框架为4.68分（对比无检索4.21分，接近oracle条件4.80分），跨生成器实验呈现一致趋势。

Conclusion: 构建期三元组质量可以作为持久信号有效贯通知识图谱构建与下游问答推理，融合结构符合性与证据支持的质量信号在分布漂移下仍具强判别力，并能显著提升证据落地能力、降低知识遗漏与输出冲突，获得临床医生认可，验证了将构造期质量控制复用于推理期证据使用的价值。

Abstract: Large language models have facilitated knowledge graph (KG) construction from clinical guidelines, but extracted triples vary in structural validity and evidential support. Meanwhile, graph-augmented question answering (QA) systems typically optimize query relevance during retrieval, with limited reuse of quality information produced during KG construction. This creates a disconnect between construction-time quality control and inference-time evidence use. We investigate whether construction-time triple quality can serve as a persistent signal for downstream evidence selection and presentation. We propose a quality-aware framework that models structural conformance (SchemaConf) and evidential support (EvidScore) as complementary dimensions and fuses them into a per-triple quality signal, Q(t). Rather than using quality solely for filtering, the framework retains Q(t) and derived quality tiers as graph attributes and propagates them into quality-weighted subgraph retrieval and tier-conditioned evidence prompting, while preserving passage-level provenance. Experiments on Chinese diabetes clinical guidelines show that the utility of the quality signal is distribution dependent. Under cross-version and cross-model shift, the fused Q(t) provides stronger triple-quality discrimination than either component alone (AUC 0.748 vs. 0.703 for EvidScore and 0.645 for SchemaConf). In guideline-grounded QA, propagating construction-time quality reduces required-knowledge omission from 16.3% to 5.3% and conflicting outputs from 16.3% to 2.7%, with an evidence-grounded precision of 81.6% and near-zero invalid citations. Blinded clinician ratings favor the full framework over no retrieval (4.68 vs. 4.21 on a five-point scale) and approach the oracle condition (4.80), while cross-generator experiments show consistent trends.

</details>


### [291] [GRACE:Gradient-guided Coreset Selection for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28361)
*Praveen Bushipaka,Andrea D'Angelo,Lucia Passaro,Tommaso Cucinotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GRACE，一种基于梯度引导的核集（coreset）选择方法，用于在大语言模型机器遗忘中从异构语料中自动构建遗忘集和保留集：先由种子示例计算遗忘方向，再用非负正交匹配追踪选出紧凑遗忘核集；在剔除遗忘方向后在剩余梯度空间中用聚类正交匹配追踪选取保留示例，在保持遗忘质量的同时提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 机器遗忘方法通常假设遗忘集和保留集已预先给定，但现实场景中请求往往只提供少量体现不良行为的示例，需要从异构语料中推断遗忘集与保留集，这一数据选择问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: GRACE 分两步：1) 从诱发不良行为的种子示例计算遗忘方向，并用非负正交匹配追踪（NN-OMP）选取遗忘核集，使其梯度近似该方向；2) 在投影掉遗忘方向后的剩余梯度空间中，采用聚类正交匹配追踪（clustered OMP）选取保留示例以保持模型效用。

Result: 在两个目标领域、两个模型家族和四种遗忘算法上，GRACE 在维持遗忘质量相当的同时提升了模型效用，且相较于先前的基于梯度的选择方法具有持续且一致的提升。

Conclusion: GRACE 有效解决了遗忘场景中遗忘集与保留集的数据选择问题，证明梯度引导的核集选择能够兼顾遗忘效果与模型效用保持，为实际部署中的 LLM 机器遗忘提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning methods for Large Language Models typically assume pre-specified forget and retain sets. In realistic settings, however, requests may provide only a few examples of undesired behavior, requiring forget and retain sets to be inferred from heterogeneous corpora. We study this data-selection problem and propose GRACE , a gradient-guided coreset selection method that constructs both forget and retain sets for LLM unlearning. GRACE first computes a forget direction from seed examples that elicit the undesired behavior, then selects a compact forget coreset whose gradients approximate this direction using non-negative orthogonal matching pursuit. To preserve model utility, it selects retain examples after projecting out the forget direction and applying clustered orthogonal matching pursuit in the remaining gradient space. Across two target domains, two model families, and four unlearning algorithms, GRACE improves model utility while maintaining comparable forget quality, with particularly consistent gains over prior gradient-based selection methods.

</details>


### [292] [EvoUndo: Recoverability-Constrained Self-Evolution for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28363)
*Tanmay Sah,Dolly Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoUndo是一个用于表示、合成、诊断和独立验证大语言模型智能体自我修改可恢复性的框架。在600个一次性自我进化任务中发现197个提升能力但无法通过可恢复性验证的突变；传统修复策略恢复0/197，扩展恢复演算使预言机恢复达到191/197。通过协议锁定的2x2"状态接地×表达力"干预分离出两个瓶颈，并发现模型依赖的负交互效应，最终表明可靠的智能体自我进化需要协同设计验证、状态接地、见证语义与恢复语言表达力，而非仅依赖迭代提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在运行时越来越多地修改自身的提示词、工具、中间件、资源和执行框架。这种自我进化可以提升能力，但一次成功的突变可能留下持久性影响，在与其创建时不同的状态下无法安全逆转，因此需要系统性地保障自我修改的可恢复性。

Method: 提出EvoUndo框架，用于表示、合成、诊断并在反事实状态下独立验证模型生成自我修改的可恢复性。方法包括：将恢复问题编码在原始恢复语言L0与扩展恢复演算中；采用确定性预言机分析；并设计一个协议锁定的2x2"状态接地(x)表达力"干预实验，以分离状态地址接地的精确性与恢复语言表达力两类瓶颈。

Result: 在600个未见过的单次自我进化任务中识别出197个提升能力但无法通过可恢复性验证的突变。原始恢复表示下传统修复策略恢复0/197；确定性预言机在原始语言L0下恢复48/197，扩展恢复演算使预言机恢复提升至191/197。2x2干预显示：原始语言足够时，精确状态地址接地使成功率从0/48提升至38/48（79.2%）；扩展恢复语言使预言机定义的S1层143个失败中的142个（99.3%）得以恢复。在gpt-oss-120b主骨干上，丰富语言加精确地址诊断使恢复降至133/143（93.0%）；Qwen3.8-27B复现了接地与表达力效应但未复现该负交互，说明该交互依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体自我进化不能仅依赖迭代提示，而需要协同设计验证机制、状态接地、见证语义与恢复语言表达力，四者缺一不可；同时恢复性能可能受具体模型特性的影响，需要跨模型评估稳健性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly modify their own prompts, tools, middleware, resources, and execution harnesses at runtime. Such self-evolution can improve capability, but a successful mutation may leave persistent effects that cannot be safely reversed in states different from the one in which it was created. We introduce EvoUndo, a framework for representing, synthesizing, diagnosing, and independently verifying recoverability of model-generated self-modifications across counterfactual states. Across 600 unseen one-shot self-evolution tasks, we identify 197 capability-improving mutations that fail recoverability verification. Under the original recovery representation, conventional repair strategies recover 0/197 of these natural failures. Deterministic oracle analysis recovers 48/197 under the original recovery language L0, while the extended recovery calculus increases empirical oracle recovery to 191/197. A protocol-locked 2x2 grounding-by-expressivity intervention then separates two bottlenecks: exact state-address grounding increases successful recovery from 0/48 to 38/48 (79.2%) when the original language is sufficient, while extending the recovery language enables recovery on 142/143 (99.3%) failures in the oracle-defined S1 stratum. On the primary gpt-oss-120b backbone, adding exact-address diagnostics to the richer language reduces recovery to 133/143 (93.0%); a Qwen3.8-27B replication preserves the grounding and expressivity effects but not this negative interaction, indicating that the latter is model-dependent. These results indicate that reliable agent self-evolution requires co-designing verification, state grounding, witness semantics, and recovery-language expressivity rather than relying on iterative prompting alone.

</details>


### [293] [MAP: A Benchmark on Multimodal Accessibility Planning for Real World Places](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28384)
*Jason Armitage,Ioannis Tsochantaridis,Linda Mazzone,Chuqiao Yan,Srini Narayanan,Sarah Ebling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAP，这是首个用于评估多模态 AI 系统作为无障碍需求用户规划实地访问时辅助工具的基准。它包含两项新型评估任务：面向无障碍规划的事实声明验证，以及面向无障碍规划的视觉证据检索；通过定期刷新数据支持跨时间的系统比较，并采用自动评分与人工评分相结合的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态 AI 评估基准未针对用户的实际无障碍需求（如行动不便、视障等）进行建模，缺乏在真实世界地点访问场景下系统验证和推荐无障碍场所能力的评测标准，也难以应对场所信息随时间变化带来的评估过时问题。

Method: 构建 MAP 基准，包含两项创新评估：(1) 声明验证——检验系统对地点及其无障碍特性声明的支持情况，并识别满足特定无障碍要求的场所；(2) 视觉证据检索——考察系统能否针对所请求的地点与无障碍特性选出相应的视觉证据。评估在固定时间点执行并刷新真实标注数据，以支持系统间的横向比较；评分采用自动评估为主、对部分响应辅以人工评估的机制。

Result: MAP 为多模态 AI 系统在无障碍规划场景中的性能提供了可量化的评测框架，能够识别符合用户无障碍需求的场所，并检验系统是否可检索到对应的视觉证据；通过定期刷新技术可维持基准在场所与无障碍信息动态变化环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: MAP 填补了无障碍需求场景下多模态 AI 评估的空白，为衡量 AI 助手在实际出行规划中的可用性与可靠性提供了可持续、可比较的评测手段，并兼顾自动与人工评分以保证评估质量。

Abstract: We introduce MAP, the first benchmark to evaluate multimodal AI systems as assistants for users with accessibility requirements when planning visits to places in the real world. In our evaluation, systems are presented with requests to verify or recommend a point of interest meeting an accessibility requirement. MAP contains two novel assessments: Claim verification for accessibility planning assesses if information on places and stated accessibility features is supported and identifies places that satisfy requested accessibility features. Visual evidence retrieval for accessibility planning checks if a multimodal AI system can select visual evidence for the requested place and accessibility feature. Our methodology supports comparison of AI systems in a setting where place information and accessibility information can change over time by evaluating systems and refreshing ground truth data at scheduled times. The benchmark is based on automatic rating and human rating for a proportion of responses.

</details>


### [294] [Timing-Aware Repurchase Prediction for Web-Scale E-Commerce: Survival Models for Multi-Surface Grocery Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28393)
*Akshay Kekuda,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Ishan Bhatt,Yanan Cao,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用生存分析模型替代电商复购场景中按时间窗口分别训练的多个二分类预测器。作者在大型食品杂货电商平台上对数百万客户、30余种消融配置进行评估，发现边际风险率略递减（k≈0.9），Log-Normal拟合与排序最优，单个AFFT模型可替代三个逐视界二分类器并减少约3倍树数量；进一步提出4参数参数化校准，Exponential AFT在ECE上约为Log-Normal的1/10，揭示了校准-排序之间的权衡，最终按使用场景分别选用两种分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商复购推荐被表述为"客户是否会在W天内再次购买该商品"的二分类问题，每个预测视界都需要单独训练一个模型，导致模型栈臃肿且无法直接刻画购买时间间隔。此外，已有直觉认为食品杂货类商品距上次购买越久复发概率越高（递增风险率），但缺乏大规模实证检验。作者希望用统一的生存分析模型直接预测复购时间，替代多视界二分类器堆栈，并厘清危害率形态与校准/排序的权衡。

Method: 作者采用加速失效时间（AFT）生存模型直接预测复购时间，替代每个视界分别训练的二分类模型。研究包括：(1) 基于大规模客户数据的边际危害分析，拟合不同参数分布（Weibull、Log-Normal等）并比较边际拟合（R^2）与条件残差拟合；(2) 以单个AFT模型替代三个逐视界二分类器，对比效果并分析特征重要度的重排；(3) 提出4参数参数化校准，将原始生存CDF映射为逐视界概率，并比较不同AFT分布（Exponential、Log-Normal等）在校准误差（ECE）与排序指标上的差异。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 边际危害率略递减（k≈0.9），与"越久越可能复购"的直觉相反；Log-Normal的边际拟合最优（R^2=0.998）且排序最优，尽管Weibull的条件残差拟合更好，二者存在明显差异并被详细分析。(2) 单个AFT模型在各自视界上持平或超越三个二分类器，且总树数减少约3倍；生存目标下特征重要性发生重排，渠道-节奏与近因信号上升、聚合频次特征下降。(3) 4参数校准实现零跨视界单调性违例；校准质量在AFT族内相差一个数量级，Exponential AFT的ECE约为1e-4，比Log-Normal低约10倍，而排序指标相对差异仅0.3%。最终在概率消费型场景采用Exponential AFT、纯排序场景采用Log-Normal。

Conclusion: 本文证实了AFT生存模型作为电商复购预测统一框架的有效性：单一模型即可覆盖多个视界并减少计算开销，且修正了对危害率形态的常见直觉（实际为略递减）。同时揭示了AFT族内部校准与排序之间的原则性权衡——Exponential AFT更适合概率消费型表面，Log-Normal更适合纯排序任务——为按场景选择分布提供了可操作的指导。大规模实证与30余种消融配置表明该结论具有较强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Repurchase recommenders in e-commerce are commonly framed as a binary question asking "will this customer buy this item within W days", a formulation that requires a separately trained model for every horizon of interest. We replace this stack with survival models that predict time-to-repurchase directly, and evaluate them on millions of customers from a major grocery e-commerce platform across more than thirty ablation configurations. Our study makes three contributions. First, an empirical hazard analysis reveals a slightly decreasing marginal hazard (k ~ 0.9), differing from the common intuition that grocery items become more likely to be repurchased the longer since the last purchase (increasing hazard, k > 1). Log-Normal achieves the best marginal fit (R^2 = 0.998) and the best ranking, despite Weibull providing the best conditional residual fit, revealing an apparent discrepancy we analyze in detail. Second, a single Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model replaces three per-horizon binary classifiers, matching or exceeding each at its own horizon while using roughly 3x fewer total trees. Feature importance reshuffles under the survival objective: channel-cadence and recency signals rise while aggregate frequency counts fall. Third, a 4-parameter parametric calibration maps raw survival CDFs to per-horizon probabilities with zero cross-horizon monotonicity violations. Calibration quality varies by an order of magnitude across the AFT family: Exponential AFT (Weibull k=1) achieves expected calibration error (ECE) ~1e-4, roughly 10x lower than Log-Normal, while ranking metrics agree within 0.3% relative. We adopt Exponential AFT for probability-consuming surfaces and Log-Normal for pure ranking, exposing a principled calibration-ranking trade-off within a single AFT family.

</details>


### [295] [RetailAgent: Structured Adverse Timing in Self-Conditioned Multimodal LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28399)
*Yupeng Zhang,Liuyuan Jiang,Hongyi Huang,Bingheng Li,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过RetailAgent实验框架研究大语言模型（LLM）智能体在金融市场的顺序决策是否具有可预测的方向性结构。LLM观测匿名日内股价历史并反复选择持有（long）或空仓（flat），采用暴露匹配度量（剔除多头决策占比）比较同一股票日内路径上两类区间的回报，发现持续的负向择时效应，并通过洗牌、自写记忆等检验确认该结构与动作-回报对齐相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融市场中，对价格变动做出系统性反应的顺序策略可能被其他市场参与者预测。本文旨在探究LLM智能体的顺序金融决策是否也表现出这种可预测的方向性结构，从而为研究其他参与者如何应对可预测策略提供行为信号。

Method: 构建RetailAgent实验框架：LLM观察匿名的日内股票价格历史及允许的状态，在每个时间区间前选择long（持有）或flat（空仓），随后揭示下一区间的回报。通过暴露匹配度量（剔除long决策的总体占比）比较同一股票日内路径上long与flat区间的回报差异；并采用动作序列洗牌作为对照，检验动作与后续回报对齐的作用；此外引入将自述记忆反馈进决策的实验条件。

Result: 研究发现：暴露匹配的负向择时效应在模态、时间跨度、状态与模型家族等多个维度上持续存在；对保存的动作序列进行洗牌会显著削弱该效应，说明动作与后续回报的对齐驱动了负向评分；将自写记忆纳入决策会进一步增强策略持续性，而在两动作均被使用的股票日中择时负向程度更深。

Conclusion: LLM顺序金融决策中存在稳定且可恢复的方向性结构，即智能体的决策序列可以被识别与预测。该结果为理解可预测策略如何被其他市场参与者响应提供了行为层面的度量信号。

Abstract: In financial markets, a sequential policy that reacts systematically to price movements may become predictable to other market participants. This paper studies whether large language model (LLM) agents exhibit such directional structure through RetailAgent, an experimental framework in which an LLM observes anonymized intraday equity price histories and permitted state, then repeatedly chooses long (hold the stock) or flat (stay out) before the subsequent interval return is revealed. We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

</details>


### [296] [VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28402)
*Menghan Liu,Elynn Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 VERA-8B，一个端到端审计推理系统，通过统一 SFT 与 GRPO、引入弃权与不确定性限定机制以及设计 AuditBridge，将原始申报文件转化为可验证记录和审阅就绪报告，从而在执法行动之前识别审计风险，并以证据支撑的推理超越所有评估基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准金融语言模型以流畅性而非证据为优化目标，虽适用于一般金融推理，却可能生成貌似合理但模棱两可的答案，存在"grounding gap"，无法满足审计工作对合理证据支撑的严格要求。且此前没有机器学习工作针对执法前(pre-enforcement)审计风险预测，构建此类模型缺乏先例。

Method: 作者首次将监督微调(SFT)与群组相对策略优化(GRPO)统一到同一证据标准下，用于证据支撑的审计推理；引入弃权(abstention)与不确定性限定(uncertainty qualification)机制，以推迟处理证据不完整或不确定的案例；并设计 AuditBridge，将原始申报文件转换为已验证记录，再生成审阅就绪报告，实现金融与计算的桥接。

Result: VERA-8B 在执法前审计风险预测任务上的性能超越了所有评估的基线，并产出可审计、可供审阅的审计工作输出。

Conclusion: 上述组件共同使模型在审计场景中获得可靠的证据根基，产出适合实际审计工作的、具备可审计性与审阅就绪性的输出，弥补了通用金融语言模型在审计证据支撑方面的不足。

Abstract: Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

</details>


### [297] [Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28421)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PLVR（可验证奖励的程序学习）方法，主张推理能力不应固化在语言模型权重内部，而应外化为由确定性原语和神经原语组合而成的显式程序。PLVR通过"符号反向传播"机制，利用类型推断在程序各层间向后传播输入所需的类型约束（类比链式法则，但信用分配是可推导的而非估计的），从而在带可验证中间步骤的任务上直接由输入-输出样例学习程序。实验表明，在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基础模型经PLVR训练平均超过RL方法27.8分、超过大一个数量级的前沿模型13.6分；消融实验证明优势来源于反向传播而非类型系统本身。作者发布了符号反向传播库和一致性检查器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练（监督微调和强化学习）将推理能力写入模型权重，导致能力无法被检视、无法逐步验证、也无法迁移到其他模型。作者认为，对于中间步骤可验证的任务，推理应外部化为显式程序（由确定性与神经原语组合而成），而不是内化于权重之中。

Method: PLVR是一种直接从输入-输出样例学习程序的后训练方法，核心机制是"符号反向传播"：每个程序层携带类型化本体（typed ontology），在输出端按ground truth计算损失，并通过原语签名上的类型推断将所需输入本体自后向前传播——这是链式法则的类比，其中信用分配是一次推导而非估计。与RLVR只验证最终结果不同，PLVR的奖励是稠密于程序结构之上的逐步骤契约判定（per-step contract verdict）。具体模块包括：单原语库（可服务多个基准）、100个样例的程序搜索、无新增微调数据，以及配套的符号反向传播库与一致性检查器。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基础模型的PLVR在匹配预算下平均超越RL方法27.8个百分点，超越大一个数量级的前沿模型13.6个百分点。单个原语库即可服务两个基准，新任务的边际成本仅为100个程序搜索样例且无需新微调数据。消融实验显示：在相同类型合法空间内用均匀采样替代损失引导搜索时，中位程序质量从65.6骤降至17.5，证明优势源于反向传播（后向传递）而非类型系统本身。

Conclusion: 作者认为，对于可验证中间步骤的任务，推理应放置在基础模型权重之外、以显式程序形式存在；PLVR通过符号反向传播实现了从样例直接学习此类程序，其逐步骤稠密奖励相比仅在终端验证的RLVR更有效。作者公开了符号反向传播库和一致性检查器，使该方法可应用于其他自建的原语库。

Abstract: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than the type system as the source of the advantage. We release the symbolic backpropagation library and a conformance checker so the method can be applied to primitive libraries other than our own.

</details>


### [298] [Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433)
*Shuze Chen,Kunal Marwaha,Xiaoyang Lu,Henry Yuen,Tianyi Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Prove2Me，一个开放协作平台，让人类通过自然语言向 AI 智能体发起数学形式化"任务"，智能体贡献机器可验证的证明，从而将数学形式化转变为面向任意持有 AI 智能体用户的规模化众包工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lean 4 等证明助手承诺实现形式化验证数学，但大规模形式化项目存在两大门槛：需要同时具备形式化验证与数学领域专长，且撰写形式化证明耗时巨大。AI 编程智能体已显著降低这些门槛，使得人类可用自然语言驱动智能体编写复杂证明，为互联网规模的"人机协同 + 机器校验正确性"数学协作创造了可能。为落实这一可能性，需要专门平台支撑。

Method: 提出并实现开放协作平台 Prove2Me：用户发起形式化"任务"（missions），AI 智能体为完成任务贡献形式化证明；平台设计了专门的协作机制与定制化 harness，使智能体能够基于他人已有工作构建新证明、自由复用既有结果，从而支持大规模并行协作。

Result: Prove2Me（https://prove2.me）成功将数学形式化构建为可扩展的众包式努力，使任何拥有 AI 智能体的用户都能参与，实现人类与 AI 智能体在机器校验正确性保障下的互联网规模数学协作。

Conclusion: Prove2Me 表明，借助 AI 智能体与适当的平台机制，数学形式化可以突破专家门槛与时间成本，发展为开放、可扩展、机器校验正确性的大规模协作事业，任何具备智能体的用户都可加入。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean 4 promise the paradigm of formally verified mathematics, but large-scale formalization projects have faced major barriers to entry, including the need for expertise in formal verification (as well as the underlying mathematics) and the significant time required for writing formal proofs. AI coding agents have dramatically reduced these barriers; human users can now use natural language to prompt agents to write complex proofs in Lean. This opens up the intriguing possibility of internet-scale mathematical collaboration involving both humans and AI agents, where correctness is machine-checked.
  To realize this possibility, we introduce Prove2Me (https://prove2.me), an open collaborative platform for formalizing mathematics. Users launch formalization "missions", to which AI agents contribute formal proofs toward completion. We designed mechanisms and a specialized harness in Prove2Me that enable large-scale collaboration so that agents can build on one another's work and freely reuse existing results. In doing so, Prove2Me aims to turn math formalization into a scalable, crowd-sourced effort open to anyone with an agent.

</details>


### [299] [Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28447)
*Minghui Xu,Zi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在 Countdown 数学推理任务中通过计算器工具调用提升大语言模型的推理能力。作者先分析了推理失败原因，发现计算错误占错误响应的大部分；随后构建监督微调数据集教会模型使用工具，并在此基础上应用 RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO 等在线强化学习方法，以可自动验证的最终答案作为奖励。实验在全新的 1,024 题 held-out 基准上进行，结果显示计算器工具集成可稳定提升 SFT 与 RL 基线约 10 个百分点，其中 Tool-DAPO 表现最佳，将 pass@1 从 Tool-SFT 的 35.8% 提升至 66.0%。分析表明，即使仅提供最终答案奖励，RL 也能促进更有效的工具使用，工具集成减少算术与验证错误，而 RL 提高正确推理轨迹的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正越来越多地借助外部工具完成需要可靠计算与验证的任务。作者以 Countdown 任务为场景，首先分析推理失败的原因，发现计算错误在错误响应中占比很大，这成为引入计算器工具以改进数学推理的动因。

Method: 构建监督微调数据集以教会模型有用的工具调用模式及如何解读工具返回结果；在此基础上，采用可自动验证的最终答案奖励，应用 RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO 等多种在线强化学习方法；同时构建一个与训练数据无重叠的全新 1,024 题 held-out Countdown 基准以保证评估可靠性。

Result: 计算器工具集成一致地提升了 SFT 与 RL 基线，在 pass@k 上带来约 10 个百分点的提升；其中 Tool-DAPO 表现最强，将 pass@1 从 Tool-SFT 的 35.8% 提升到 66.0%；进一步分析显示，即使只有最终答案奖励，RL 也能促使模型更有效地使用工具。

Conclusion: 工具集成能减少算术与验证错误，而强化学习能提高正确推理轨迹出现的概率，二者结合可显著提升数学推理任务的端到端性能。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.

</details>


### [300] [COVER: Identifiable Evaluation of Coalition Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28475)
*Raghul Sugumar,Amrit Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COVER 是一种可审计的多智能体路由评估契约：在生成结果前固定公开信息边界、下游栈 G 与有限合法团队族，通过完整覆盖所有冻结策略的已选团队组合（即各策略“不同已选团队”的并集）来构造无假设的成对策略对比，从而把“团队更换导致的消息/答案变化”与真正的路由效应区分开；在多个基准（MuSiQue-12、HotpotQA-4、Llama 固定栈、ToolSandbox 变体迁移）上的受控实验表明它能暴露选择余量（selection headroom），但并不制造或宣称普遍的路由优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统更换团队时，其生成的消息与最终答案也会随之改变，因此端到端准确率差异本身无法识别“路由效应”；需要一个在结果生成之前就锁定公开信息边界、下游栈 G 与有限合法团队族、并覆盖完整对比空间的评估契约，才能把团队组合的选择效应从答案质量的混杂变化中分离出来。

Method: 提出 COVER 评估契约：1) 事先固定公开信息边界、下游栈 G 与有限合法团队族；2) 完整覆盖（complete coverage）使得在该栈上的精确有限基准 oracle 遗憾可计算；3) 对任意有限冻结策略集合，执行其“不同已选团队”的并集，是在无假设前提下支持所有成对策略对比的最小支撑集（但不支持绝对 oracle 遗憾）；4) 用源 ID 不相交划分的两张受控表格检验该工具，并加入预指定的阳性对照与回顾性对照来区分前瞻性与回顾性证据。

Result: MuSiQue-12 上预指定特权阳性对照将遗憾从 0.532 降至 0.402；后续公开接口对照达 0.424 对 0.554 但属回顾性。HotpotQA-4 上预指定公开直接打分器将遗憾从 0.313 降至 0.110。固定栈 Llama 执行中验证路线遗憾改善 0.190，但原始答案增益仅 0.010 且置信区间跨零。ToolSandbox 五家族变体迁移验证在 14 个未触碰任务变体上穷举评估 16 个声明团队（224/224 行有效）：声明族 oracle 达 0.768 安全证据完成率，前瞻冻结路由器为 0.637（遗憾 0.131），未达到预声明的 0.10 标准；后续回顾性比较器达 0.655，与全员参与（平均 5.00 工人）持平而只用 4.57 人。

Conclusion: COVER 暴露了路由选择余量（selection headroom）但未制造出路由收益，说明它是一种可审计的测量方法论，而非对栈不变性或普适智能体路由优越性的主张；交叉栈诊断显示绝对分数依赖 G，但未发现可检测的路由器×最终器交互。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system changes its team, it also changes the messages and final answer it produces, so an end-to-end accuracy gap does not by itself identify a routing effect. We introduce method, an evaluation contract that fixes a public information boundary, downstream stack G, and finite legal team family before outcomes are generated. Complete coverage identifies exact finite-benchmark oracle regret conditional on that stack. For any finite collection of frozen policies, executing the union of their distinct selected teams is the minimal assumption-free support for every pairwise policy contrast, though not for absolute oracle regret. Two controlled tables with source-ID-disjoint splits test the instrument. On MuSiQue-12, a pre-specified privileged positive control improves regret from 0.532 to 0.402; a later public-interface control reaches 0.424 versus 0.554 but is retrospective. On HotpotQA-4, a pre-specified public direct scorer improves regret from 0.313 to 0.110. In fixed-stack Llama execution, verified route regret improves by 0.190, while the raw-answer gain is 0.010 with an interval crossing zero. A five-family ToolSandbox variant-shift validation exhaustively evaluates 16 declared teams on 14 untouched task variants (224/224 valid rows): the declared-family oracle reaches 0.768 safe-evidence completion, while the prospectively frozen router gets 0.637 (regret 0.131), failing the predeclared 0.10 criterion. A later retrospective comparator reaches 0.655, matching all-workers with 4.57 versus 5.00 workers on average. Thus COVER exposes selection headroom without manufacturing a routing win. A crossed-stack diagnostic shows absolute scores depend on G but finds no detectable router-by-finalizer interaction. COVER is an auditable measurement methodology, not a claim of stack-invariant or universal agent-routing superiority.

</details>


### [301] [AcrossVAM1.0: Particle World Modeling for Text-Assisted Robot Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28491)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 AcrossVAM1.0，一个轻量级、文本辅助的机器人视频预测框架，将未来帧预测分解为基于语义粒子的物体级运动与基于最后一帧的外观编码两条路径，在 VRS 基准上将轨迹误差较持久化基线降低 21.0%，并改善 PSNR/SSIM，但语言引导增益和 LPIPS 仍是短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测机器人视频需要同时具备精确的运动推理和保留高频外观的能力，而传统的单片式像素级模型将这两个目标纠缠在一起，并且其进展很容易被"沿用最后一帧"的强基线所掩盖，难以衡量模型真正的运动建模贡献。

Method: 将未来预测分解为物体中心运动与稠密外观两部分。具体地：(1) 用冻结的 SAM3-DLP 编解码器将四帧上下文分解为机器人、手臂、夹爪对应的语义粒子及背景潜变量；(2) 用仅 0.28M 参数的时空 Transformer 对齐粒子身份并推进其状态，同时通过 FiLM 注入冻结 OpenCLIP 的指令嵌入进行条件调制；(3) 因果双流解码器将粒子渲染的运动与仅从最后观测帧编码的外观相结合，配合残差精化器和学习到的交付掩码生成五帧未来图像，全程不访问未来外观。

Result: 在由真实机器人轨迹构建的 VRS 基准上：粒子动力学较持久化基线将轨迹误差降低 21.0%；在三种交付掩码种子下，未来帧 PSNR/SSIM 由 19.97/0.796 提升至 20.573/0.8004；原始粒子生成将运动区域 PSNR 从 11.89 提升至 13.23。但交付模型在 LPIPS 上尚未超过持久化基线，且打乱语言指令仅使轨迹误差改变 2.8–3.1%，并报告了 oracle、负对照、多种子及逐机器人分析。

Conclusion: 显式粒子动力学是机器人视频预测中一种有前景的低维接口，能够解耦运动推理与外观生成；而稳健的语言接地（grounding）与外观交付仍是最主要的开放挑战。

Abstract: Predicting robot videos requires both precise motion reasoning and preservation of high-frequency appearance, yet monolithic pixel models entangle these objectives and often conceal their progress behind a strong last-frame baseline. We present AcrossVAM1.0, a lightweight, text-assisted video action model that factorizes future prediction into object-centric motion and dense appearance. A frozen SAM3-DLP codec decomposes four context frames into semantic particles for the robot, arm, and gripper, together with a background latent. A 0.28M-parameter spatio-temporal Transformer aligns particle identities, rolls their states forward, and is modulated by a frozen OpenCLIP instruction embedding through FiLM. A causal dual-stream decoder combines particle-rendered motion with appearance encoded exclusively from the last observed frame; a residual refiner and learned delivery mask produce five future frames without access to future appearance. On our VRS benchmark constructed from diverse real-robot trajectories, particle dynamics reduce trajectory error by 21.0\% over persistence. Across three delivery-mask seeds, AcrossVAM1.0 improves future-frame PSNR/SSIM from 19.97/0.796 to 20.573/0.8004, while raw particle generation improves motion-region PSNR from 11.89 to 13.23. The delivered model does not yet beat persistence in LPIPS, and correct-versus- shuffled language changes trajectory error by only 2.8--3.1%. We report these limitations alongside oracle, negative-control, multi-seed, and per-robot analyses. The results show that explicit particle dynamics are a promising low-dimensional interface for robot video prediction, while robust language grounding and appearance delivery remain the principal open challenges.

</details>


### [302] [Training Communication-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Language Models with Layer Re-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28511)
*Simeng Sun,Roger Waleffe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CE-MoE（通信高效MoE），通过将路由MoE层集中在少数层、用token-mixing层和稠密FFN层维持网络深度，减少专家并行下的all-to-all通信开销，在2B至31.5B规模下以更低的训练成本匹配甚至超越full-MoE基线的验证损失与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家并行训练MoE语言模型时，all-to-all的token dispatch与combine集合通信会消耗可观的端到端训练时间，成为训练效率瓶颈。传统的MoE模型在每个token-mixing层（如attention、Mamba-2）之后都交错插入MoE层，导致通信开销频繁出现，因此需要一种能减少通信次数但保持模型质量与深度的结构设计。

Method: 采用异构层模式（heterogeneous layer pattern），将token-mixing深度与channel-mixing深度解耦：不再将MoE层穿插于每个token-mixing层之后，而是把专家容量集中在少数选定的路由MoE层中，并通过额外增加token-mixing层和稠密FFN层来维持模型深度。

Result: 在总参数2B至31.5B的缩放阶梯上，在总参数量与激活参数量匹配的条件下，CE-MoE模型一致地降低了训练成本，同时匹配full-MoE基线的验证损失和下游基准性能。在31.5B规模下，CE-MoE减少33.3%的GPU训练时长，并同时提升了平均下游分数和推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: CE-MoE通过重排MoE层与稠密层的结构布局，有效削减了专家并行的通信开销，在不牺牲模型质量的情况下实现更高的训练与推理效率，为大规模MoE训练提供了一种省通信且可扩展的设计范式。

Abstract: When training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models with expert parallelism, all-to-all token dispatch and combine collectives can consume a substantial fraction of end-to-end training time. In this work, we study communication-efficient MoE models (CE-MoE), in which we adopt a heterogeneous layer pattern that decouples token-mixing and channel-mixing depth. Compared to conventional models which interleave MoE layers after each token-mixing layer (e.g., attention, Mamba-2), CE-MoE models concentrate expert capacity in a select few routed MoE layers, while maintaining depth by adding additional token-mixing and dense-FFN layers. Across a scaling ladder from 2B to 31.5B total parameters, under matched total and activated parameters, CE-MoE models consistently reduce training cost while matching validation loss and downstream benchmarks with full-MoE baselines. At the 31.5B scale, CE-MoE uses 33.3\% fewer GPU-hours while improving average downstream score and inference throughput.

</details>


### [303] [InstructMesh: Selective Refinement of Generative 3D Models for Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28534)
*Faraz Faruqi,Ahmed Katary,Demircan Tas,Theresa Hradilak,Ning Zhang,Jiaji Li,Fabian Manhardt,Martin Nisser,Vrushank Phadnis,Ruofei Du,Federico Tombari,Megan Hofmann,Stefanie Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InstructMesh 是一个面向生成式 3D 模型的后处理修复工具，允许用户通过区域选择与自然语言/滑杆操作，直接在中间潜在表示上对几何缺陷（如开口、孔洞、厚度不均）进行选择性修复，无需专业建模技能；两项用户研究验证了新手能够完成与制造相关的修复，并偏好“滑杆+自然语言”的混合交互界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式 AI 工具能从文本或图像生成 3D 模型，但这些模型优先考虑视觉合理性而非几何精确性，常产生缺陷并在制造（后加工）环节暴露问题。同时，缺少面向非专业用户的交互式修复手段，用户难以对生成结果进行选择性、精确且不依赖专家技能的几何修正。

Method: 作者设计了 InstructMesh：一种交互式后生成精修工具。它通过区域选择和针对性操作（如开孔、密封空隙、调整局部厚度）实现选择性修复；用户可通过自然语言提示或滑杆控件发起编辑。关键在于工具直接操作中间潜在表示（latent representation），从而在无需专家建模技能的前提下完成稳健的几何修正。此外，作者先分析了主流生成工具输出中的常见制造相关失效模式，以指导工具设计；随后开展两项用户研究验证其可用性与交互方式偏好。

Result: 两项用户研究表明：新手用户能够使用 InstructMesh 识别并执行与制造相关的生成输出修复；用户更偏好将滑杆控件与自然语言输入相结合的混合界面。

Conclusion: InstructMesh 展示了通过直接操作潜在表示实现交互式、可选择性的 3D 生成模型几何修复的可行性，使非专业用户无需建模技能即可完成制造前的缺陷修正；混合交互界面（滑杆+自然语言）最受用户欢迎，可作为此类精修工具的设计方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI allow users to create 3D models from text or images. However, these models prioritize visual plausibility over geometric accuracy, often generating results with flaws that compromise their intended use post-fabrication. We present InstructMesh, an interactive post-generation refinement tool that enables selective repair of generative 3D models through region selection and targeted operations, such as opening or sealing voids, or adjusting local thickness. Users can invoke edit operations via natural language prompts or slider controls. By operating directly on the intermediate latent representation, InstructMesh allows users to apply robust geometric corrections without requiring expert modeling skills. To inform our design, we first analyze common fabrication-related failure modes in outputs from state-of-the-art generative tools. We then conduct two user studies, demonstrating that novices can identify and perform fabrication-relevant repairs on generative outputs using InstructMesh, and revealing user preference for hybrid interfaces that combine slider controls with natural language input.

</details>


### [304] [Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Hao Cheng,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了现有智能体工具（单一进程共享上下文的插件形式）并非由建模或演算本身所绑定，并基于无状态语言模型与仅依赖状态空间的可靠性不变量，构造了类ROS的跨进程智能体运行框架Logos：插件即进程，仅共享追加式转录。实验表明在工具调用周期四个边界处注入故障后，80个会话均能无重复效应地恢复；相比单进程配置中一个故障中断所有同驻会话，对等进程构造中一个故障只在一个节点终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体系统在运行时动态组装能力，时空可组合性演算为此提供了完整的形式化处理，其中能力是携带逆向追踪的组件，智能体以插件形式组装。然而该插件形式由单一进程承载、共享一个上下文，导致所有组件处于同一物理故障域：一个故障同时挂起所有组件，进程死亡也会中断其承载的所有会话。本文旨在证明这种单进程绑定并非必然，从而改善故障隔离性。

Method: 论文先指出三点关键观察：建模与演算均不将智能体绑定到单一进程；语言模型的无状态性使所有跨步骤状态都保持在模型之外；可靠性不变量仅定义在状态空间上。这些观察被凝练为四条引理，其前提是演算的假设与语言模型推理的无状态性。在此基础上，论文构造了Logos——一个类ROS的跨进程智能体运行框架，其中每个插件都是一个独立进程，唯一共享状态是追加式转录（append-only transcript）。

Result: 在工具调用周期的四个边界处注入故障（kill）后，80个会话均能无重复效应地恢复；在与单进程参考配置的同故障对比实验中，单进程配置中一个故障会中断所有同驻会话，而对等进程构造中一个故障只在一个节点终止。

Conclusion: 智能体插件可以被构造为独立进程且演算的可靠性不变量依然成立；插件即进程的构建在保持形式化基础的同一前提下，将故障影响隔离在单个节点，支持会话无重复效应地恢复，显著优于单进程共享故障域的参考配置。

Abstract: Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [305] [Dimension Bridging for 3D RANS with Neural Network Accelerated Gaussian Functional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27639)
*Wesley Lao,Thomas Scott,Tan Bui-Thanh,Paul Laiu,Matt Bement*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出使用高斯函数回归（GFR）学习从2D RANS低保真模型到3D RANS高保真模型的气动系数修正，通过设计具有函数结构的核函数，仅需更少的高保真模型评估即可达到与标准平稳核相当的精度，并通过神经网络加速核将推理时间缩短数百万倍，使计算成本进入实时计算范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算科学与工程问题中，反复求解完全分辨的物理模型（高保真模型）来计算关注量（QoI）往往成本过高、难以处理；虽然使用低保真模型可以降低开销，但会因忽略或错误分辨某些特征而引入误差。因此需要一种能学习并修正低保真模型误差的方法，以高效逼近高保真模型的预测结果。

Method: 采用高斯函数回归（GFR）学习2D Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes（RANS）模型预测气动系数的修正项，以逼近3D RANS模型的结果。该模型对在降维导致的物理差异上使用GFR，属于此前未探索的应用场景。此外，文中提出了一种新颖的神经网络加速核函数，用于大幅加速核评估。

Result: 实证结果表明：在恰当选取低维（LD）模型时，所提出的核函数相比标准平稳核能以更少的高维（HD）模型评估回归出同等精度的响应面；该核还提供更具信息量的不确定性估计，有利于驱动自适应采样算法；神经网络加速核核在保持良好预测精度的同时，将评估速度提升数百万倍（墙钟时间），使计算预算进入实时范围。

Conclusion: 所提出的GFR方法能够有效修正低保真模型误差，显著降低高保真模型评估次数，并通过神经网络加速核实现实时级别的计算速度；这为依赖高保真模拟的多查询设计优化问题提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In many computational science and engineering problems, repeatedly solving fully resolved physics-based models to design for a quantity of interest (QoI) can quickly become intractable, requiring the use of low-fidelity models to predict the same QoI but introduce errors where some features are neglected or are otherwise inaccurately resolved. We use Gaussian Functional Regression (GFR) to learn a correction to a 2D Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) model to predict the aerodynamic coefficients from a 3D RANS model. This model pair has a disparity in the governing physics from the reduced dimensionality, a previously unexplored application for GFR. Empirically, our results show that with a proper choice of low-dimensional (LD) model, the proposed kernel allows for the use of fewer high-dimensional (HD) evaluations to regress a response surface to the same level of accuracy as standard stationary kernels. Moreover, the new kernel provides more informative uncertainty quantification, which we show is advantageous when used to drive an adaptive sampling algorithm. Finally, we propose a novel neural network accelerated kernel, which we show offers predictions in good agreement while speeding up evaluations by millions of times in wall clock measurements, bringing the computational budget within the real-time regime.

</details>


### [306] [Investigating Forecast Proficiency of Hurricane-Induced Compound Flooding With a Discontinuous Galerkin Shallow Water Equation Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27778)
*Matthew Scarborough,Chayanon Wichitrnithed,Shintaro Bunya,Ethan Kubatko,Suranjan Nepal,Clint Dawson,Eirik Valseth*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文采用不连续伽辽金浅水方程求解器，将参数化降雨直接耦合到有限元网格中，以模拟复合洪水（降雨径流与风暴潮的相互作用）。以2024年飓风贝丽尔为例，对比了仅风暴潮模型与复合洪水模型，并结合NOAA短期咨询预报数据评估了参数化降雨在预报场景中的有效性。结果表明复合洪水模型大幅提高了淹没模拟精度，捕获了大部分实测高水位标记，且多数相对误差低于10%；含降雨强迫的预报模拟在贝丽尔接近德克萨斯州海岸时（咨询预报第38号，登陆前数小时）最接近最佳路径后报结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国墨西哥湾沿岸近期严重风暴（如飓风贝丽尔）凸显了复合洪水的挑战，即降雨径流与风暴潮之间的非线性相互作用。历史上许多研究忽视了这类非线性交互，导致传统单一驱动模型（仅考虑风暴潮）严重低估淹没范围，无法捕获实测高水位标记，因此需要能够同时考虑降雨与风暴潮耦合效应的数值模拟框架。

Method: 采用不连续伽利金（DG）浅水方程求解器，允许将降雨输入直接施加于有限元网格上，从而实现降雨与风暴潮的耦合模拟。以2024年飓风贝丽尔为案例，使用参数化降雨模型，并利用美国国家海洋和大气管理局（NOAA）的短期咨询预报数据与最佳路径（best track）数据进行比较，评估参数化降雨在预报场景中的有效性，包括咨询预报模拟与最佳路径后报模拟的对比分析。

Result: 参数化降雨输入使淹没模拟显著更准确，影响最大的是降雨量大但风暴潮较低的区域，许多区域峰值水位比基线（仅风暴潮）模型高出50厘米以上。标准模型几乎无法捕获贝丽尔实测高水位标记，而复合洪水模型捕获了其中大部分，且多数相对误差低于10%。在含降雨强迫时，咨询预报模拟结果远优于仅风暴潮情形，更接近最佳路径后报模拟，即使早期预报对风暴轨迹预测偏差较大；随着贝丽尔接近德克萨斯海岸，预报模拟精度进一步提升，在咨询预报第38号（登陆前数小时）采样峰值水位最接近最佳路径。

Conclusion: 复合洪水模拟（将参数化降雨耦合进不连续伽利金浅水方程求解器）在飓风预报场景中显著优于仅风暴潮的传统模型，能够更准确地重现实测高水位并降低预报误差。这表明在墨西哥湾沿岸等复合洪水多发区域，必须考虑降雨-风暴潮的非线性耦合；同时，即使早期轨迹预报不准确，随着风暴临近、咨询预报的降雨强迫也能使水位预报迅速收敛到更接近实际状态，体现了该参数化降雨方法用于业务化洪水预报的潜力。

Abstract: Recent severe storms on the U.S. Gulf coast have demonstrated the challenges presented by compound flooding, such as the interactions between rainfall runoff and storm surge. Historically, many studies have neglected these nonlinear interactions, but we propose to use a discontinuous Galerkin shallow water equation solver, which allows for incorporation of rainfall inputs directly onto the finite element mesh. In this work, we analyze the use of parametric rainfall for forecasting scenarios, using Hurricane Beryl (2024) as a case study. Beryl led to extensive flooding due to rainfall and storm surge along the Gulf. We use a collection of the National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration's short-term advisories along with the best track data to demonstrate the efficacy of the parametric rainfall model for forecasting.
  Results show that the parametric rainfall input allowed for much more accurate inundation. Areas with heavy rainfall and low surge were affected the most, with many areas peaking over 50 cm above the baseline surge model. Almost none of the available high water marks from Beryl were captured by the standard models, but the compound flooding models capture many of them, the majority of which show relative errors under 10 percent. Results from the advisory forecast simulations were shown to be much closer to the best track hindcast simulation when rainfall forcing was used, even while early forecasts predicted the storm's trajectory much less accurately. The advisory simulations improved even further as Beryl neared the Texas coast, with sampled peak elevations most closely approximating the best track at Advisory 38, a few hours before landfall.

</details>


### [307] [A Compact Selective State-Space Model for Cross-Sectional Stock Return Ranking from Raw Intraday Bars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28060)
*Mingju Chen,Enze Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出 STRATA（Staggered-Timescale Residual Architecture），一个仅 244,633 参数的序列模型，直接以五天原始五分钟 K 线与订单簿数据预测次日横截面收益排名，无需任何手工特征，在约 1000 只中盘 A 股的四年训练、一年留出评估中取得风格残差化秩 IC 0.0728，全面优于六个参数匹配的序列基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始输入设定存在结构性障碍：价格序列非平稳且不同股票量级差异巨大，模型容易“偷懒”地依赖价格水平而非价格动态来拟合，导致预测失效。因此需要一个能从原始数据出发、显式消除价格水平偏差并保持同质建模能力的轻量序列架构。

Method: STRATA 包含三部分：(1) 一个五分支 stem——四个可学习的因果深度卷积（有效核被初始化为求和为零）加一个跨字段线性对比，用于消除价格水平/尺度偏差；(2) 四个选择性状态空间块（selective SSM），其衰减偏置（decay biases）在堆叠中被错开（staggered），形成多时间尺度表征；(3) 一个四路径 readout 输出次日横截面收益排序。评估时所有模型的分数先对八个价格-成交量风格因子做残差化再计算指标，以排除“偏向常见风格因子即可获得高分”的干扰。

Result: 在四年数据（约千只中盘中国 A 股）训练并在留出年单次评估，STRATA 达到风格残差化秩信息系数 0.0728（信息比率 1.128，信号多空 Sharpe 12.85），在全部四项指标上领先六个参数匹配的序列基线；日度配对秩 IC 差距对每个基线均 p<0.001 显著，且在预测力相当的模型中 STRATA 的分数被控制因子解释的程度最低。值得注意的是，采用收盘-收盘目标时信号在“分数尚不存在时已开启”（存在前视/可交易性问题），若改从首个可执行价格计量，十分位价差与零无异，但七种架构的排序不变且 STRATA 的优势扩大。

Conclusion: STRATA 表明，在避免依赖价格绝对水平并残差化风格因子之后，轻量级纯原始输入序列模型也能在横截面收益排序预测上稳定超越参数匹配的基线；同时其可交易性证据提示预测能力可能集中在开盘时段或受工具限制，需要谨慎解读（close-to-close 目标的收益在信号生成时点之后才公开，实际可执行价差为零），但架构间的相对优势稳健。

Abstract: We present STRATA (Staggered-Timescale Residual Architecture), a 244,633-parameter sequence model that maps five trading days of raw five-minute bar and order-book data directly to a next-day cross-sectional return ranking, with no hand-crafted features. The raw-input setting has a structural obstacle: price series are non-stationary and differ across stocks by orders of magnitude, so a model easily latches onto price level rather than dynamics. STRATA addresses it with a stem of five branches--four learnable causal depthwise convolutions whose effective kernels are initialised to sum to zero, plus one cross-field linear contrast--followed by four selective state-space blocks whose decay biases are staggered across the stack and a four-path readout. Because a score that merely tilts toward common style factors scores well on raw rank correlations, every model's scores are residualised against eight price-volume style factors before any metric is computed. Trained on four years of data covering roughly one thousand mid-capitalisation Chinese A-shares and evaluated once on a held-out year, STRATA reaches a style-residualised rank information coefficient of 0.0728 (information ratio 1.128, signal long-short Sharpe 12.85), ahead of six parameter-matched sequence baselines on all four reported metrics; on rank IC the day-level paired gap against every baseline is significant at p < 0.001, and among the arms competitive on predictive power STRATA's scores are the least explained by the controls. The close-to-close target opens before the score exists: measured instead from the first executable price, the decile spread is indistinguishable from zero, while the ordering of the seven architectures is unchanged and STRATA's margin widens.

</details>


### [308] [Towards Stellarator Geometry Optimisation for Nuclear Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28224)
*Tobias Weißberg,Moritz Heep,Zorah Lähner,Florian Bernard*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部几何细化方法和一种2D潜在表示，凭借该方案于2026年5月在ConStellaration榜单的几何任务中登顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何任务中，现有方法在全局几何建模上可能存在精度瓶颈，局部细节处理不足，需要更高效的表示与细化策略来提升性能并竞争排行榜领先地位。

Method: 方法包含两项核心贡献：一是局部几何细化（local geometry refinement）方法，用于对几何结构进行精细调整；二是构建一种2D潜在表示（2D latent representation），作为几何信息的紧凑编码，二者协同以提升几何任务的精度。

Result: 在ConStellaration排行榜的几何任务中取得第一名（2026年5月），验证了所提方法在同类方法中的优越性。

Conclusion: 局部几何细化结合2D潜在表示是解决几何任务的有效方案，能够达到当前最优水平，为其在更广泛几何应用中的推广提供了依据。

Abstract: We present a local geometry refinement method and a 2D latent representation that took us to the top of the ConStellaration leaderboard on the geometric task in May 2026.

</details>


### [309] [Machine-learning-assisted multiscale topology optimization of functionally graded superimposed lattice structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28513)
*Prashant Kumar Gupta,Jonathan Stollberg,Dominik Schillinger,Mohammad Ashraf Iqbal*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习辅助的多尺度拓扑优化框架：离线计算均匀化生成刚度数据，训练受Cholesky约束的神经网络代理重构物理可容许的均匀化刚度张量，并用另一个神经网络预测相对密度；随后结合SIMP宏观拓扑与MMA微观晶格参数优化，在三维MBB梁算例中高效生成与柔度最小化一致的空间变晶格参数与密度场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能梯度晶格结构可通过空间可调刚度与密度实现轻量化设计，但其在多尺度拓扑优化中的应用受限于重复计算均匀化的高昂成本。该问题阻碍了多尺度优化框架的高效实现。

Method: 提出机器学习辅助的多尺度优化框架：1) 将体心立方(BCC)、面心立方(FCC)和简单立方(SC)晶格组件叠加构成单胞，各组件由独立几何参数控制；2) 离线计算均匀化生成有效刚度数据，训练Cholesky约束神经网络代理以物理可容许形式重构均匀化刚度张量；3) 用另一神经网络基于蒙特卡洛密度估计预测相对密度；4) 采用两阶段拓扑优化策略：先用SIMP法获得宏观拓扑，再在实体区域进行微观晶格优化，以移动渐近线法(MMA)更新局部晶格参数，由训练好的代理替代在线均匀化计算。

Result: 方法在三维Messerschmitt-Bölkow-Blohm (MBB)梁基准算例上得到验证，生成了空间变化的晶格参数与相对密度场，其结果与材料约束下柔度最小化目标一致，同时避免了重复在线均匀化计算。

Conclusion: 所提出的机器学习辅助框架能够以较低计算成本实现规则叠加晶格结构的多尺度拓扑优化，生成满足柔度最小化与材料约束的物理可容许设计，为功能梯度晶格的工程应用提供了高效途径。

Abstract: Functionally graded lattice structures enable lightweight designs with spatially tunable stiffness and density, but their use in multiscale topology optimization is limited by the cost of repeated computational homogenization. This work presents a machine learning-assisted multiscale optimization framework for regular superimposed lattice structures. The unit cell is formed by combining body-centered cubic, face-centered cubic, and simple cubic lattice components, each controlled by an independent geometric parameter. Offline computational homogenization is used to generate effective stiffness data, which are then used to train a Cholesky-constrained neural network surrogate. This representation reconstructs the homogenized stiffness tensor in a physically admissible form. A separate neural network is trained to predict relative density from Monte Carlo-based density estimates. We incorporate our surrogates into a two-stage topology optimization strategy. First, a macroscale topology is obtained using the solid isotropic material with penalization (SIMP) method. The resulting solid region is then used for microscale lattice optimization, where the local lattice parameters are updated using the method of moving asymptotes (MMA). The trained stiffness and density surrogates replace repeated online homogenization during this stage. The method is demonstrated on a three-dimensional Messerschmitt-Bölkow-Blohm (MBB) beam benchmark, producing spatially varying lattice parameters and relative density fields consistent with compliance minimization under a material constraint.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [310] [Covering 1024 syndromes with 50 columns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27494)
*Stephen Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文构造了一个二进制线性[50,40]_2码，覆盖半径为2，从而证明ℓ_2(10,2) ≤ 50，比自2003年以来保持的Kaikkonen–Rosendahl长度51（目前Davydov–Marcugini–Pambianco在arXiv:2511.02542中R=2族的基础）少一列。新矩阵具有(2,0)-划分为十个块，通过Construction QM_2^2可传播为r=18和r=20时长度分别为815和1631的可穷举验证码，并给出渐近密度2601/2048的族n=51·2^{r/2-5}−1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩短二进制线性覆盖码的长度：此前ℓ_2(10,2)的上界由Kaikkonen–Rosendahl于2003年给出的长度51保持至今，本文旨在找到一个更短的[50,40]_2码（覆盖半径2），以改进该上界并为Davydov–Marcugini–Pambianco的R=2码族提供更优的种子。

Method: 构造一个二进制线性[50,40]_2码的校验矩阵，该矩阵允许一个(2,0)-划分成十个块；利用Construction QM_2^2将这一划分传播，生成更长码，并通过穷举验证器加以确认；代码、验证器和源码全部开源在GitHub仓库（commit 736a38f）。

Result: 得到一个长度为50、覆盖半径2的二进制线性码，即ℓ_2(10,2) ≤ 50，比此前纪录少一列；由它传播出的穷举验证码在r=18和r=20时长度分别为815和1631，并产生asymptotic密度为2601/2048的无限族n=51·2^{r/2−5}−1。

Conclusion: 通过构造[50,40]_2码及其(2,0)-划分，作者改进了二进制覆盖半径2码的最短长度上界（降至50），并利用Construction QM_2^2给出了可验证的更长的码及渐近族，展示了该构造方法的有效性。

Abstract: We exhibit a binary linear $[50,40]_2$ code of covering radius $2$, so $\ell_2(10,2)\le 50$, one column below the Kaikkonen--Rosendahl length $51$ that has stood since 2003 and that still seeds the $R=2$ family of Davydov--Marcugini--Pambianco (arXiv:2511.02542). The new matrix admits a $(2,0)$-partition into ten blocks, so Construction $\mathrm{QM}_2^2$ propagates it to exhaustively verified codes of lengths $815$ and $1631$ at $r=18$ and $r=20$, and to the family $n=51\cdot 2^{r/2-5}-1$ of asymptotic density $2601/2048$. The matrices, verifiers, and source are at https://github.com/wustep/maths, pin problems/covering/share/2026-08-24/ at commit 736a38f.

</details>


### [311] [Selective Interference Suppression of Siamese-Net in Heterogeneous Interference Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27635)
*Arkadeep Sinha,Shubham Paul,R. Manivasakan,Nambi Seshadri,R. David Koilpillai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究N用户实数高斯干扰信道下的端到端学习短码，采用Siamese式耦合训练，重点考察4用户非均等干扰场景（含一对强干扰与弱背景耦合）下，网络能否针对性地仅对主导干扰对抑制干扰（使码本近似正交），同时保留弱耦合对之间的对齐以维持编码增益，从而实现BLER与编码增益间的最优折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质成对干扰强度的多用户高斯干扰信道中，现有端到端学习编码方案可能强制施加全局正交性，而实际场景往往只有少数主导干扰源。需要验证Siamese式耦合训练能否选择性适应编解码，仅在主导干扰对上实现正交化，而非无视现实地引入不必要的全局正交约束，以取得正交性与编码增益之间的最佳权衡。

Method: 采用Siamese风格的耦合训练端到端学习短码；针对4用户非均等干扰配置（主导干扰对(1,2)强度为Λ，弱背景耦合强度为λ）进行实验；利用潜在空间中跨用户相似性统计量（如最坏情况相干性度量、平均相似性度量等）量化学习到的码本几何结构，并将这些几何特征与不等干扰下的BLER鲁棒性建立关联。

Result: 观察到选择性干扰抑制现象：学习得到的码本主要对主导干扰对近似正交，而弱耦合的干扰对仍保持对齐以维持编码增益；该几何特征与实测的BLER鲁棒性一致，表明SiameseNet能够针对不同干扰用户对选择性地抑制干扰。

Conclusion: Siamese式耦合训练能够根据干扰强度的异质性自适应决策，选择性抑制各干扰用户对的干扰，从而在由正交性所决定的编码增益与BLER之间取得最优折衷，无需强制全局正交性。

Abstract: We study an end-to-end learnt short-block codes for a $N$-user real Gaussian interference channel with heterogeneous pairwise interference strengths, while keeping single-user decoding at every receiver. In this paper, we study the case wherein only a few dominant interferers exist and investigate whether Siamese-style coupled training can adapt selectively to encode (\& decode) to ensure optimal performance corresponding to best tradeoff between orthogonality and coding gain or it enforces unnecessary global orthogonality oblivious of the reality. Our work focuses on a 4-user unequal-interference configuration with one dominant pair $(1,2)$ (of strength $Λ$) and a weak pair (with weak background coupling ($λ$)), through which we demonstrate a selective interference suppression phenomenon where the learned codebooks become near-orthogonal primarily for the dominant pair, while weakly coupled pairs retain alignment needed for coding gain. We quantify this behaviour using latent-space cross-user similarity statistics (worst-case coherence measure, average similarity measure, etc) and connect these geometric signatures to the observed BLER robustness under unequal interference. It seems that the SiameseNet selectively suppresses the interferences from various interferring user pairs to yield optimal tradeoff between coding gain and BLER dictated by orthogonality.

</details>


### [312] [Low-Altitude Fluid Antenna Network with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27909)
*Tong Zhang,Yanfei Su,Shuai Wang,Wanli Ni,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对低空无线网络（LAWNs）中动态信道、突发遮挡和异构干扰等挑战，提出基于电磁数字孪生（EM-DT）辅助的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，并结合两阶段迁移学习弥补仿真与现实的差距，实现流体天线（FA）位置与波束成形的快速联合优化，案例表明系统总速率较固定位置基线提升118.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络融合地面与空中平台，为无人机和电动垂直起降飞行器提供通信、感知与定位服务，但动态的空对地/空对空信道、突发遮挡和异构干扰严重阻碍了这一目标的实现。流体天线通过重构天线位置解锁额外空间自由度，有望克服这些挑战，因此需要研究低空FA网络中快速且高性能的FA重配置方案。

Method: 提出一个电磁数字孪生（EM-DT）辅助的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，用于低空FA网络的快速重配置；同时引入两阶段迁移学习框架来填补仿真到现实的（sim-to-real）差距，实现FA阵列在毫秒级时间尺度上的动态重配置与波束自适应指向。

Result: 案例研究表明，相较固定位置基线，联合FA位置与波束成形优化可使系统总速率提升118.5%，该增益来源于FA阵列的毫秒级动态重配置以及对移动空中用户的波束自适应转向。

Conclusion: 联合优化流体天线位置与波束成形、借助EM-DT与MARL及迁移学习，能够实现低空FA网络的高性能快速重配置，显著提升系统总速率，为低空FA网络的落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) integrate terrestrial and aerial platforms to provide ubiquitous communication, sensing, and localization services for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) aircraft. However, dynamic air-ground and air-air channels, abrupt blockages, and heterogeneous interference hinder the realization of this goal. Nevertheless, fluid antenna (FA), a cutting-edge multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technique, overcomes these challenges by reconfiguring antenna positions to unlock additional spatial degrees-of-freedom. In this paper, towards bringing low-altitude FA networks into reality, we study the fast and high-performance FA reconfiguration for low-altitude FA networks with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Specifically, we present an electromagnetic digital twin (EM-DT)-assisted MARL framework. To fill the sim-to-real gap, we introduce a two-stage transfer learning framework. Our case study shows that joint FA positions and beamforming optimization can enhance the system sum-rate by 118.5%, compared to the fixed position baseline. This gain comes from the dynamic millisecond timescale reconfiguration of FA arrays and the adaptive steering of beams toward aerial users with mobility.

</details>


### [313] [Finite Sample Bounds for Composite Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28068)
*El{í}as Vera-Sig{ü}enza,Amedeo Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限样本下、非对称误差约束的复合二元假设检验问题，利用Rényi散度推导了最优第二类错误概率的显式可达界与不可达界，识别出相变阈值（由零假设类与备择假设类的联合KL投影给出），并在相变之上建立强逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合假设检验（零假设与备择假设均为分布类）在有限样本情形下往往缺乏精确的误差指数刻画，尤其是在第一类错误以指数速率衰减的非对称约束下。现有结果多依赖最小有利（least favourable）分布对或渐进分析，如何在有限样本下获得显式且紧的界、并刻画相变行为是核心动机。

Method: 借助Rényi散度构造联合Rényi投影（joint Rényi projection），其对数似然比定义单一检验，可在两类假设上实现一致的误差控制，且不要求投影对为最小有利对；在此框架下同时给出可达性与不可达（converse）界，用于第一类错误指数衰减的情形。

Result: 在有限字母表上、具有完全支撑的紧凸分布类情形下，确定了相变两侧的精确误差指数，并证明可达指数在唯一的Rényi阶处取得；该框架还恢复了固定第一类错误下的复合Chernoff–Stein指数，并给出有限样本可达结果的多项式阶精化；同时刻画了投影对在有限样本下为最小有利对的条件。

Conclusion: 本文给出了复合二元假设检验在非对称有限样本下误差指数的完整刻画，揭示了相变现象与联合KL投影阈值之间的联系，并统一了Chernoff–Stein型结果。这一框架无需最小有利假设即可实现均匀误差控制，为复合检验的理论分析提供了更一般且更精细的工具。

Abstract: We investigate composite binary hypothesis testing in the finite sample regime under asymmetric error constraints. Using Rényi divergences, we derive explicit achievability and converse bounds for the optimal Type II error. When the Type I error is constrained to decay exponentially with sample size, the bounds identify a phase transition and yield a strong converse above it. In the composite problem, the phase transition threshold is given by the joint KL projection over the alternative and null classes. Achievability is obtained through a joint Rényi projection whose log likelihood ratio defines a single test with uniform error control over both hypothesis classes, without requiring the projected pair to be least favourable. For compact convex classes with full support on a finite alphabet, we determine the exact error exponents on both sides of the transition and show that the achievable exponent is attained at a unique Rényi order. The same framework recovers the fixed Type I composite Chernoff--Stein exponent and yields a polynomial refinement of the finite sample achievability result. We further identify conditions under which the projected pair is least favourable at finite sample size.

</details>


### [314] [ODMA-based MIMO Massive Unsourced Random Access with Soft-Output Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28085)
*Tianya Li,Xiaoran Zhang,Nan Hu,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang,Xiang-Gen Xia,Chengshan Xiao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对大规模无源随机接入（URA）系统中的MIMO场景，提出一种基于软输出（SO）极化码的开关分多址（ODMA）传输方案：采用三段式导频解耦编码结构降低数据段码率，并通过层级模式检测框架（粗粒度候选集筛选 + 基于消息传递的模式检测 + MAP估计）与基于SO信息的联合模式检测与数据解码算法，显著提升检测与解码性能，同时降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模无源随机接入系统中，MIMO与ODMA相结合需要高效的传输与检测方案，而现有方法在编码增益、检测精度与计算复杂度之间存在矛盾。作者希望在不增加传输开销的前提下降低数据段编码速率以提升系统性能，并利用极化码的软输出信息增强模式检测与解码性能，同时通过层级化设计控制计算复杂度。

Method: 首先提出ODMA框架下的三段式导频解耦编码方案（将传输分为导频、模式指示、数据等段，数据段码率降低且不增加开销）。随后构建层级模式检测框架：先通过相关运算获得粗粒度的传输模式候选集；再基于消息传递（MP）算法迭代估计传输模式的后验概率；最后通过最大后验（MAP）估计获得精确模式检测结果。进一步提出基于极化译码器逐比特软输出（SO）信息的联合模式检测与数据解码算法，利用后验概率信息精化模式检测；并利用逐比特SO信息的SCL极化译码器开发基于MP的迭代译码算法以显著提升译码性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案同时获得解耦ODMA框架的传输增益、短块长条件下极化码的编码增益以及SO信息带来的迭代译码增益，并通过层级检测框架显著降低计算复杂度，整体展现出很强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方案验证了将解耦ODMA传输框架、短块长极化码编码与软信息迭代机制相结合的可行性，在MIMO大规模无源随机接入系统中实现了检测与译码性能的提升和复杂度的折中，为后续无源随机接入的传输设计提供了有效思路。

Abstract: This paper investigates the design of the on-off division multiple access (ODMA) transmission scheme for multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) massive unsourced random access (URA) systems with soft-output (SO) polar codes. First, a three-segment pilot-uncoupled coding scheme is introduced under the ODMA framework, which reduces the coding rate of the data segment without increasing the transmission overhead, improving the overall system performance. Building upon this architecture, a hierarchical pattern detection framework is developed. Specifically, a coarse-grained candidate set of transmission patterns is first identified through correlation operations. Based on this, a message-passing (MP)-based pattern detection algorithm is developed to iteratively estimate the posterior probabilities of transmission patterns, followed by the \textit{maximum a posteriori} (MAP) estimation to obtain the precise pattern detection result. Furthermore, a joint pattern detection and data decoding algorithm based on the bit-wise SO information of polar decoder is investigated, where the posterior probability information provided by the polar decoder is exploited to refine the pattern detection and contribute to an improved accuracy. In addition, by leveraging bit-wise SO information of the successive cancellation list polar decoder, an MP-based iterative decoding algorithm is developed to significantly enhance the decoding performance. The proposed scheme simultaneously exploits the transmission gain of uncoupled-ODMA framework, the coding gain of polar codes in the short-blocklength regime, and the iterative decoding gain enabled by SO information, while the computational complexity is significantly reduced through the hierarchical detection framework. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves strong robustness ...

</details>


### [315] [Ada-TokenCom: Rate-Adaptive Token Communications via Large-Model-Driven Token Compression and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28086)
*Zijun Zhang,Li Qiao,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Zhen Gao,Mehdi Bennis,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出Ada-TokenCom，一种基于大型自回归模型的速率自适应Token通信（TokenCom）框架，通过将下一词元预测与算术编码相结合实现超低比特率语义通信，并采用Lyapunov优化算法自适应调整压缩率与调制编码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TokenCom作为新兴范式，以token作为通信与计算的统一单元，可实现高效的多模态语义与目标导向传输；但现有方法缺乏在token层面实现超低比特率传输以及适应时变网络条件的速率自适应机制。

Method: 提出混合重构/生成方案：发射端利用预训练自回归大模型编码并仅传输token序列开头的高信息量token，接收端用同一模型预测剩余token；将下一词元预测与算术编码结合实现token级语义编码；并设计基于Lyapunov的算法动态联合优化信源压缩率与调制编码方案（MCS），以适配时变网络条件。

Result: 仿真结果表明，Ada-TokenCom在性能上优于基于数字通信和深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）的语义通信基线。

Conclusion: Ada-TokenCom验证了基于大自回归模型的速率自适应token通信可实现超低比特率语义传输，并在时变网络下优于现有语义通信基线，为高效语义/目标导向通信提供了新思路。

Abstract: Token Communications (TokenCom) has recently emerged as a new paradigm in which tokens serve as unified units for communication and computation, enabling efficient multimodal semantic and goal-oriented transmission. In this paper, we develop Ada-TokenCom, a rate-adaptive TokenCom framework based on large autoregressive models, which integrates next-token prediction with arithmetic coding to achieve ultra-low bitrate semantic communication at the token level. We propose a mixed reconstruction/generation scheme, where the transmitter encodes and transmits the highly informative tokens at the beginning of the token sequence leveraging a pre-trained autoregressive large model, while the receiver uses an identical model to predict the rest. Moreover, we design a Lyapunov-based algorithm to dynamically optimize both the source compression rate and the modulation and coding scheme, adapting to time-varying network conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed Ada-TokenCom framework outperforms both digital and deep joint source-channel coding-based semantic communication baselines.

</details>


### [316] [Fine Difference Structure and Prime-Power Depth of Bent Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28133)
*Zhaorui Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文无条件证明了 p 元 bent 划分的深度 K 必为 p 的幂：对每个非零平移 h，恰有 p^n/K 个点停留在同一细胞中，因此 K | p^n；结合经典胞尺寸定理与已知的三元三纤维参数限制，进一步得到 t ≤ ⌊n/2⌋。证明基于对平衡细化取精确有限平均，并已在 Lean 4 中完成形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前提出了一个问题：p 元 bent 划分的每个可能深度 K 是否都是 p 的幂。对于一般的 p，以往的肯定性结论要么要求正则性（regularity），要么要求胞对称（cell-symmetry）等额外假设。本文旨在去掉这些条件，给出更强的无条件证明。

Method: 证明的核心是对平衡细化（balanced coarsenings）进行精确的有限平均。由此证明：对每个非零 h，恰有 p^n/K 个点在 h 平移后仍处于同一细胞中。这等价于细胞构成一个划分差分族（partitioned difference family），且细标签映射是零差平衡（zero-difference balanced）的。随后依次运用 K | p^n 的整除性结论、偶数维中的经典胞尺寸定理，以及已知的奇数维三元三纤维参数限制，得出全局上界。

Result: 得到无条件结论：K | p^n，故 K = p^t；由非空细胞进一步可得 1 ≤ t < n。在偶数维中，经典胞尺寸定理给出 K | p^{n/2}；结合已知的奇数维三元三纤维参数限制，得到全局界 t ≤ ⌊n/2⌋。文中关键计数恒等式及若干推论已在 Lean 4 中形式化并通过内核检查。

Conclusion: 本文以更强的无条件陈述回答了深度是否为 p 的幂的问题，无需任何正则性或胞对称假设；同时给出了深度指数 t 的全局上界，并通过 Lean 4 形式化验证了主要计数恒等式及其推论，增强了证明的可信度。

Abstract: A $p$-ary bent partition of $\mathbb{F}_p^n$ is a partition into $K$ nonempty cells such that every balanced assignment of its cells to $\mathbb{F}_p$ produces a bent function. It was asked whether every possible depth $K$ is a power of $p$; for general $p$, previous affirmative results required regularity or cell-symmetry hypotheses. We prove the stronger unconditional statement that, for every nonzero $h$, exactly $p^n/K$ points remain in the same fine cell under translation by $h$. Thus the fine cells form a partitioned difference family and the fine label map is zero-difference balanced. Consequently $K\mid p^n$, so $K=p^t$; nonempty cells further give $1\le t<n$. In even dimension, the classical cell-size theorem yields $K\mid p^{n/2}$. Together with the known odd-dimensional ternary three-fibre parameter restriction, this gives the global bound $t\le\lfloor n/2\rfloor$. The proof is an exact finite average over balanced coarsenings. The main counting identity and selected consequences are formalized and kernel-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [317] [Shortest self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings of linear codes over Fq+uFq](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28222)
*Junmin An,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文确定了线性码在环 F_q+uF_q 上最短自正交嵌入与最短 LCD（线性补对偶）嵌入的精确长度，给出完整公式及构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性码在环 F_q+uF_q 上的自正交嵌入与 LCD 嵌入问题，旨在确定可嵌入为自正交码或 LCD 码时的精确最短长度，从而推动编码理论中嵌入式构造与最优码的实现。

Method: 将 F_q+uF_q 上的 Gram 矩阵分解为 F_q 上的一对对称矩阵，从而把嵌入问题归约为有限域上对称矩阵与交错矩阵的合同分类；并利用 Witt 理论构造所有最短自正交嵌入，同时依据在生成矩阵后附加指定大小的可逆矩阵与任意矩阵来刻画最短 LCD 嵌入。

Result: 给出了最短自正交嵌入长度的完整公式，且在奇、偶特征下各出现两种情形；证明了具有非零自由秩的自正交码均可作为另一码的最短自正交嵌入；建立了最短 LCD 嵌入的完整刻画；并给出若干使最小距离最大的自正交与 LCD 嵌入实例，其中部分 Gray 像是 F_q 上的最优码。

Conclusion: 通过合同分类与 Witt 理论，本文完整解决了 F_q+uF_q 上线性码最短自正交嵌入与最短 LCD 嵌入的长度确定与构造问题，并为最优码构造提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper determines the exact lengths of shortest self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings of linear codes over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$. By decomposing Gram matrices over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$ into pairs of symmetric matrices over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$, the embedding problems are reduced to the congruence classification of symmetric and alternate matrices over finite fields. Complete formulas for the shortest self-orthogonal embedding length are obtained, with two distinct cases arising in both even and odd characteristic. We also show that every self-orthogonal code over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$ with nonzero free rank can be viewed as a shortest self-orthogonal embedding of another code. We use Witt theory to construct all shortest self-orthogonal embeddings. A complete characterization of shortest LCD embeddings is also established in terms of invertible and arbitrary matrices of prescribed sizes appended to a generator matrix. Examples of self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings with the largest minimum distance for the code considered are also presented, some of whose Gray images are optimal codes over $\mathbb{F}_q$.

</details>


### [318] [Significance-Driven Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28441)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Sirin Chakraborty,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以显著性驱动的跨层语义通信设计框架：基于统计决策理论定义逐样本数据显著性度量，并在物理层提出元学习变分信息瓶颈（Meta-VIB）语义收发机、在MAC层将信道分配建模为多重动作restless多臂老虎机（MA-RMAB）并采用Q-Maximization算法，共同优化语义谱效。在真实行人安全数据集上，该联合设计相比基线在平均SNR为0 dB和5 dB时分别获得高达1000倍和40倍的语义谱效增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信研究多局限于单层设计，缺乏对数据样本任务价值（显著性）的量化与跨物理层/MAC层的联合优化，难以在有限带宽与信道资源下最大化语义信息传递效率，因此需要一种能同时刻画数据语义价值并协同优化编解码与资源分配的方案。

Method: 首先基于统计决策理论引入逐样本数据显著性度量（信息论意义上的任务价值指标）；据此构建以最大化语义谱效（单位带宽单位时间所传递的语义价值）为目标的跨层优化问题。物理层采用Meta-VIB：利用元学习超网络将高维观测压缩为语义显著潜变量，可在动态信道与可变符号预算下瞬时自适应而无需在线训练；MAC层将信道分配建模为Multi-Action Restless Multi-Armed Bandit（MA-RMAB），并使用Q-Maximization算法根据传感器语义信息价值动态分配信道资源。

Result: 在真实行人安全数据集上的实验表明，该联合跨层设计相对基线显著提升语义谱效：在平均SNR为0 dB时可达约1000倍增益，在平均SNR为5 dB时可达约40倍增益。

Conclusion: 该工作论证了将数据显著性度量与跨层联合优化相结合能够大幅提升语义通信的谱效，验证了Meta-VIB在动态环境下无需重训练的自适应压缩能力以及MA-RMAB动态信道分配的有效性，为语义通信的资源感知设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a significance-driven cross- layer semantic communication design problem. Based on sta- tistical decision theory, we introduce an information-theoretic measure of per-sample data significance that quantifies the task-specific value of each individual observation. Using this metric, we formulate a cross-layer optimization problem that simultaneously optimizes (i) physical-layer semantic encoding and inference and (ii) MAC-layer resource allocation, with the objective of maximizing semantic spectrum efficiency, defined as the semantic value delivered per unit bandwidth per unit time. At the physical layer, we develop Meta-Learning Variational Information Bottleneck (Meta-VIB), a new semantic transceiver that employs a meta-learned hypernetwork to compress high- dimensional observations into semantically significant latents, enabling instantaneous adaptation to dynamic channel conditions and varying symbol budgets without online retraining. At the MAC layer, we model channel allocation as a Multi-Action Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (MA-RMAB) and adopt the Q- Maximization algorithm, which dynamically allocates channel resources to sensors based on their semantic value of information. Experimental results on a real-world pedestrian safety dataset demonstrate that our joint design achieves substantial gains in semantic spectrum efficiency over baselines, reaching up to 1000 times gain at an average SNR of 0 dB and 40 times gain at an average SNR of 5 dB.

</details>


### [319] [Distributed Cross-Layer Optimization for Covert Multi-Hop, Multi-Modal Networks: Exponentially Fast Convergence and Robust Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28469)
*Sirin Chakraborty,Andrea Panebianco,Yuchen Tian,Kevin S Chan,Fikadu Dagefu,Yin Sun,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向隐蔽多跳、多模态无线网络的分布式跨层算法，统一解决拥塞控制、路由、调度与功率控制问题。针对检测错误概率（DEP）非凹导致优化困难的问题，构造log-DEP最紧凹下界以形成保守凸问题，并基于并行近端ADMM（PP-ADMM）求解，同时证明了全局Q线性收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐蔽多跳多模态无线网络中，敌对监测者（Willie）通过能量检测监视无线电模态，导致检测错误概率(DEP)关于发射功率一般非凹，使得基于DEP的隐蔽网络优化极具挑战性；此外缺乏能同时处理拥塞控制、路由、调度和功率控制的分布式跨层算法。

Method: 构造log-DEP的最紧凹下界，将原问题转化为保证满足原始DEP约束的保守凸问题，从而统一了硬隐蔽性约束与隐蔽性效用最大化；随后开发了并行近端交替方向乘子法(PP-ADMM)求解该跨层问题，并在标准正则性条件下证明其全局Q线性收敛（指数级快速收敛）到最优解集合。

Result: 数值结果证实了算法的线性收敛性，并表明在信道衰落和Willie移动场景下具有鲁棒的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为隐蔽网络提供了分布式跨层优化框架，通过保守凸近似保证DEP约束的可靠性，同时以指数级收敛速度获得最优解，为隐蔽通信网络的实用化与动态环境适应提供了理论保证与算法支撑。

Abstract: This paper develops the first distributed cross-layer algorithm for joint congestion control, routing, scheduling, and power control in covert multi-hop, multi-modal wireless networks, where adversarial wardens (Willies) monitor radio modalities via energy detection. The Detection Error Probability (DEP), the probability that a Willie fails to reliably detect ongoing transmissions, is generally non-concave in the transmit powers, making DEP-based covert network optimization challenging. We resolve this by constructing the tightest concave lower bound on the log-DEP, yielding a conservative convex problem that guarantees satisfaction of the original DEP constraints and unifies hard covertness constraints and covertness-utility maximization in a single problem. We develop a Parallel Proximal Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (PP-ADMM) algorithm for the resulting cross-layer problem and prove global Q-linear convergence, i.e., exponentially fast convergence, to the set of optimal solutions under standard regularity conditions. Numerical results confirm linear convergence and demonstrate robust tracking performance under channel fading and Willie mobility.

</details>


### [320] [A Complete Characterization of Tensorizable $f$-divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28556)
*Rodrigo Cruz,Flavio P. Calmon,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文对f-散度的张量化（tensorization）概念进行了形式化精炼，证明任何可能的张量化公式都具有由单一参数刻画的多仿射形式，并据此完整刻画了所有满足该张量化定义的f-散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Csiszár的f-散度定义了一个庞大的散度函数族，但统计与信息论中的大多数应用仅依赖少数几种f-散度（如KL散度、χ²散度和平方Hellinger距离），其原因在于这些散度在乘积测度下具有简洁的组合公式，即张量化性质。现有文献对张量化的形式化尚不完善，本文旨在精炼这一形式化，并系统刻画所有具备张量化性质的f-散度。

Method: 作者首先对文献中已有的张量化形式化框架进行细化与精炼；随后利用代数结构的分析证明：任何满足所采纳张量化定义的公式必然具有多仿射（multi-affine）形式，且该形式由单一参数完全刻画；最后据此识别出全部可张量化的f-散度。

Result: 证明了任意可能的张量化公式均为由单一参数决定的多仿射形式，并完整确定了在所采纳张量化意义下所有可张量化的f-散度。

Conclusion: 通过在精炼后的张量化框架下进行完备刻画，本文回答了"哪些f-散度满足张量化"这一基本问题，为理解KL散度、χ²散度和平方Hellinger距离等少数散度在应用中的特殊地位提供了理论依据，并为进一步构建新的可张量化散度奠定了代数基础。

Abstract: Csiszar's formulation of the $f$-divergence introduced a vast family of functionals for quantifying dissimilarity between probability distributions. However, many applications in statistics and information theory rely only on a few $f$-divergences, such as the Kullback-Leibler divergence, the $χ^2$-divergence, and the squared Hellinger distance. These divergences are especially useful because they admit simple compositional formulas under product measures, a property sometimes referred to as tensorization. In this work, we refine a formalism of tensorization previously introduced in the literature. Then, we show that any possible tensorization formula has a multi-affine form characterized by a single parameter, and identify all tensorizable $f$-divergences under our adopted notion of tensorization.

</details>
